ḍaśavidhahetunirūpaṇa

[maṅgalācaraṇam]

[1.1]     śrīmadvikhanasa mahāgurave namaḥ |
[1.2]     śrī śrīnivāsa parabrahmaṇe namaḥ ||
[1.3-4]     śrī śrīnivāsamakhikṛte śrīvaikhānasagṛhyasūtratātparyacintāmaṇau daśavidhahetunirūpaṇam
[1.5]     vaikhānasakulodbhūtaṃ veṅkaṭeśārcanāratam |
[1.6]     vedāntadeśikaṃ vande śrīnivāsāryadīkṣitam ||
[1.7]     śrīveṅkaṭeśamakhileśamanantamādyaṃ kāruṇyavāridhimaśeṣasuraissuvandyam |
[1.8]     vande samastajagatāṃ sthitisṛṣṭihetuṃ mānyān gurūṃśca śataśo bhṛgukaśyapādīn ||
[1.9]     śrutismṛtinadīpūrṇaṃ śāstrakallolasaṅkulam |
[1.10]     viṣṇubhaktyudakaṃ śuddhaṃ vande vaikhānasārṇavam ||
[1.11]     yenānyakḷptasūtrāṇāṃ netraṃ nistimirīkṛtam |
[1.12]     sūtraṃ vaikhānasaṃ hyetatcaturvargaphalapradam ||
[1.13]     śrutismṛtītihāsādyaiḥ pratipāditavaibhavām |
[1.14]     paramātmaparāṃ vande sūtropaniṣadaṃ parām ||

[vyākhyānakartṛgotranāmādi]

[1.15]     śrīmatkauśikavaṃśyena govindācāryasūnunā |
[1.16]     śrīveṅkaṭeśapādābjasaparyāsu ratātmanā ||
[1.17]     śrutismṛtipurāṇādisiddhamārgānuvartinā |
[1.18]     pūrvācāryaparikṣuṇṇasampradāyānuyāyinā ||
[1.19]     vedāntācāryavaryeṇa śrīnivāsāryayajvanā |
[1.20]     vaikhānasasya sūtrasya vyākhyā samyakprakāśyate ||



[vikhānasasūtrasya tadanuyāyināṃ ca sarvotkṛṣṭatve daśavidhahetūnāṃ nirdeśaḥ]

[2.1-3]     atha satyatvādisamastakalyāṇaguṇaviśiṣṭātparabrahmaṇaḥ śrīmannārāyaṇādutpannena vikhanaśśabdavācyena caturmukhabrahmaṇā praṇītasya vaikhānasasūtrasya sarvasūtrottamatve tatsūtroktadharmānuṣṭhātṝṇāṃ sarvotkṛṣṭatamatve ca daśavidhahetavo nirūpyante.
[2.4]     yathā:
[2.5-9]     akhilajagatkāraṇabhūtena vikhanasā praṇītatvāt. sarvasūtrāṇāmāditvāt. sarvakarmasu śrutimārgānusāritvāt. samantrakasarvakriyāpratipādakatvāt. niṣekasaṃskārādimatvāt. aṣṭādaśaśārīrasaṃskārapratipādakatvāt. sāṅgakriyākalāpavattvāt. manvādyaiḥ svīkṛtatvāt. akhilajagadekakāraṇabhūtaśrīmannārāyaṇaikaparatvāt. etatsūtroktadharmānuṣṭhānavatāmeva bhagavatpriyatamatvopapatteśca. iti.
[2.10-12]     ebhirhetubhirasya sūtrasya sarvasūtrottamatvametatsūtroktadharmānuṣṭhānādeva vaikhānasānāṃ sarvebhyaḥ śreṣṭhatamatvāñca śrutismṛtipurāṇādimukhena pratipādyate. tatrādau akhilajagatkāraṇabhūtena vikhanasā praṇītatvāditi heturnirūpyate.
[2.13]     vikhanaśśabdārthanirṇayaḥ

[nārāyaṇasya vikhano vaikhānasādiśabdavācyatvam]

[2.14]     vikhanā nāma kaścidṛṣiḥ, uta nārāyaṇaḥ? nārāyaṇa eva kevalaṃ.
[2.15-16]     munimātrasya vaikhānasaśabdavācyatvena pratipādanābhāvāt. paramātmano nārāyaṇasya vikhano vaikhānasādiśabdavācyatvaṃ śrutismṛtipurāṇādiṣvavagamyate. yathā:
[2.17]     khilaśrutau: "dhenurvahāṇāmaditissurāṇāṃ brahmā ṛbhūṇāṃ vikhanā munīnām".
[2.18-19]     iti śruyate. ayamarthaḥ. vahānāmataḍudādīnāṃ dhenuḥ utpattikāraṇam. surāṇāmindrādīnāmaditiḥ. ṛbhūṇāṃ:
[2.20]     "sādhyā viśve ca vasavo maruto ṛbhavastathā |
[2.21]     tathaivāṅgiraso rudrā ādityāśca tathaiva ca |
[2.22]     ete devagaṇāścāṣṭau manvantaragaṇeśvarāḥ ||"
[2.23]     iti bhṛgūktānāṃ sādhyādīnāṃ brahmā. munīnāṃ muniśabdavācyānāṃ vikhanāḥ.
[2.24]     brahmaṇo muniśabdavācyatvaṃ bhārate cāvagamyate
[2.25]     "niśāṃ suptvātha bhagavān kṣapānte pratyabuddhyata | [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.265]

[3.1]     paścādbuddhvā sasarjāpaḥ tāsu vīryamavāsṛjat || [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.266]
[3.2]     tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham | [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.267]
[3.3]     ahaṅkṛtyā tatastasmin sasarja prabhurīśvaraḥ | [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.268]
[3.4]     hiraṇyagarbhaṃ viśvātmā brahmāṇaṃ jalajaṃ munim ||" iti [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.269]
[3.5]     aṇḍabahutvābhiprāyeṇa munīnāmiti bahuvacanam. yathā
[3.6]     "aṇḍānān tu sahasrāṇāṃ sahasrāṇyayutāni ca |
[3.7]     īdṛśānāmanantānāṃ koṭikoṭiśatāni c |"ti smaraṇāt.
[3.8]     niśā cātra pralayaḥ, sā etāvatīti kutrāvagamyata iti cet.
[3.9]     mahābhārate: "caturyugasahasrāṇi caturyugaśatāni ca | [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.261]
[3.10]     prāhuḥ kalpasahasran tu brāhmaṇāstattvadarśinaḥ | [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.262]
[3.11]     daśakalpasahasrāṇi apyayassā mahāniśā ||" iti.  [ṃBh13, āpp. 3.263]
[3.12-13]     anayā khilaśrutyā bhagavato vikhanaśśabdavācyatvaṃ kathamavagamyata iti cetucyate.

[itihāsapurāṇābhyāmeva vedārthasya nirṇeyatvam]

[3.14]     "durbodhā vaidikāśśabdā prakīrṇatvācca ye khilāḥ |
[3.15]     tadjñaista eva spaṣṭārthāḥ smṛtitantre pratiṣṭhitāḥ ||" iti
[3.16]     "yadi vidyāccaturvedān sāṅgopaniṣadān dvijaḥ |
[3.17]     na cetpurāṇasadvidyā na vedārthavicakṣaṇaḥ ||" iti
[3.18]     "itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samupabṛhmayet |
[3.19]     bibhetyalpaśruvādvedo māmayaṃ pratariṣyati ||" iti ca  [ṃBh1.1.204]
[3.20-21]     smaraṇātitihāsapurāṇābhyāmevārthanirṇayaḥ kartavyaḥ. bhārate mokṣadharme nāradastutau:
[3.22-23]     "namaste devadeveśa" ityārabhya stotramadhye "phenapācārya vālakhilya vikhano vikhanasa vaikhānasācārya abhagnayoga abhagnaparisaṅkhyāna" iti.
[3.24]     tatraiva: "yattasminnupalabhyaṃ te tattejastattrayīmayam |
[3.25]     tadvaikhānaḥ paraṃ brahma iti vedavido viduḥ ||" iti coktam. [ṃBh]



[vikhanaśśabdanirvacanam]

[4.1-2] tathā nāmasahasrādhyāye "vaikhānassāmagāyanaḥ"" iti smaryate.

[jagatsṛṣṭiprayojanam]

[4.3]     pañcarātre ātmasaṃhitāyām:
[4.4]     "mahābhūtena bhūtena vyāptaṃ vikhanasā jagat |
[4.5]     tameva śaraṇaṃ gaccha jagatkāraṇamacyutam ||" [ātmasaṃhitā]
[4.6]     iti śāṇḍilyena śatānandamabhilakṣyoktam.
[4.7]     vaikhānase ānandasaṃhitāyāṃ:
[4.8]     "vedāntatattvamīmāṃsākhananaṃ kṛtavāthariḥ |
[4.9]     nāmnā vikhanasaṃ prāhuryañca vaikhānasaṃ tathā ||" iti proktam. [āṣ]
[4.10-12]     atra khananaśabdena saṅkalpa ucyate. "so'kāmayata. bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti sa tapo'tayata" ityādi śrutibhyaḥ. avāptasamastakāmasyāpi bhagavataḥ sṛṣṭikārye pravṛttirbhavati.

[samaṣṭisṛṣṭilakṣaṇam]

[4.13]     nanu satyavādisamastakalyāṇaguṇaviśiṣṭasya bhagavataḥ.
[4.14]     "aiśvaryasya samagrasya vīryasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ |
[4.15]     jñānavairāgyayoścaiva ṣaṇṇāṃ bhaga itīraṇā ||"
[4.16]     iti samagraṣāḍguṇyaparipūrṇasya avāptasamastakāmasya kimarthaṃ sṛṣṭyadikamiti cetucyate.
[4.17]     "aprameyo'niyojyaśca yatra kāmagamo vaśī | [ṃBh2, āpp. 21.1605]
[4.18]     modate bhagavān bhūtaiḥ bālaḥ kīḍanakairiva ||"  [ṃBh2, āpp. 21.1606]
[4.19]     ityādi bhāratavacanānusāreṇa līlārthameveti.
[4.20]     jagadsṛṣṭyādilīlānubhavārthaṃ bhagavatā samaṣṭisṛṣṭeranantaraṃ vyaṣṭisṛṣṭau
[4.21-22]     devamanuṣyādivividhaviśeṣasṛṣṭyarthaṃ caturmukhasṛṣṭiṃ prati dhyānaṃ kṛtam. eṣā cidacidātmikā sṛṣṭiḥ bhagavatā kriyate. vyaṣṭisṛṣṭistu brahmaṇā. samaṣṭisṛṣṭirnāma:
[4.23]     "bhūtaistu pañcabhiḥ prāṇaiḥ svāntairdaśabhirindriyaiḥ |
[4.24]     caturviṃśāti tattvāni sāṅkhyaśāstravido viduḥ ||



[5.1]     mahān kālaḥ pradhānañca māyāvidyā ca pūruṣaḥ |
[5.2]     iti paurāṇikāḥ prāhuḥ triṃśattatvāni taissaha ||"

[vyaṣṭisṛṣṭilakṣaṇam]

[5.3-9]     iti vacanāttattvasṛṣṭirityavagamyate. "svāntair" ityanena "mano buddhirahaṅkāraścittañca karaṇānyamī" ityantaḥkaraṇacatuṣṭayamucyate. vyaṣṭisṛṣṭirnāma: aṇḍāntarvartibhūtajātasṛṣṭiḥ. "ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇām" "nityo nityānāṃ cetanaścetanānām". ityādi śrutibhiḥ nityatvena śrutānāṃ prakṛtipuruṣānāṃ sṛṣṭirnāma pralayadaśāyāṃ sūkṣmarūpeṇa avibhaktanāmarūpeṇa ca paramātmanyekībhūtānāṃ sṛṣṭikāle paramātmano'nupraveśānantaraṃ sthūlarūpavatvaṃ vibhaktanāmarūpabhāktvañca. "tatsṛṣṭvā". "tadevānuprāviśat. tadanupraviśya. sacca tyaccābhavat" ityādi śruteḥ.
[5.10]     viṣṇupurāṇe: "tadetadakṣayaṃ nityaṃ jaganmunivarākhilam |
[5.11]     avirbhāvatirobhāvajanmanāśavikalpavat || [Viṣṇuড় ]
[5.12]     prakṛtiṃ puruṣañcaiva praviśyātmecchayā hariḥ |
[5.13]     kṣobhayāmāsa samprāpte sargakāle vyayāpyayau ||" iti  [Viṣṇuড় ]
[5.14]     gītāsu: "prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ caiva viddhyanādī ubhāvapi |" [bhagavadgītāī = ṃBh6.35.19 b]
[5.15]     mama yonirmahadbrahma tasmin garbhaṃ dadhāmyaham |
[5.16]     sambhavassarvabhūtānāṃ tato bhavati bhārata ||" [bhagavadgītāī = ṃBh6.36.3]

[aṇḍotpattyādiprakāraḥ]

[5.17-21]     iti coktarītyā sṛṣṭikāle jagadyonibhūtaṃ "mahabrahma" tadīyaṃ prakṛtyākhyamācidvastu  yat, tasmin cetanākhyaṃ garbhaṃ yojayati. tatkṛtāccidacitsaṃsargātdevādisthāvarāntānāmacinmiśrāṇāṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ sambhavo bhavati. evameva paramātmanā pañcīkṛtānāṃ bhūtānāmanyonyasaṃyogādinā aṇḍoptattyādikaṃ śrīviṣṇupurāṇe pratipādyate. yathā:
[5.22]     "ākāśavāyutejāṃsi salilaṃ pṛthivī tathā |
[5.23]     śabdādibhirguṇairbahmasaṃyuktānyuttarottaraiḥ ||  [Viṣṇuড় 1.2.46]
[5.24]     śāntā ghorāśca mūḍhāśca viśeṣāstena te smṛtāḥ |
[5.25]     nānāvīryāḥ pṛthagbhūtāḥ tataḥ te saṃhatiṃ vinā || [Viṣṇuড় ]




[6.1]     nāśaknuvan prajāḥ sraṣṭumasamāgamya kṛtsnaśaḥ |
[6.2]     sametyānyonyasaṃyogaṃ parasparasamāśrayāḥ   || [Viṣṇu.ড় ]
[6.3]     ekasaṅghātalakṣyāśca saṃprāpyaikyamaśeṣataḥ | [Viṣṇuড় 1.2.49 d]
[6.4]     puruṣādhiṣṭhitatvāccāpyavyaktānugraheṇa ca || [Viṣṇuড় 1.2.50 b]
[6.5]     mahadādyā viśeṣāntā hyaṇḍamutpādayanti te |
[6.6]     tatkrameṇa prabuddhaṃ tajjalabudbudavatsamam |
[6.7]     bhūtebhyo'ṇḍaṃ mahābuddhe pravṛddhamudakeśayam |
[6.8]     prākṛtaṃ brahmarūpasya viṣṇoḥ sthānamanuttamam   || [Viṣṇu.ড় 1.2.51]
[6.9]     tatrāvyaktasvarūpo'sau vyaktarūpī jagatpatiḥ |
[6.10]     viṣṇurbrahmasvarūpeṇa svayameva vyavasthitaḥ ||" [Viṣṇuড় ]
[6.11]     ityārabhya
[6.12]     "tasminnaṇḍe'bhavadbrahmā sadevāsuramānuṣaḥ ||" [Viṣṇuড় 1.2.54]
[6.13-14]     ityādibhiḥ samaṣṭisṛṣṭyādikamuktam. viśeṣeṇa vyaṣṭisṛṣṭāvapi brahmaṇo'ṇḍādiṣūtpattiprakāraḥ śrūyate. yathā:
[6.15-21]     mahopaniṣadi: "athāto mahopaniṣadaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ. eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīd" ityārabhya "tasya dhyānāntassthasya yajñastomamucyate. tasmin caturdaśapuruṣā jāyante. ekā kanyā. daśendriyāṇi mana ekādaśaṃ tejo dvādaśamahaṅkāraḥ trayodaśaḥ praṇāścaturdaśaḥ ātmā pañcadaśaḥ buddhirbhūtāni pañca tanmātrāṇi pañca mahābhūtāni ca sa ekaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ puruṣaḥ." "atha punareva nārāyaṇasso'nyatkāmo manasā dhyāyata tasya dhyānāntassthasya lalāṭātsvedo'patat, ta imāḥ pratatā āpaḥ, tāsu tejaḥ, tattejo hiraṇmayamaṇḍaṃ tatra brahmā caturmukho'jāyata." iti.

[brahmaṇo'ṇḍādiṣūtpattiprakāraḥ]

[6.22]     yajuṣi: "sa prajāpatirekaḥ puṣkaraparṇe samabhavat" iti ca.
[6.23]     manau: evaṃ "apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamavāsṛjat ||" ityārabhya [ṃnuḍhś1.8 d]
[6.24]     "tasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ ||" iti [ṃnuḍhś1.9 d]





[7.1]     bhārate: "svapatastasya devasya padmaṃ sūryasamaprabham |
[7.2]     nābhyāṃ vinissṛtamabhūttatrotpannaḥ prajāpatiḥ ||" iti ca smaryate. [ṃBh3.194.11]
[7.3-4]     nanu nābhyāmaṇḍe cotpattirekasya kathamupapadyata iti cet- tatkalpāntara iti vaktayam. taduktaṃ hayaśira upākhyāne:

[ekasya brahmaṇaḥ nābhyāmaṇḍe ca utpattiḥ]

[7.5]     "tvatto me mānasaṃ janma prathamaṃ dvijapūjitam |
[7.6]     cākṣuṣaṃ vai dvitīyaṃ me āsījjanma purātanam ||
[7.7]     tvatprasādācca me janma tṛtīyaṃ vācikaṃ mahat |
[7.8]     tvatto me śrāvaṇañcāpi caturthaṃ janma vai prabho ||
[7.9]     nāsikyañcaiva me janma tvattaḥ pañcamamucyate |
[7.10]     aṇḍajañcāpi me janma tvattaḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vinirmittam ||
[7.11]     idan tu saptamaṃ janma padmajaṃ me'mitaprabha |" iti.

[caturmukhasya vaikhānasādiśabdavācyatvam]

[7.12-14]     atassamastajagajjanmasthitidhvaṃsamahānandaikahetoḥ bhagavataḥ parabrahmaṇo nārāyaṇasya vaikhānasādiśabdavācyatvasambhavāttannābhinalinasañjātasya brahmaṇo'pi "aṅgādaṅgād" iti mantroktaprakāreṇa bhāratādipratipāditarītyā ca vaikhānasādiśabdavācyatvamupapadyate.
[7.15]     śāntiparvaṇi: "viśeṣeṇākhanatyasmātbhāvanāmunisṛṣṭaye |
[7.16]     tasmādvikhanaso nāma sa āsīdaṇḍajaḥ priyaḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh]
[7.17]     tatraiva: "sraṣṭuṃ sa tu samudyukto brahmayonimayaḥ prabhuḥ |
[7.18]     khanitvā cātmanātmānaṃ dharmādiguṇasaṃyutam |
[7.19]     dhyānamāviśya yogena hyāsīdvikhanaso muniḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh]
[7.20-21]     śrībhāgavate: "na khalu gopikānandano bhavānakhiladehināmantarātmadṛk |
[7.22-23]     vikhanasārthito viśvaguptaye sakha udeyivān sāttvatāṃ kule ||" iti [bhāgavatapurāṇa  10.31.4]

[śrīraṅge vaikhānasārcanam]

[7.24]     gārūḍe - śrīraṅgamāhātmye:
[7.25]     "brahmāpi divyahaṃsena sitameghopamena ca |
[7.26]     pṛṣṭhato'nuyayau viṣṇuṃ vastreṇocchādya cānanam ||




[8.1]     tatrārcako mahābhāgo bhagavān vikhanāstadā |
[8.2]     dadau prasādaṃ devānāṃ raṅganāthābhyanujñayā ||" iti  [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[8.3]     śrīvaikhānase - ānandasaṃhitāyām:
[8.4]     "antarhitānāṃ khananādvedānān tu viśeṣataḥ |
[8.5]     sa vibhuḥ procyate sarvaiḥ vikhanā brahmavādibhiḥ |
[8.6]     vaikhānasaśca bhagavān procyate sa pitāmahaḥ ||" iti  [āṣ]
[8.7]     vyāsanighaṇṭau: "vikhanasañca vidhātāraṃ viriñca taṃ caturmukham |" iti
[8.8]     "nābhijanmāṇḍajaḥ pūrvo vikhanāḥ kamalāsanaḥ ||
[8.9]     śatānandaśśatadhṛtissatyako haṃsavāhanaḥ |
[8.10]     padmāsano vikhanasaścāharmukhaḥ itīritaḥ ||" iti ca pratipāditam.
[8.11-12]     evaṃ kāryakāraṇayorabhedāvagamātpramāṇabāhulyācca bhagavati nārāyaṇe caturmukhe ca vaikhānasaśabdaprayogaprakāraḥ pratipāditaḥ.
[8.13]     vaikhānasānāmutpattiprakāraḥ

[śrutyuktaḥ vaikhānasānāmutpattiprakāraḥ]

[8.14-23]     vaikhānasānāmutpattiḥ viśeṣeṇa śrūyate. yajuṣi āruṇaketuke iṣṭakopadhānakathanānuvāke sṛṣṭiprakaraṇe - "āpo vā idamāsan salilameva. sa prajāpatirekaḥ puṣkaraparṇe samabhavat. tasyāntarmanasi kāmassamavartata. idaṃ sṛjeyamiti" ityārabhya "sa tapastaptvā. śarīramadhūnuta. tasya yanmāṃsamāsīt. tato'ruṇāḥ ketavo vātaraśanā ṛṣaya udatiṣṭhan. ye'nakhāḥ te vaikhānasāḥ. ye vālāḥ te vālakhilyāḥ." iti. asyārthaḥ. samaṣṭisṛṣṭeranantaraṃ vyaṣṭisṛṣṭau caturmukhaḥ devamanuṣyādivividhaviśeṣasṛṣṭyarthaṃ matsyādirūpeṇāvatariṣyamāṇasya  bhagavato nārāyaṇasya paramavaidikatvena sampratipannaśrīvaikhānasabhagavacchāstramārgeṇa samārādhdanarthañca svātmatulyāḥ prajāssambhaveyuriti svāntaryāmiṇaṃ paramātmānaṃ dhyonenānukṛtavānayamevārtho brāhmāṇḍe'pyucyate
[8.24]     "omityekākṣaraṃ brahma vyāharan saṃsmaran harim |
[8.25]     padmāsanastho bhagavān paramaṃ tapa āsthitaḥ ||" [Brahmāṇḍaড় ]




[sanakādisṛṣṭiprakāraḥ]

[9.1-5]     ityārabhya brahmaṇā brahuśaḥ kṛtaṃ tapaścaraṇamuktam. tapaścaraṇakāle sākṣātkṛtabhagavatsvarūpavaibhavānubhavātiśayātbhagavaddattasṛṣṭiśaktilābhāttadupajanitaviśeṣavegācca pulakitasarvāṅgaḥ śarīramadhūnuta - kampamakarot. "tasya yanmāṃsamāsīd" māṃsaṃ mānasamiti yāvat. "arthātprakaraṇālliṅgādaucityādarthanirṇayaḥ" ityarthādibhiḥ samīcīnārthanirṇayavidhānāt"akṣarasāmyānnirbrūyād" iti niruktavidhānāñca.
[9.6-10]     tathā ca tasya manaḥ paramātmani saṃsaktamāsīditi yat, tena hetunā tadanugrahassamajani. tenānugṛhītātbrahmaṇaḥ "ketavo vātaraśanāḥ ṛṣayaḥ udatiṣṭhan". "aruṇāḥ" paramātmani vasantaḥ taditararaviktāḥ "ketavaḥ" ucchritasvabhāvāḥ paramātmapravaṇānāmagresarāḥ "vātaraśanā" digvāsasaḥ "ṛṣayaḥ" sarvārthadarśinaḥ sanakādayaḥ "udatiṣṭhan" utkṛṣya samabhavaniti.
[9.11]     idaṃ viṣṇupurāṇe'pi: "sanandanādayo ye ca pūrvaṃ sṛṣṭāstu vedhasā |
[9.12]     na te lokeṣvasajjanta nirapekṣāḥ prajāvidhau || [Viṣṇuড় 1.7.9]
[9.13]     sarve te yogino jātā vītarāgā vimatsarāḥ |
[9.14]     teṣvevaṃ nirapekṣeṣu lokasṛṣṭau mahātmanaḥ ||" ityādinā pratipāditam. [Viṣṇuড় 1.7.10]
[9.15-19]     sa prajāpatiḥ evaṃvidhān svakāryāsādhakān tān dṛṣṭvā kiñcidviṣādamāpannaḥ punarapi svakāraṇaṃ bhagavantaṃ dhyānaprayāyeṇa khananenātoṣat. "ye nakhā" ityādi. bhagavadanugrahavikasite prajāpatermanasi vaikhānasā nāma ṛṣayassambhūtāḥ atra "ye nakhā" ityatra ādyantavarṇaviparyayo bhavati. "kaśyapaḥ paśyako bhavatī"tivat"khanā ityetatnakhā" iti viparyastamiti yāvat.
[9.20]     "suptiṅupagrahaliṅganarāṇāṃ kālahalacsvarakartṛyaṅañca |
[9.21]     vyatyayamicchiti śāstrakṛdeṣāṃ so'pi ca siddhyati bāhulakena ||"
[9.22-24]     iti, "bahulaṃ chandasī"ti cātra praṇāman. atra "anakhā" iti cchittvā kecidevamūcuḥ. anakhā ityanena saumyendriyavatvamuktamiti.





[vaikhānasānāmācāryapuruṣatvam]

[10.1-3]     evañca sati "saumyaṃ jitendriyaṃ" "suprasannendriyaṃ" ityādinā bhagavacchāstreṣvācāryalakṣaṇasya pratipāditatvāt"vaikhānasa vikhanasācarya" iti. viṣṇorācāryatvena śāntyparvaṇyabhihitatvāt.
[10.4]     "vikhanā iti viṣṇustajjā vaikhānasāḥ smṛtāḥ | [Krā 36.28 d]
[10.5]     viṣṇoreva samutpannā bhṛgvādyā munayastathā ||"

[vaikhānasānāṃ triśuklatvam]

[10.6-7]     iti vacanāt, "gurossānavṛttirvaikhānasa" iti bodhāyanasūtre uktatvācca bhāgavatādiṣu  pratipāditam "ācāryapuṃstvameteṣāṃ vaikhānasānāmeve"tyavagamyate.
[10.8-9]     yadvā "anaghā" iti pāṭhaḥ. anena triśuklatvaṃ sūcyate. janma vidyā karma ceti. advārakatvena bhagavataḥ caturmukhāccoptattiśravaṇāt,
[10.10]     "tatkarma yanna bandhāya sā vidyā yā vimuktaye |
[10.11]     āyāsāyāparaṃ karma vidyānyā śilpanaipuṇam ||"
[10.12-13]     ityuktarītyā advārakabhagavatsvarūpajñānatadārādhanavidhipratipādakaśāstraniṣṭhatvena ca eṣāṃ triśuklatvam. śuklavatāmevādhikyamāraṇyaparvaṇi prathamādhyāye pratipāditam. yathā:
[10.14]     "yeṣāṃ trīṇyavadātāni yonirvidyā ca karma ca |
[10.15]     te sevyāstaissamāsyā hi śāstrebhyo'pi garīyasī ||" ti  [ṃBh3.1.25]

[ānandasaṃhitānusāreṇa vaikhānasotpattyādikam]

[10.16]     śrīvaikhānase ānandasaṃhitāyāṃ vikhanasotpattyādikaṃ pratipāditam:
[10.17]     "atha sasmāra bhagavān viśvasṛṣṭyarthamacyutaḥ |
[10.18]     dhātāraṃ viśvanirmāṇacaturaṃ caturānanam || [āṣ4.6]
[10.19]     smaraṇānantarādeva prādurāsītpuraḥ prabhoḥ |
[10.20]     dhātā samastajagatāmutpattikaraṇakṣamaḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.7]
[10.21]     taṃ prāha bhagavān viṣṇuḥ praṇataṃ purataḥ sthitam |
[10.22] śrībhagavānuvāca: brahman tvayā jagatsarvaṃ kartavyaṃ sacarācaram ||  [āṣ4.8]
[10.23]     loke vihartumicchāmi yadahaṃ padmasambhava |
[10.24]     tatastvatsṛṣṭajagatāmavicchinnapravṛttaye |



[11.1]     dharmasaṃrakṣaṇārthāya vedaśāstrārthasiddhaye || [āṣ4.9]
[11.2]     alasā alpasattvāśca manuṣyāssvalpabuddhayaḥ |
[11.3]     te paravyūhavibhavānātmānāñca na menire || [āṣ4.10]

[ānandasaṃhitānusāreṇa sanakādisṛṣṭiḥ]

[11.4]     saulabhyāya tu bhaktānāṃ sarvalokānukampayā || [āṣ4.11 d]
[11.5]     arcāvatārarūpeṇa loke'smiṃścaturānana |
[11.6]     avatāraṃ kariṣyāmi mahyā lakṣmyā samanvitaḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.12]
[11.7]     madarcāyai sṛja brahman sṛṣṭyādau munisattamam | [āṣ4.13 b]
[11.8]     ityājñaptastadā brahmā muhūrtaṃ tatsisṛkṣayā |
[11.9]     cintayāmāsa tatkarmā kṣaṇamekaṃ mahāmuniḥ || [āṣ4.14]
[11.10]     ūcuḥ na śaknuyāmevaṃ sraṣṭumicchan tathāvidham |
[11.11]     tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā devadevo harissvayam   || [ā.ṣ]
[11.12]     cintayāmāsa deveśaḥ svanirmalatarātmani |
[11.13]     vedāntatattvamīmāṃsā khananaṃ kṛtavān hariḥ || [āṣ4.15]
[11.14]     nāmnā vikhanasaṃ cakre tatpadānvarthayogataḥ |
[11.15]     tathā cintayatastasya manaso dvau babhūvatuḥ || [āṣ4.18]
[11.16]     saṅkalpadharmau sannamya prāñjaliṃ purataḥ sthitam | [āṣ4.19 b?]
[11.17]     brahmāṇamabravīdviṣṇurbhūtasṛṣṭyantahetave (kārakaṃ) |
[11.18]     saṅkalpadharmau bhavatastava sṛṣṭyantahetave ||
[11.19]     śuddhasattvasamutpanno nirmito'yaṃ tavāgrataḥ || [āṣ4.35 d]
[11.20]     muniśreṣṭho mahātejā matkāryakaraṇakṣamaḥ | [āṣ4.36 b?]
[11.21]     matkāryasādhakāneva tvaṃ vai sraṣṭumathārhasi ||
[11.22]     hṛṣīkeśasya tadvākyaṃ bhyādhāya kṛtañjaliḥ |
[11.23]     caturmukhaścāpi tathā sanakādimunīśvarān |
[11.24]     dhyāyanmukhaiścaturbhistu kramādasṛjadabjabhūḥ ||




[ānandasaṃhitānusāreṇa dakṣādimunidaśakasṛṣṭiḥ]

[12.1]     sanatkumārañca tathā sanakañca sanātanam |
[12.2]     sanandanamiti khyātān brahmavidyāviśāradān || [āṣ2.20]
[12.3]     tataste ca muniśreṣṭhā jñānātiśayavaibhavāt |
[12.4]     viraktacittāssañjagmuḥ kaivalyaṃ padamuttamam || [āṣ4.21]
[12.5]     svakāryasādhakān brahmā sṛṣṭavān punareva hi |
[12.6]     prāṇācca cakṣuṣastadvadabhimānācca karmaṇaḥ || [āṣ4.22]
[12.7]     hṛdayācchirasaḥ śrotrādudānādvyānatastataḥ |
[12.8]     samānācca tathāpānādṛṣiśreṣṭhānimān daśa || [āṣ4.23]
[12.9]     dakṣaṃ marīcinaṃ nīlalohitaṃ bhṛgumeva ca |
[12.10]     tathāṅgirasamatriṃ ca pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ tathā || [āṣ4.24]
[12.11]     vasiṣṭhaṃ ca kratuṃ caiva kramādasṛjadabjabhūḥ |
[12.12]     nava brahmāṇa evaite vinā syurnīlalohitam || [āṣ4.25]

[ānandasaṃhitānusāreṇa vaikhānasasya muniśreṣṭhatvam]

[12.13]     dharmasaṅkalpasahitāssanakādyā munīśvarāḥ |
[12.14]     bhṛgvādimunayaścaiva śrutismṛtyādivedanāt || [āṣ4.26]
[12.15]     śiṣyā vikhanasaḥ proktāssarvaśāstrārthapāragāḥ |
[12.16]     vaikhānasānāṃ bhṛgvādyā vaṃśakartāra īritāḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.27]
[12.17]     sarveṣāmagrajaṃ śreṣṭhamātmaputraṃ muniṃ tathā |
[12.18]     taṃ hareḥ purataḥ kṛtvā proktavān kamalāsanaḥ ||  [āṣ4.28]
[12.19]     brahmovāca: devadeva jagannātha sṛṣṭisthityantakāraṇa |
[12.20]     bhagavaṃstvatprabhāvena sṛṣṭo'yaṃ munisattamaḥ || [āṣ4.29]
[12.21]     jātāḥ cintayato mattastvatsṛṣṭau munayastvime |
[12.22]     etairapi kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ jagadetaccarācaram ||  [āṣ4.30]
[12.23]     matputrāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāmagrajaḥ puruṣottamaḥ |
[12.24]     vaiṣṇaveṣvagrajaśśreṣṭho munīnāṃ prathamo muniḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.31]



[ānandasaṃhitānusāreṇa vaikhānasya garbhavaiṣṇavatvam]

[13.1]     viśeṣakhananājjāto viṣṇorvaikhānasastathā |
[13.2]     bhṛgvādīnupanīyātha sāvitrīmupadekṣyati || [āṣ4.32]
[13.3]     parabrahmopadeṣṭāsāvayameva gurussmṛtaḥ |
[13.4]     etaduktaprakāreṇa sarve vai mama sūnavaḥ ||  [āṣ4.33]
[13.5]     cariṣyanti yathānyāyaṃ laukikādhyātmavaidikān |
[13.6]     ebhiḥ pravartitā sṛṣṭiravicchinnā pravarttate || [āṣ4.34]
[13.7]     bhavānavataratvatra mama sṛṣṭau janārdana |
[13.8]     śuddhasattvasamutpanno nirmito'yaṃ tvayā vibho || [āṣ4.35]
[13.9]     ṛṣiśreṣṭho mahātejāḥ tvatkarmakaraṇakṣamaḥ |
[13.10]     tathāsau khananādviṣṇoḥ dhyāyādantaśca garbhataḥ || [āṣ4.36]
[13.11]     yasmāttava samutpanno jñeyo'sau garbhavaiṣṇavaḥ |
[13.12]     ityukto bhagavān prāha muniśreṣṭhaṃ taponidhiṃ   || [ā.ṣ4.37]
[13.13]     śrībhagavānuvāca: devabrahma ṛṣīṇān tu madīyārādhanaṃ prati |
[13.14]     mayā sañcodito yasmāttvameva munisattama || [āṣ4.38]
[13.15]     tapodhano bhavāneva dhanyo'si bhuvanatraye |
[13.16]     ātmārthaṃ vā parārthaṃ vā nityanaimittikādi yat  || [ā.ṣ4.39]
[13.17]     tatsarvaṃ matprasādena madīyārādhanaṃ kuru |
[13.18]     athāha sa ṛṣistasmai praṇamya jagadātmane ||  [āṣ4.40]

[ānandasaṃhitānusāreṇa vaikhānasānāmadvārakabhagavadyajanena nityakarmānuṣṭānapūrṇatā]

[13.19]     śrīvikhānā uvāca: bhavatānugṛhīto'haṃ dhanyo'smi puruṣottama |
[13.20]     nātaḥ parataraṃ kāryaṃ kimapyanyaddivaukasām || [āṣ4.41]
[13.21]     tathāpi devadeveśa svakarma munibhissadā |
[13.22]     udayādyastaparyantaṃ kriyate niyatātmabhiḥ || [āṣ4.42]
[13.23]     sarveṣāñca dvijātīnāṃ śrutismṛtisamīritam |
[13.24]     kartavyaṃ satataṃ deva svasvakarma prayatnataḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.43]





[14.1]     bhavadārādhanaṃ viṣṇo kathaṃ kāraṃ karomyaham |
[14.2]     tvatkarma kriyate cettu karmalopo bhavenmama |
[14.3]     ityukto bhagavānāha muninā tattvavedinā ||  [āṣ4.44]
[14.4]     śrībhagavānuvāca: vikhanastvaṃ muniśreṣṭha madārādhanakarmaṇi || [āṣ4.45]
[14.5]     svakarmalopasaṃtrāsavyākulīkṛtacetasaḥ |
[14.6]     tava karmāṇyanūnāni saṃtu nityaṃ madājñayā ||  [āṣ4.46]
[14.7]     tvadvaṃśajānāṃ sarveṣāṃ kāle vai kṛtakarmaṇām |
[14.8]     niṣekādiśmaśānāntāḥ kāryāḥ mantrasamanvitāḥ || [āṣ4.47]
[14.9]     aṣṭādaśa ca karmāṇi śārīrāṇi pracakṣate |
[14.10]     yajñāśca viṃśatirdvau ca dharmaṃ vaiṣṇavamuttamaṃ   || [ā.ṣ4.48]
[14.11]     vede vaikhānase sūtre yo dharmaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
[14.12]     sarvaissa dharmo'nuṣṭheyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || [āṣ4.49]
[14.13]     tvadājñaiva bhṛgvādyā nārāyaṇaparāyaṇāḥ || [āṣ4.67 d]
[14.14]     vadantī paramaṃ dharmaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ śrutisammatam |
[14.15]     taddharmaniratā ye tu te vai bhāgavatāḥ smṛtāḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.68]
[14.16]     matkarmakāriṇāṃ teṣāṃ karmalopo na vidyate |
[14.17]     tisraḥ koṭyo munīnāṃ tu karma kurvanti me sadā || [āṣ4.69]
[14.18]     jñātvā yajūṃṣi sāṅgāni manmatāssarva eva te |
[14.19]     manmantrakalpajātāni matpurāṇānyanukramāt  || [ā.ṣ4.70]
[14.20]     adhītya sarvakarmāṇi kurvate te madarcanam |
[14.21]     kṛtādhikāriṇaste tu kṛtasambandhino mama || [āṣ4.71]
[14.22]     matprasādabhujassaumyā atipriyatamā mama |
[14.23]     ṣaṭkarmaniratāste vai sāttvikāhāratatparāḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.72]
[14.24]     vaikhānasena sūtreṇa niṣekādikriyānvitāḥ |


[15.1]     bhavanti bhāvitātmāno matkarmakaraṇakṣamāḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.73]
[15.2]     tasmādbhāgavataśreṣṭhā bhavantyaprākṛtā bhuvi |
[15.3]     sarvadhā "ye nakhāste vaikhānasā" iti vedataḥ || [āṣ4.74]
[15.4]     tasmādvaikhānasāśśuddhāssarvakarmasu pūjitāḥ |
[15.5]     tataḥ prāha muniśreṣṭhastamevaṃ vādinaṃ harim || [āṣ4.75]
[15.6]     śrīvikhanāḥ: bhagavaṃstvatprasādena karma sāṅgaṃ yathoditam |
[15.7]     sarveṣāṃ matkulīnānāṃ bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ   || [ā.ṣ4.76]

[mahābhāratokto vaikhānasānāmutpattyādiprakāraḥ]

[15.8-9]     ato'tra śrutyuktamārgeṇa vaikhānasānāṃ sṛṣṭyādikaṃ, advārakabhagavadyajanenaiteṣāṃ nityakarmānuṣṭhānapūrṇatvaṃ ca pratipāditam.
[15.10-11]     atha pañcamavedatvena prasiddhe mahābhārate ānuśāsanikaparvaṇi 85 ame adhyāye bhṛgvādimaharṣīṇāṃ vaikhānasānāñcotpattiḥ nirūpitā. yathā:
[15.12]     vasiṣṭha: "api cedaṃ purā rāma śrutaṃ me brahmadarśanam |
[15.13]     pitāmahasya yadvṛttaṃ brahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ || [ṃBh13.85.1]
[15.14]     devasya mahatastāta vāruṇīṃ bibhratastanum |
[15.15]     aiśvarye vāruṇe rāma rudrasyeśasya vai prabhoḥ || [ṃBh13.85.2]
[15.16]     ājagmurmunayaḥ sarve devāścāgnipurogamāḥ |
[15.17]     yajñāṅgāni ca sarvāṇi vaṣaṭkāraśca mūrtimān ||  [ṃBh13.85.3]
[15.18]     mūrtimanti ca sāmāni yajūṃṣi ca sahasraśaḥ |
[15.19]     ṛgvedaścāgamattatra padakramavibhūṣitaḥ || [ṃBh13.85.4]
[15.20]     lakṣaṇāni surāstomā niruktaṃ surapaṅktayaḥ |
[15.21]     oṅkāraśchandasāṃ netraṃ nigrahapragrahau tathā || [ṃBh13.85.5]
[15.22]     tathā vedāssopaniṣado vidyāssāvitryathāpi ca |
[15.23]     bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyacca dadhāra śirasā śivaḥ |
[15.24]     sañjuhāvātmanātmānaṃ svayameva tathā prabho || [ṃBh13.85.6]
[15.25]     yajñañca śobhayāmāsa bahurūpaṃ pinākadhṛt || [ṃBh13.395.1]



[16.1]     dyairnabhaḥ pṛthivī khañca tathā caivaiṣa bhūpatiḥ |
[16.2]     sarvavighneśvaraśśrīmāneṣa cāpi vibhāvasuḥ ||
[16.3]     eṣa brahmā śivo rudro varuṇo'gniḥ prajāpatiḥ | [ṃBh13.3954]
[16.4]     kīrtyate bhagavān devassarvabhūtapatiśśivaḥ   || [ṃB.h13.3955]
[16.5]     tasya yajñaḥ paśupatestapaḥ kratava eva ca | [ṃBh13.3956]
[16.6]     dīkṣā dīptavratā devī diśaśca sadigīśvarāḥ || [ṃBh13.3957]
[16.7]     devapatnyaśca kanyāśca devānāṃ caiva mātaraḥ |
[16.8]     ājagmuḥ sahitāstatra tadā bhṛgukulodvaha ||  [ṃBh13.85.7]
[16.9]     yajñaṃ paśupateḥ prītā varuṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
[16.10]     svayaṃbhuvastu tā dṛṣṭvā retaḥ samapatadbhuvi | [ṃBh13.85.8]
[16.11]     tasya śukrasya niṣṣyandātpāṃsūn saṃgṛhya bhūmitaḥ |
[16.12]     prāsyatpūṣā karābhyāṃ vai tasminneva hutāśane ||  [ṃBh13.85.9]
[16.13]     tatastasmin saṃpravṛtte satre jvalitapāvake |
[16.14]     brahmaṇo juhvatastatra prādurbhāvo babhūva ha   || [ṃB.h13.85.10]
[16.15]     skannamātraṃ ca tacchukraṃ sruveṇa pratigṛhya saḥ |
[16.16]     ājyavanmantravaccāpi so'juhodbhṛgunandana ||  [ṃBh13.85.11]
[16.17]     tatastu janayāmāsa bhūtagrāmaṃ sa vīryavān |
[16.18]     tatastu tejasastasmājjajñe lokeṣu taijasam   || [ṃB.h13.85.12]
[16.19]     tamasastāmasā bhāvā vyāpi sattvaṃ tathobhayam |
[16.20]     sa guṇastejaso nityastasya cākāśameva ca   || [ṃB.h13.85.13]
[16.21]     sarvabhūteṣu ca tathā sattvaṃ tejastvathottamam |
[16.22]     śukre hute'gnau tasmiṃstu prādurāsaṃstrayaḥ prabho   || [ṃB.h13.85.14]
[16.23]     puruṣā vapuṣā yuktā taistaiḥ prasavajairguṇaiḥ |
[16.24]     bharjanādbhṛgurityevamaṅgārebhyo'ṅgirābhavat || [ṃBh13.85.15]





[17.1]     (bhṛgityeva bhṛguḥ pūrvamaṅgārebhyo'ṅgirābhavat  || [ṃB.h13.85.15 d]
[17.2]     aṅgārasaṃśrayāccaiva kavirityaparo'bhavat |
[17.3]     saha jvālābhirutpanno bhṛgustasmādbhṛguḥ smṛtaḥ   || [ṃB.h13.85.16]
[17.4]     marīcibhyo marīcistu mārīcaḥ kaśyapo hyabhūt |
[17.5]     aṅgārebhyo'ṅgirāstāta vālakhilyāḥ śiloccayāt |
[17.6]     atraivātreti ca vibho jātamatriṃ vadantyapi ||  [ṃBh13.85.17]
[17.7]     tathāgnestasya bhasmabhyo brahmarṣigaṇasaṃmatāḥ |
[17.8]     vaikhānasāḥ samutpannāstapaḥśrutaguṇepsavaḥ |
[17.9]     aśruto'sya samutpannāvaśvinau lokasaṃmatau || [ṃBh13.85.18]
[17.10]     śeṣāḥ prajānāṃ patayaḥ srotobhyastasya jajñire |
[17.11]     ṛṣayo romakūpebhyassvedācchando balānmanaḥ || [ṃBh13.85.19]
[17.12]     etasmātkāraṇādāhuragniḥ sarvāścaabhiertelugu devatāḥ |
[17.13]     ṛṣayaḥ śrutasaṃpannā vedaprāmāṇyadarśanāt  || [ṃB.h13.85.20]
[17.14]     yāni dārūṇi te māsā niryāsāḥ pakṣasaṃjñitāḥ |
[17.15]     ahorātrā muhūrtāstu pittaṃ jyotiśca vāruṇam ||  [ṃBh13.85.21]
[17.16]     raudraṃ lohitamityāhurlohitātkanakaṃ smṛtam |
[17.17]     tanmaitramiti vijñeyaṃ dhūmācca vasavaḥ smṛtāḥ   || [ṃB.h13.85.22]
[17.18]     arciṣo yāśca te rudrāstathādityā mahāprabhāḥ |
[17.19]     uddiṣṭāste tathāṅgārā ye dhiṣṇyeṣu divi sthitāḥ   || [ṃB.h13.85.23]
[17.20]     ādikartā ca lokasya tatparaṃ brahma taddhruvam |
[17.21]     sarvakāmadamityāhustadrahasyamuvāca ca || [ṃBh13.85.24]
[17.22]     tato'bravīnmahādevo varuṇaḥ pavanātmakaḥ |
[17.23]     [varuṇa] mama satramidaṃ divyamahaṃ gṛhapatistviha ||  [ṃBh13.85.25]
[17.24]     trīṇi pūrvāṇyapatyāni mama tāni na saṃśayaḥ |






[18.1]     iti jānīta khagamā mama yajñaphalaṃ hi tat  || [ṃB.h13.85.26]
[18.2]     agni: madaṅgebhyaḥ prasūtāni madāśrayakṛtāni ca |
[18.3]     mamaiva tānyapatyāni varuṇo hyavaśātmakaḥ   || [ṃB.h13.85.27]
[18.4]     brahmā:athābravīllokagururbrahmā lokapitāmahaḥ |
[18.5]     mamaiva tānyapatyāni mama śukraṃ hutaṃ hi tat  || [ṃB.h13.85.28]
[18.6]     īśvara: ahaṃ kartā hi satrasya hotā śukrasya caiva hi |
[18.7]     yasya bījaṃ phalaṃ tasya śukraṃ cetkāraṇaṃ matam   || [ṃB.h13.85.29]
[18.8]     tato'bruvan devagaṇāḥ pitāmahamupetya vai |
[18.9]     kṛtāñjalipuṭāḥ sarve śirobhirpraṇipatya vai   || [ṃB.h13.85.30]
[18.10]     vayaṃ ca bhagavan sarve jagatetaccarācaram |
[18.11]     tavaiva prasavaṃ sarvaṃ tasmādagnirvibhāvasuḥ |
[18.12]     varuṇaśceśvaro devo labhatāṃ kāmamīpsitam   || [ṃB.h13.85.31]
[18.13]     nisargādvaruṇaścāpi brahmaṇo yādasāṃ patiḥ |
[18.14]     jagrāha vai bhṛguṃ pūrvamapatyaṃ sūryavarcasam ||  [ṃBh13.85.32]
[18.15]     īśvaro'ṅgirasaṃ cāgnerapatyārthe hyakalpayat |
[18.16]     pitāmahastvapatyaṃ vai kaviṃ jagrāha dharmataḥ  || [ṃB.h13.85.33]
[18.17]     tadā sa vāruṇiḥ khyāto bhṛguḥ prasavakarmakṛt |
[18.18]     āgneyastvaṅgirāḥ śrīmān kavirbrāhyo mahāyāśāḥ |
[18.19]     bhārgavāṅgirasau loke lokasaṃtānalakṣaṇau ||  [ṃBh13.85.34]
[18.20]     ete hi prasavāssarve prajānāṃ patayastrayaḥ |
[18.21]     sarvaṃ saṃtānameteṣāmidamityavadhāraya || [ṃBh13.85.35]
[18.22] ityādinā vaikhānasānāmutpattyādikaṃ viśeṣeṇa pratipāditam.
[18.23] purāṇāntare:
[18.24]     yajñe devasya vitate mahato varuṇasya ha |
[18.25]     brahmaṇo'psarasaṃ dṛṣṭvā retaścaskanda karhicit ||




[19.1]     tatpratīkṣya sa parṇena saṃjuhāva vibhāvasau |
[19.2]     tato'rciṣo'bhūdbhagavān bhṛguraṅgārato'ṅgirāḥ ||
[19.3]     atraivānveṣaṇādatriḥ khananādvikhanā muniḥ |
[19.4]     khananaṃ tattvamīmāṃsetybishierteluguāhuḥ kecana sūrayaḥ ||
[19.5]     apare nigamārthānāṃ khananāditi naśśrutam |
[19.6]     tatastadvaṃśajā viprā vedavedāṅgavedinaḥ ||" iti.
[19.7-10]     "ye vālāḥ te vālakhilyāḥ" ityatra bakārasthāne vakāra. jñānātiśayavaibhavātkāmādidoṣarāhityena ca bālā ucyante. śrūyate hi: "tasmādbrāhmaṇaḥ pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidya bālyena tiṣṭhāsetbālyañca pāṇdityañca nirvidyātha muniḥ" iti.
[19.11-13]     atra kecidevamūcuḥ: brahmaṇaḥ vidhūtāccharīrātyanmāṃsaṃ vikīrṇamabhavattato ketavo vātaraśanā ṛṣaya udatiṣṭhanniti. tadasat. evamarthasvīkāre sṛṣṭikāraṇabhūtasya brahmaśarīrasya śaithilyamuktaṃ syāditi.
[19.14-20]     kiñca "tasyāntarmanasi kāmassamavartata" ityādinā "kāmastadagre samavartatādhi" ityantena sopapattikaṃ vartamānaṃ saṅkalparūpaṃ mananavyāpāramuktvā "manaso retaḥ prathamaṃ yadāsīd" ityatra tatra vidyamānasya vyāpārasya prajāpateḥ sṛṣṭikāraṇatvaṃ retaśśabdebishiertelugunoktvā "sato bandhumasati niravindann" ityārabhya "ya evaṃ veda" ityantena brahmaṇastatkālavidyamānamānasaśaktimāabhiertelugutreṇa vyaṣṭisṛṣṭeraniṣpannatvamuktvā manaso vyaṣṭikāraṇalābhāya "sa tapo'tapyata" iti bhagavadupāsanamakarod" ityuktam. ayamevārthaḥ "omityekākṣaraṃ brahme"tyādinā brahmāṇḍe'pyuktam.

[bhrgvādīnāṃ bhagavanmānasaputratvam]

[19.21]     bhagavanmānasaputrā bhṛgvādaya iti bhagavataivoktam:
[19.22]     "maharṣayassapta pūrve catvāro manavastathā |
[19.23]     madbhāvā mānasā jātā yeṣāṃ loka imāḥ prajāḥ ||" [ṃBh6.32.6]

[bhṛgvādīnāṃ vaikhānasavaṃśakartṛtvam]

[19.24]     bhṛgvādaya eva vaikhānasānāṃ vaṃśakartāraḥ iti bhārate'pyuktam.




[20.1]     yathā śāntiparvaṇi:
[20.2]     "bhṛgvaṅgiromarīcyatri pulastyapulahāḥ kratuḥ | [ṃBh13, āpp. 7.49]
[20.3]     tathā vasiṣṭho dakṣaśca nava svāyambhuvā dvijāḥ ||
[20.4]     ete vaikhānasānān tu ṛṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām |
[20.5]     vaṃśakartāra ucyante sāttvikāhārabhojinām ||" iti [ṃBh]
[20.6-8]     tasmādbhagavatā nārāyaṇena brahmaṇā ca sṛṣṭānāṃ bhṛgvādīnāmṛṣīṇāṃ tadvaṃśajānāṃ ca niṣekādikriyāvatāmadvārakabhagavadyajanādhikāravatāmeva loke vaikhānasā iti prasiddhiḥ.

[caturmukhasṛṣṭānāṃ sarveṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ vaikhānasatvaṃ samānamityākṣepasya parihāraḥ]

[20.9]     nanu brahmaṇā sṛṣṭānāṃ sarveṣāmapi manuṣyānāṃ vaikhānasatvaṃ samānam.
[20.10-11]     kathameteṣveva vyavahriyate iti cetsatyam. nāmarūbishiertelugupakṛtyavibhāgādikaṃ vedaśabdebhya eva brahmaṇā kṛtamiti śrūyate. "vedena nāmarūpe vyākarotsatāsatī prajāpatir" iti.
[20.12]     tathā bhārate: "anādinidhanā hyeṣā vāgudsṛṣṭā svayambhuvā || [ṃBh12.224.55 d]
[20.13]     ādau vedamayī divyā yatassarvāḥ prasūtayaḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh12.671.1]
[20.14]     śrīviṣṇupurāṇe: "nāma rupañca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyānāñca praabhiertelugupañcanam |
[20.15]     vedaśabdebhya evādau devādīnāṃ cakāra saḥ ||" iti. [Viṣṇuড় 1.5.63]
[20.16]     manu: "sarveṣāñca sa nāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthakpṛthak |
[20.17]     vedaśabdebhya evādau pṛthaksaṃsthāśca nirmame ||" [ṃnuḍhś1.21]
[20.18-19]     iti ca nāmarūpavibhāgasya śrutismṛtiṣu pratipāditatvātbrahmaṇā tādṛśaśrutismṛtyanusāreṇa sṛṣṭānāmeva vaikhānasatvavyavahāraḥ. ṇānyeṣām.
[20.20-23]     kiñca ekasmin paramapuruṣe mukhādiṣu jātānāṃ yathā brāhmaṇādivyavahāraḥ yathā viśvāmitra bhāradvājādi gotreṣūtpannānāmekasūtraniṣṭhatvaṃ vinā anekasūtrānusāritvaṃ, yathā ca kumudakahlārādiṣu "paṅke jāyata" iti vyutpattyā paṅkajatve samāno'pi yogarūḍhibhyāṃ paṅkajatvaṃ tāmarasa eva vartate tathaiva vaikhānasatvamapi. tathoktam:
[20.24]     "kalpe kalpe kṣayotpattyā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ |
[20.25]     śrutismṛtisadācāra- nirṇetārastu sarvadā ||




[21.1]     na kāścidvedakartā'sti vedavaktā caturmukhaḥ |
[21.2]     tathaiva dharmaṃ smarati manuḥ kalpāntarāntare ||"

[tṛtīyāśramaniṣṭhānāṃ na naisargikaṃ vaikhānasatvam]

[21.2-6] nanu: "brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca bhikṣurvaikhānasastathā" iti tatra tatra tṛtīyāśramaniṣṭhānāmeva vaikhānasatvāvagamātta eva vaikhānasaśabdavācyāḥ kiṃ na syuriti cetna. śrutismṛtipurāṇādiṣu uktaprakāreṇa brahmaṇā sṛṣṭānāmeva vaikhānasatvāvagamātteṣāmeva tatra tatrotkṛṣṭatvapratipādanācca.

[vaikhānasānāṃ brahmasabhāpraveśaḥ]

[21.7-8]     tathā hi: śrutāveva vaikhānasānāmutpattiḥ śrūyate. "ye nakhāḥ te vaikhānasāḥ" iti. chandogyabrāhmaṇe: "vaikhānasā vā ṛṣaya indrasya priyā āsan"
[21.9-11]     "pauruhanmahaṃ bhavati. puruhānnādo vā etena vaikhānaso'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat" iti. atra indraśabdena paramātmā nārāyaṇa ucyate. "sa brahmā sa śivassendrasso'kṣaraḥ paramassvarāṭ" ityādi śrutibhyaḥ.
[21.12]     "āśvatthaḥ kapilā gāvastulasī vikhanāstathā |
[21.13]     catvāro matpriyā rājaṃsteṣāṃ vaikhānaso varaḥ ||" [āṣ19.10]
[21.14]     ityupabrahmaṇāt. brahmasūtre indraprāṇādhikaraṇe tathā nirṇayācca.
[21.15]     ānuśāsanike: "tathāgnestasya bhasmabhyo brahmarṣigaṇasamimtāḥ |
[21.16]     vaikhānasāssamutpannāstapaśśrutaguṇepsavaḥ||" [ṃBh13.85.18]
[21.17]     iti vaikhānasanāmnaivotpattipratipādanāt.
[21.18]     tathā kiṣkindhākāṇḍe:
[21.19]     "śṛṅgaṃ saumanasaṃ nāma jātarūpamayaṃ dhruvam   || [ṛā.m 4.39.51 d]
[21.20]     tatra pūrvaṃ padaṃ kṛtvā purā viṣṇustrivikrame |
[21.21]     dvitīyaṃ śikhare raudraṃ cakāra puruṣottamaḥ   || [ṛā.m 4.39.52]
[21.22]     taduttare pratikramya jambūdvīpaṃ divākaraḥ |
[21.23]     dṛśyo bhavati bhūyiṣṭhaṃ śikharaṃ tanmahocchrayam   || [ṛā.m 4.39.53]
[21.24]     tatra vaikhānasā nāma vālakhilyā maharṣayaḥ |
[21.25]     prakāśamānā dṛśyante sūryavarṇāstapasvinaḥ ||" iti  [ṛām 4.39.54]





[22.1]     tatraiva catvāriṃśe'dhyāye:
[22.2]     mainākaśca vicetavyassasānuprasthakandaraḥ |
[22.3]     strīṇāmaśvamukhīnāñca niketāstatra tatra tu   || [ṛā.m 4.42.30]
[22.4]     taṃ deśaṃ samatikramya āśramaṃ siddhasevitam |
[22.5]     siddhā vaikhānasāstatra vālakhilyāśca tāpasāḥ   || [ṛā.m 4.42.31]
[22.6]     vandyāste tu tapassiddhāstapasā vītakalmaṣāḥ |
[22.7]     praṣṭavyā cāpi sītāyāḥ pravṛttirvināyānvitaḥ  || [ṛā.m 4.42.32]
[22.8]     hemapuṣkarasañchannaṃ tatra vaikhānasaṃ saraḥ |" [ṛām 4.42.33 b]
[22.9]     iti ca vānaprasthaparatvaṃ vinā "vaikhānasā vālakhilyā" iti vālakhilyasamānatayā pṛthagvaṃśatvena kīrtanāt.
[22.11]     tathā śrīviṣṇupurāṇe - dvitīṃyāṃśe navame'dhyāye:
[22.12]     "tataḥ prayāti bhagavān brāhmaṇairabhirakṣitaḥ |
[22.13]     vālakhilyādibhiścaiva prabhurvaikhānasairapi || [Viṣṇuড় 2.8.58]
[22.14]     mahātmābhirmahātmā vai jagatāṃ pālanodyataḥ ||" iti [Viṣṇuড় ]
[22.15]     tathā sabhāparvaṇi:
[22.16]     pitāmahasya bhavanaṃ jagmuśca kṛpayānvitāḥ || [ṃBh1.203.2 d]
[22.17]     tato dadṛśurātmānaṃ saha devaiḥ pitāmaham |
[22.18]     siddhairmaharṣibhiścaiva samantātparivāritam || [ṃBh1.203.3]
[22.19]     tatra viṣṇurmahādevastatrāgnirvāyunā saha |
[22.20]     candrādityau ca dharmaśca parameṣṭhī tathā budhāḥ || [ṃBh1.203.4]
[22.21]     vaikhānasā vālakhilyāssomapāśca marīcipāḥ |
[22.22]     ajāścaivājamīḍhāśca tejogarbhāstapasvinaḥ |
[22.23]     ṛṣayassarva eveti pitāmahamupāsate ||" [ṃBh1.203.5]
[22.24-25]     iti ca vaikhānasānāṃ brahmasabhāpraveśavarṇanāt. tasya ca tṛtīyāśramaniṣṭheṣvasambhavāt.





[vānaprasthānāṃ śrautakarmaniṣedhaḥ]

[23.1]     ānuśāsanike
[23.2]     "śṛṇu rājan vasiṣṭhasya mukhyaṃ karma yaśasvinaḥ |  [ṃBh13.140.15 d]
[23.3]     vaikhānasavidhānena gaṅgātīraṃ samāśritāḥ ||  [ṃBh13.140.647*.1]
[23.4]     ādityāssatramīhanti saro vaikhānasaṃ prati ||" iti [ṃBh13.140.16]
[23.5-7]     atra vānaprasthavidhau satrayāgakramavidhānābhāvātvānaprasthāśramapraviṣṭhānāṃ tena śārīreṇa punarapi devatvayogyatā'sambhavācca vaikhānasavidhāneneti vaikhānasapadena vānaprasthānāṃ gṛhītumaśakyatvāt.
[23.8]     kiñca śrībhāgavate ekādaśaskandhe:
[23.9]     "vanyaiścarupuroḍāśairnirvapetkālacoditām |
[23.10]     na tu śrautena vidhinā māṃ yajeta vanāśramī ||" [bhāgavatapurāṇa  11.18.7]
[23.11]     iti vānaprasthānāṃ śarutavidhiniṣedhapratipādanāt.

[bhagavadarcanakramaḥ]

[23.12]     tathā vṛddhamanau: "vedāntavedibhirvipraistadvaikhānasasūtribhiḥ |
[23.13]     yājayedyajñapuruṣaṃ viṣṇuṃ rājā jagaddhitaḥ ||" iti

[vaikhānasapāñcarātrikayoḥ caturmūrtiṣu saṃjñāyāṃ vaiṣamyam]

[23.14]     tathā āśvamedhike:
[23.15]     yudhiṣṭhirah: "kathaṃ tvamarcanīyo'si mūrtayaḥ kīdṛśāśca te |  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1652]
[23.16]     vaikhānasāḥ kathaṃ brūyuḥ kathaṃ vā pāñcarātrikāḥ   || [ṃB.h14, āpp. 4.1653]
[23.17]     śrībhagavān: "śṛṇu pāṇḍava tatsarvamarcanākramamātmanaḥ |  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1654]
[23.18]     sthaṇḍile padmakaṃ kṛtvā sāṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam   || [ṃB.h14, āpp. 4.1655]
[23.19]     aṣṭākṣaravidhānena athavā dvādaśākṣaraiḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1656]
[23.20]     vaidikairathavā mantrairmama sūktena vā punaḥ   || [ṃB.h14, āpp. 4.1657]
[23.21]     sthitaṃ māṃ mantratastasminarcayetsusamahitaḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1658]
[23.22]     viṣṇuñca puruṣaṃ satyamacyutañca yudhiṣṭhira   || [ṃB.h14, āpp. 4.1659]
[23.23]     aniruddhañca māṃ prāhurvaikhānasavido janāḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1660]
[23.24]     anye tvevaṃ vijānānti māṃ rājan pāñcarātrikāḥ  || [ṃB.h14, āpp. 4.1661]






[24.1]     vāsudevañca rājendra saṅkarṣaṇamathāpi vā | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1662]
[24.2]     pradyumnañcāniruddhañca caturmūrtiṃ pracakṣate || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1663]
[24.3]     etāścānyāśca rājendra saṃjñābhedena mūrtayaḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1664]
[24.4]     viddhyanarthāntarā eva māmevañcārcayedbudhaḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1665]
[24.5]     tathā pāñcarātre pauṣkarasaṃhitāyām:
[24.6]     "viprā vaikhānasākhyā ye te bhaktāstattvamucyate || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.260 d]
[24.7]     ekantinassusattvasthā dehāntaṃ nānyayājinaḥ |
[24.8]     kartavyamiti deveśaṃ saṃyajante phalaṃ vinā ||" iti [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.261]

[śuddha, vaidika, tāntrikabhedena pūjātraividhyam]

[24.9]     tathā tatraiva tantrabhedanirṇaye:
[24.10]     "śuddhañca vaidikañceti tāntrikañca tridhā bhavet |
[24.11]     pāñcarātreṇa pūjā tu śuddhaṃ viṣṇoriti smṛtam ||  [ড়auṣkaraṣ]

[vaikhānasapūjāyāḥ vaidikatvaṃ]

[24.12]     vaikhānasena sūtreṇa pūjayedviṣṇumavyayam |
[24.13]     vaidikaṃ taditi proktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ praśasyate ||" iti [ড়auṣkaraṣ]
[24.14]     tathā viṣṇusmṛtau:
[24.15]     "pauruṣaṃ sūktamāsthāya ye yajanti dvijottamāḥ |
[24.16]     saṃhitājapamāsthāya te māṃ prāpsyanti vai dvijāḥ ||
[24.17]     alābhe vedamantrāṇāṃ pāñcarātroditena vā ||" iti
[24.18]     tathā saṅkarṣaṇasaṃhitāyām:
[24.19]     "pūjayetpañcarātraistu mantrairmokṣaphalapradaiḥ |
[24.20]     vaikhānasoktairdivyairvā mantraissarvārthasiddhaye ||" iti  [ṣaṅkarṣaṇasaṃhitā]
[24.21]     tathā śilpe hariharādīnāṃ sthānanirṇaye:
[24.22]     "sahasraviprāvāseṣu śataviprādhikeṣu ca |
[24.23]     pañcamūrtividhānena madhye viṣṇuṃ samarcayet ||
[24.24]     tadvāstvaṅgālaye viṣṇuṃ tāntrikeṇa na cārcayet |
[24.25]     arcayedvaidikoktena vaikhānasavidhānataḥ ||" iti





[vaiṣṇavasamayasya traividhyam]

[25.1]     tathā jyautiṣe hayagrīvasaṃhitāyām:
[25.2]     "śrīśāstreṇaiva kartavyaṃ grāmamadhyālayārcanam |
[25.3]     pāñcarātravidhānena nācarediti śāsanam ||" iti  [ḥyagrīvasaṃhitā]
[25.4]     tathā śaive ca suprabhede:
[25.5]     "sahasrabhūsurādūrdhve grāme brahmāṅkaṇe'pi ca |
[25.6]     vaikhānasena sūtreṇa niṣekādisusaṃskṛtaiḥ || [ṣuprabhedā ]
[25.7]     bhārgavādimahātantramantrabhedavicakṣaṇaiḥ |
[25.8]     anuddhṛtairmantragaṇairvedavedāntasambhavaiḥ || [ṣuprabhedā ]
[25.9]     kramādhyayanasampannaissāṅgopāṅgaiśca saṃskṛtaiḥ |
[25.10]     pañcamūrtiprakāreṇa pratiṣṭhāpyārcayeddharim   || [ṣuprabhed.ā ]
[25.11]     vaidikaṃ taditi proktaṃ rājarāṣṭravivardhanam ||" iti. [ṣuprabedhā ]
[25.12]     tathā kāraṇe grāmavinyāsavidhau:
[25.13]     "brahmāṃśaṃ tu caturbhāgaṃ kṛtvā vāyavyabhāgataḥ |
[25.14]     pañcamūrtikrameṇaiva viṣṇuṃ saṃsthāpya pūjayet ||" iti [ṣuprabhedā ]
[25.15]     tathā bhīmasaṃhitāyāṃ samayabhedopanyāsāvasare:
[25.16]     "śaivamevaṃ samākhyātaṃ vaiṣṇavan tvadhunocyate |
[25.17]     vaiṣṇavañca tridhā proktaṃ tathā samayabhedavat || [Bhīmasaṃhitā]
[25.18]     pāñcarātraṃ bhāgavataṃ tathā vaikhānasaṃ matam |
[25.19]     śuddhaṃ miśraṃ tathā saumyaṃ yathā saṃkhyakrameṇa tu || [Bhīmasaṃhitā]
[25.20]     vaidikācārabāhyaṃ yattadraudramiti kīrtitam |
[25.21]     vaidikācārasambandhātsaumyaṃ śreṣṭhamudāhṛtam ||  [Bhīmasaṃhitā]
[25.22]     grāmādīnāṃ samīpe tu miśroktaṃ sthāpayedbudhaḥ |
[25.23]     abdhinadyāditīreṣu śuddhoktaṃ sthāpayettathā || [Bhīmasaṃhitā]
[25.24]     viprāvāseṣu sarveṣu saumyoktenaiva kārayet ||" iti  [Bhīmasaṃhitā]





[26.1]     tathā gāruḍe: "adhvaryubhirvipravarairmate vikhanasassthitaiḥ |
[26.2]     tatrārādhanamīhasva viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ ||" iti [ṅaruḍaড় ]

[śrīraṅge vaikhānasārcanam]

[26.3]     tathā tatraiva śrīraṅgamāhātmye:
[26.4]     "brahmā'pi divyahaṃsena sitameghopamena ca |
[26.5]     pṛṣṭhato'nuyayau viṣṇuṃ vastreṇācchādya cānanam   || [ṅaruḍa.ড় ]
[26.6]     tatrārcako mahābhāgo bhagavān vikhanāstathā |
[26.7]     dadau prasādaṃ devānāṃ raṅganāthābhyanujñayā ||" iti [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[26.8]     tathā uparicaravasucarite:
[26.9]     "vaikhānasānāṃ vacanairjapahomārcanādibhiḥ |
[26.10]     yajeddhariṃ surapatiṃ bhūmervivarago'pi san ||" iti [ṅaruḍaড় ]

[vṛṣagirau vaikhānasārcanam]

[26.11]     tathā vṛṣagirimāhātmye:
[26.12]     "antaḥpraviśya munayassaha vaikhānasairdvijair |
[26.13]     praṇamantyanvahaṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam ||" iti  [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[26.14]     tathā āgneyapurāṇe phullāraṇyamāhātmye:
[26.15]     "phullaśiṣyāstu yakṣāste pūjayanti yuge yuge |
[26.16]     vaikhānasena vidhinā yathā phullamukhāccyutam || [agmipurāṇa  ]
[26.17]     mānuṣāḥ pūjayantīha vaikhānasamate sthitāḥ ||" iti  [agmipurāṇa  ]
[26.18]     tathā brahmakaivarte puṣkaratīrthavaibhavavarṇane:
[26.19]     "nimnagānāṃ yathā gaṅgā devānāṃ bhagavān hariḥ |
[26.20]     varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇāśśreṣṭhā āśramāṇāṃ yatiryathā || [Brahmakaivartaড় ]
[26.21]     śrutīnāmādibhūtānāmādirekāyanī yathā |
[26.22]     vyūhānāmādibhūtastu vāsudevo yathā varaḥ |
[26.23]     trimūrtīnāṃ yathā viṣṇuśśivānān tu sadāśivaḥ || [Brahmakaivartaড় ]

[vipreṣu vaikhānasānāṃ, sūtreṣu vaikhānasasūtrasya ca śreṣṭhyam]

[26.24]     dharmāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavo dharmaḥ smṛtīnāṃ mānavī smṛtiḥ |
[26.25]     viprāṇāṃ vedaviduṣāṃ yathā vaikhānaso varaḥ || [Brahmakaivartaড় ]




[27.1]     sūtrāṇāṃ tatpraṇītan tu yathā śreṣṭhatamaṃ smṛtam |
[27.2]     tathaiva puṣkaro rājaṃstīrthānāmuttamottamaḥ ||" [Brahmakaivartaড় ]
[27.3-6]     iti ca pratipādanātīdṛśānāṃ vaikhānasānāmeva śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇādiṣu caturmukhātsāmānyatvena vinā sarvotkṛṣṭatvenotpattiśravaṇātbrahmasabhāpraveśasūryapathādyāśrayaṇādipratipādanācca vaikhānasā na tṛtīyāśramaniṣṭhāḥ, na vā brahmaṇā sāmānyataḥ sṛṣṭā bhavitumarhanti.

[jambudvīpe bhāratavarśasya śreṣṭhyam]

[27.7]     śrīviṣṇupurāṇe: "atrāpi bhārataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ jambūdvīpe mahāmune |
[27.8]     yato hi karmabhūreṣā hyato'nyā bhogabhūmayaḥ   || [Viṣṇu.ড় 2.2.22]
[27.9]     kṛtaṃ tretā dvāparañca kaliścānyatra na kvacit |
[27.10]     tapastapyanti yatayo juhvate cātra yajvanaḥ ||  [Viṣṇuড় ]
[27.11]     dānāni cātra dīyante paralokārthamādarāt |
[27.12]     puruṣairyajñapuruṣo jambūdvīpe sadejyate   || [Viṣṇu.ড় ]
[27.13]     yajñairyajñamayo viṣṇuranyadvīpeṣu cānyathā ||  [Viṣṇuড় ]
[27.14]     atra janmasahasrāṇāṃ sahasrairapi sattama |
[27.15]     kadācillabhate janturmānuṣyaṃ puṇyasañcayāt ||" [Viṣṇuড় 2.2.23]
[27.16-19]     iti bhāratakhaṇḍavyatiriktasthaleṣu brahmacaryādyāśramādivyavasthāvyavahārādyabhavātpāñcarātraśilpajyautiṣādiṣu eṣāmadvārakabhagavadyajanādhikāritvaparamavaidikaparamaikāntitvādeḥ pratipādanāṅca eṣveva vaikhānasatvaṃ naisargikam. vānaprastheṣu vaikhānasatvaṃ na naisargikam.
[27.20-22]     kintu teṣāṃ sūtrāntareṣvanuktānāṃ vānaprasthāśramasvīkāraprayogaśrāmaṇakāgnikuṇḍavidhānādīnāṃ vikhanassūtre pratipāditānāṃ tatsūtroktaprakāreṇa vānaprasthāśramasvīkārādinaiva vaikhānasatvasiddhiriti.
[27.23]     idamevāha baudhāyanaḥ:

[vānaprasthānāṃ mukhyavṛttyā vaikhānasaśabdavācyatvābhāvaḥ]

[27.24-28.2]      "vānaprastho vaikhānasaśāstrasamudācāro vane mūlaphalāśī tapaśśīlassavaneṣūdakamupaspṛśan śrāmaṇakenāgnimādhāyāgrāmyabhojī devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtarṣipūjakaḥ sarvātithiḥ pratiṣiddhavarjaṃ bhaikṣamapyupayuñjīta. na phālakṛṣṭamadhitiṣṭhetgrāmaṃ vā na praviśet" iti.





[28.3]     manuḥ: "ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi dharmaṃ vaikhānasāśrayam ||" ityārabhya
[28.4]     "puṣpamūlaphalairvāpi kevalairvartayetsadā |
[28.5]     kālapakvaissvayaṃ śīrṇairvaikhānasamate sthitaḥ ||" iti [ṃnuḍhś6.21]
[28.6-8]     hārīta: "vānaprastho grāmyā oṣadhīḥ parivarjayet. tretāgnīnāṃ sthāne śrāmaṇakaṃ vāgnimādhāya valkalaśāṇacarmakuśamuñjādivalkalaṃ vā vasāno vaikhānasaproktena vidhinā vānaprastho bhavati. anujñāyiko'nuprasthāyika" iti.
[28.9]     yama: "śrāmaṇakena vidhānena sāyaṃ prātarupaspṛśed" iti.
[28.10-11]     śaṅkhalikhitau: "śrāmaṇakenāgnimādhāya kāle kuruvindaveṇuśyāmākanīvārādibhirvartayed" iti.
[28.12]     vasiṣṭho'pi: "śrāmaṇakenāgnimādhāyāhitāgnirvṛkṣamūlika" iti.
[28.13]     asya yajñasvāmivyākhyāne:
[28.14]     "śrāmaṇakaṃ nāma vaikhānasaśāstram. taduktena vidhināgnimādhāya" iti.
[28.15]     śukānuśāsane vyāso'pi:
[28.16]     "mūlaireke phalaireke puṣpaireke dṛḍhavratāḥ | [ṃBh12.236.13 d]
[28.17]     vartayanti yathānyāyaṃ vaikhānasamate sthitāḥ ||" iti [ṃBh12.236.14 b]
[28.18]     smṛtyantaramapi: "yasminneva tu samproktaṃ sūtre vikhanasā param |
[28.19]     vanasthānān tu sarveṣāṃ vidhiśrāmaṇakābhidhaḥ ||
[28.20]     vānaprasthāstatassarve ye dvijāstvanyasūtriṇaḥ |
[28.21]     tatsūtravidhyanuṣṭhānātsmṛtā vaikhānasāstu te ||" iti ca varṇayanti. [āṣ11.4]
[28.22]     ataśca vānaprasthā mukhyatvena vaikhānasaśabdavācyā na bhavanti.
[28.23]     ānuśāsanike ca:
[28.24]     "taṃ patantamabhiprekṣya prekṣya ca rṣigaṇānatha | [ṃBh13, āpp. 1ā.313]
[28.25]     āsīnāna vasubhissārdhaṃ satreṇa jagatīpateḥ ||  [ṃBh13, āpp. 1ā.314]





[29.1]     vaikhānasānāmeteṣāṃ vālakhilyagaṇānapi | [ṃBh13, āpp. 1ā.315]
[29.2]     tasya bhīrāviśattatra patagendrasya bhārata   || [ṃB.h13, āpp. 1ā.316]
[29.3]     tuṇḍena gṛhya tāṃ śākhāmutpapāta khageśvaraḥ ||" iti [ṃBh13, āpp. 1ā.318]
[29.4]     tatraivomāmaheśvarasaṃvāde:
[29.5]     "vaikhānasānāṃ vai dharmaṃ śrotumicchāmi dharmataḥ || ityārabhya
[29.6]     teṣu vaikhānasā nāma vānaprasthāśśubhekṣaṇe | [ṃBh13, āpp. 15.702]
[29.7]     tīvreṇa tapasā yuktā dīptimantassutejasaḥ ||" iti [ṃBh13, āpp. 15.703]
[29.8]     harivaṃśe ca akrūrāgamanasamaye:
[29.9]     "śakapotrāvyayā? mode pradoṣe'bhyāśataskare | [ḥriV 68.4]
[29.10]     kiñcidabhyudite some śātiteṣu tamassu ca || [ḥriv68.9]
[29.11]     vanyairvaikhānasairmantraiḥ hūyamāne hutāśane ||" iti. [ḥriV 68.5 d]
[29.12]     pādme ca: "brāhmaṇaṃ vṛddhahārītaṃ vaikhānasamate sthitam ||" iti ca varṇanāt[ড়dmaড় ]
[29.13-15]     vānaprasthasya śrautaviṣaye pṛthaktvena mantrapratipādanābhāvācca bodhāyanādibhiruktaprakāreṇa vānaprasthasya vaikhānasatvaṃ vaikhānasasūtroktavānaprasthāśramasvīkārādevetyavagantavyam. etāvatā vikhanaśśabdārtho nirṇītaḥ.
[29.16]     (ādyahetunirūpaṇaprakāraḥ)

[vaikhānasasūtraśāstrayorvikhanasā praṇītatvam]

[29.17-18]     atha vaikhānasasūtraśāstrayoḥ akhilajagatkāraṇabhūtena vikhanasā praṇītatvamupapādyate.
[29.19]     viṣṇudharme: "kathayāmi yathāpūrvaṃ matpitrā kathitaṃ mama |
[29.20]     tasyāpi kila tatpitrā tasmai cāha kilośanāḥ || [Viṣṇuḍh1.38]





[30.1]     tenāpi bhṛgumārādhya prāptamārādhanaṃ hareḥ |
[30.2]     sakāśādbrahmaṇaḥ prāptaṃ bhṛguṇāpi mahātmanā   || [Viṣṇuḍ.h1.39]
[30.3]     prāptaṃ sakāśāddevasya brahmaṇo'vyaktajanmanaḥ ||" iti. [Viṣṇuḍh1.40 b]

[vaikhānasamārgasya upadeśaparamaṃparā]

[30.4]     hārītasmṛtau: "pūrvaṃ vikhanaso viṣṇuḥ proktavān svayamacyutaḥ |
[30.5]     bhṛgoḥ proktaṃ vikhanasā bhṛguṇā ca mameritam ||" iti

[pāñcarātrāgamotpattyādiprakāraḥ]

[30.6]     gāruḍe: "purā caturmukhādeśāccatvāro munayo'malāḥ |
[30.7]     praṇīya vaiṣṇavaṃ śāstraṃ bhūmāvabhyarcayannṛpa || [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[30.8]     marīcirmandare viṣṇumarcayamāsa keśavam |
[30.9]     ādeśādbrahmaṇo viṣṇuṃ śrīnivāse'trirarcayet || [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[30.10]     kaśyapo viṣṇvadhiṣṭhāne śubhakṣetre bhṛgurmuniḥ |
[30.11]     gaṅgāyā dakṣiṇe tīre sīmni tasyāntike'pi ca || [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[30.12]     tatsandhau śuciṣaṇṇāmā bhṛguṇā sthāpito hariḥ ||" iti coktam  [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[30.13]     pāñcarātre: "ādye kaliyuge prāpte somakena hṛtā trayī |
[30.14]     avaidikeṣu lokeṣu daivateṣu muniṣvapi ||
[30.15]     bhagavantaṃ samāsādya nārāyaṇamanāmayam |
[30.16]     ananto garuḍaścaiva viṣvakseṇaḥ pitāmahaḥ ||
[30.17]     śaṅkaraścaiva pañcaite papracchuḥ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
[30.18]     kṣaṇamapyacyutasyārcāṃ tyaktumeva na śaknumaḥ ||
[30.19]     avaidikeṣu lokeṣu kiṃ kurmaścalamānasāḥ |
[30.20]     tasmādupāyaṃ lakṣmīśa vadāsmākaṃ cirantana ||
[30.21]     ityuktassa haristebhyaḥ pañcarātravidhiṃ kramāt |
[30.22]     ekaikarātramekasmai tantraṃ samupadiṣṭavān ||
[30.23]     pañcalakṣapramāṇena pañcarātraṃ prapañcitam ||" ityupakramya
[30.24]     "prathamaṃ brahmarātran tu dvitīyaṃ rudrarātrakam |
[30.25]     tṛtīyamindrarātraṃ yaccaturthaṃ graharātrakam ||




[31.1]     pañcamaṃ munirātran tu pañcarātraṃ vidhīyate |
[31.2]     rātribhiḥ pañcabhiḥ proktaṃ śrutaṃ śiṣyaiśca pañcabhiḥ ||
[31.3]     tasmāttatpañcarātrākhyāṃ labdhvā tiṣṭhati bhūtale ||"
[31.4-5]     ityantena pāñcarātrasyotpattyādikaṃ nirūpya paramavaidikasya śrīvaikhānasasyotpattyādikaṃ pratipādyate.

[vaikhānasaśāstrotpattyādiprakāraḥ]

[31.6]     atha matsyākṛtiḥ śrīśaḥ praviśyāmbudhimadhyamam |
[31.7]     nirmathya somakaṃ vedānadātpaṅkajayonaye ||
[31.8]     tādṛśaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ stotraissantoṣya padmabhūḥ |
[31.9]     uvāca vacanaṃ premṇā daṇḍavatpraṇipatya ca ||
[31.10]     tāntrikeṇa tvayā proktaṃ mārgeṇa bhavadarcanam |
[31.11]     na prasīdati tenāsmanmanaḥ kamalalocana ||
[31.12]     vaidikena tvadarcāṃ me yathāpūrvaṃ vadācyuta |
[31.13]     ityukto bhagavān devaḥ śāstraṃ śrutipathāgatam ||
[31.14]     sahasrakoṭibhiḥ ślokaissaṅkhyātaṃ bahuvistaram |
[31.15]     sūtramūlamanādyantaṃ kalpe kalpe samāśritam ||
[31.16]     uvāca jagatāṃ prītyai yajñānāṃ pūraṇāya ca |
[31.17]     mūlaṃ sarvāgamānāñca purāṇānāṃ tathaiva ca ||
[31.18]     smṛtīnāṃ sarvasūtrāṇāṃ pratyaṅgopāṅgaśobhinām |
[31.19]     vedāśca vikhanaśśāstraṃ pramāṇamiti cerayan ||
[31.20]     yathā vaikhānasaṃ pūrve'han sāmabhavatīti ca |
[31.21]     ye nakhā bhuvi sañjātāste vai vaikhānasā iti ||
[31.22]     śrutyuktaṃ tadidaṃ śāstraṃ vaikhānasamahārṇavam |
[31.23]     ityuktvā bhagavānādyastatraivāntaradhīyata ||
[31.24]     tataḥ paraṃ caturvaktro jaṭākāṣāyadaṇḍabhṛt |
[31.25]     naimiśāraṇyamāsādya dvijasaṅghaniṣevitam ||





[32.1]     tapastaptvā ciraṃ kālaṃ dhyāyaṃstejastu vaiṣṇavam |
[32.2]     paścādapaśyadviṣṇūktamāgamaṃ vistarāttadā ||
[32.3]     saśrautañca sagṛhyañca vedamantrairabhiṣṭutam |
[32.4]     saṃkṣipya sāramādāya śāṇollikhitaratnavat ||
[32.5]     dhātā vikhanaso nāma marīcyādisutānmunīn |
[32.6]     abodhayadidaṃ śāstraṃ sārdhakoṭipramāṇataḥ ||
[32.7]     kalpe kalpe purā viṣṇurudbhūtaḥ pūrvavatsadā |
[32.8]     tasmādvaidikamācāraṃ yaḥ kartuṃ bhuvi vāñchati ||
[32.9]     tasyedaṃ śāstramityuktaṃ netareṣābhitīritam ||"

[vaikhānasasūtrasya tatsūtriṇāṃ ca māhātmyam]

[32.10]     purātantre saptacatvāriṃśe'dhyāye:
[32.11]     ṛṣaya ūcuḥ: "vaikhānasasya sūtrasya kiṃ māhātmyaṃ mahāmune |
[32.12]     vihāya sarvasūtrāṇi yattvayā cānuvartitam   || [ড়urātantra.]
[32.13]     tatsūtriṇāñca māhātmyaṃ śrotumicchāmahe vayam |
[32.14]     samūrtasyāsya yajñasya ye yogyāḥ iti kīrtitāḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[32.15]     bhṛguḥ: yuṣmābhiḥ paripṛṣṭaṃ yatsamyagvakṣyāmi suvratāḥ |
[32.16]     śruṇvantu munayassarve sūtramāhātmyamuttamam ||  [ড়urātantra]
[32.17]     suptotthitaściraṃ brahmā sraṣṭukāmaḥ purābhavat |
[32.18]     sasmāra vedānakhilānna sṛṣṭissampravartate   || [ড়urātantra.]
[32.19]     ciraṃ suptasya vai dhātustamasākrāntacetasaḥ |
[32.20]     vismṛtā nikhilā vedā hṛdaye tasya saṃsthitāḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[32.21]     cintāturastadā brahmā nālaṅkartuṃ kimapyabhūt |
[32.22]     tataścintān tu tāṃ tyaktvā svasthacittaḥ prajāpatiḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[32.23]     hṛdpadmamadhye puruṣaṃ parameṇa samādhinā |
[32.24]     arcayāmāsa vai viṣṇumṛgyajussāmarūpiṇam   || [ড়urātantra.]






[dakṣādinavabrahmasṛṣṭiḥ]

[33.1]     tuṣṭāva ca tadā brahmā haripravaṇamānāsaḥ |
[33.2]     nanāma ca tadā viṣṇuṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam   || [ড়urātantra.]
[33.3]     bruvato bhagavaddhyānātvinaṣṭatamaso vidheḥ |
[33.4]     sattvodriktasya tasyāśu nairmalyaṃ hṛdaye'bhavat ||  [ড়urātantra]
[33.5]     tatastu bhagavān brahmā chandomūlamavāptavān |
[33.6]     tasya dakṣiṇato vaktrāttuṣṭāva jagataḥ patim ||  [ড়urātantra]
[33.7]     sasmāra vividhān vedān sāṅgānupaniṣadgaṇān |
[33.8]     purāṇanyāyamīmāṃsā dharmaśāstrāṇi sarvaśaḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[33.9]     antarhitānāṃ khananādvedānān tu viśeṣataḥ |
[33.10]     sa vibhuḥ procyate sarvairvikhanā brahmavādibhiḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[33.11]     vaikhānasaśca bhagavān procyate sa pitāmahaḥ |
[33.12]     tatassasarja bhagavān viśvayoniḥ pitāmahaḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[33.13]     jagatsarvamaśeṣeṇa vedadṛṣṭena vartmanā |
[33.14]     prāṇācca cakṣuṣastadvadabhimānācca karmaṇaḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[33.15]     hṛdayācchirasaśśrotrādudānādvyānatastataḥ |
[33.16]     samānācca tathāpānādṛṣṭiśreṣṭhānimān daśa || [ড়urātantra]
[33.17]     dakṣaṃ marīcinaṃ nīlalohitaṃ bhṛgumeva ca |
[33.18]     tathāṅgirasamatriñca pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ tathā || [ড়urātantra]
[33.19]     vasiṣṭhañca kratuñcaiva kramādasṛjadabjabhūḥ |
[33.20]     nava brahmāṇa evaite vinā syurnīlalohitam || [ড়urātantra]
[33.21]     vedānāṃ vyasanādarvākprāgrūpaṃ militan tu yat |
[33.22]     tān tu vaikhānasīṃ śākhāmādāvadhyāpayanmunīn || [ড়urātantra]
[33.23]     (nāmnā vikhanasaṃ prāhuryañca vaikhānasaṃ tathā || [ড়urātantra]
[33.24]     bhṛgvaṅgiromarīcyatripulastyapulahāḥ kratuḥ |






[34.1]     tathā vasiṣṭho dakṣaśca nava brahmāṇa īritāḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[34.2]     nava brahmāṇa evaite vinā syurnīlalohitam || [ড়urātantra]

[dakṣādīnāṃ navānāṃ vikhanaśśiśyatvam]

[34.3]     ete vikhanasaśśiṣyāḥ śrutismṛtividhānataḥ |
[34.4]     tacchiṣyāstu mahātmano munayastattvadarśinaḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[34.5]     vedānugāni śāstrāṇi cakrurlokahitaiṣiṇaḥ |
[34.6]     kecitsūtraṇi vai cakruḥ gṛhyaśrautātmikāni tu ||  [ড়urātantra]
[34.7]     dharmaśāstrāṇi kecittu purāṇāni ca kecana |
[34.8]     itihāsāṃstathā kalpān procuranye mahārṣayaḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]

[vaikānasaśākhāniṣpattiprakāraḥ]

[34.9]     tān tu vaikhānasīṃ śākhāṃ svasūtre viniyuktavān |
[34.10]     padmabhūḥ paramo dhātā tasminnārādhanatrayam   || [ড়urātantra.]
[34.11]     tān tu vaikhānasīṃ śākhāṃ vyāsastu bhagavānmuniḥ |
[34.12]     cakre ṛgādibhedaistu vyasitvā tu pṛthakpṛthak ||  [ড়urātantra]
[34.13]     audgātraṃ sāmabhiryāge brāhmamātharvaṇena ca |
[34.14]     hautramṛgbhiryajurmantrairādhvaryavamakārayat  || [ড়urātantra.]
[34.15]     āmnāyasya vidhirbrāhmaḥ pañcakāṇḍātmikā tu sā |
[34.16]     sā ca vaikhānasī śākhā taittirīyā ca kīrtitā ||  [ড়urātantra]
[34.17]     ādikāle tu bhagavān brahmā tu vikhānā muniḥ |
[34.18]     yajuśśākhānusāreṇa cakre sūtraṃ mahattaram ||  [ড়urātantra]
[34.19]     varṇāśramācārayutaṃ śrautasmārtasamanvitam |
[34.20]     yasminneva tu samproktaṃ sūtre vikhanasā param ||  [ড়urātantra]
[34.21]     vanasthānān tu sarveṣāṃ vidhiśśrāmaṇakābhidhaḥ |
[34.22]     vānaprasthāstatassarve ye dvijāścānyasūtriṇaḥ |
[34.23]     tatsūtravidhyanuṣṭhānātsmṛtā vaikhānasāstu te || [ড়urātantra]
[34.24]     yatsūtrādyantamadhyeṣu bhagavān viṣṇuravyayaḥ |





[35.1]     yaṣṭavyo gīyate yasmātsarvasūtrottaman tu tat || [ড়urātantra]
[35.2]     vede vaikhānase sūtre yo dharmaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
[35.3]     sarvaissa dharmo'nuṣṭheyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||  [ড়urātantra]

[vaikhānasasūtrasya sūtrāntarānapekṣatvam]

[35.4]     svasūtrasya parityāgādanyasūtrasya saṃśrayāt |
[35.5]     sadyaḥ patati vai vipro na vedasya samāśraye || [ড়urātantra]
[35.6]     etadvaikhānasaṃ sūtramanyaśāstrānapekṣitam || [ড়urātantra]
[35.7]     etadvaikhānasaṃ sūtraṃ sarvavedārthasaṅgraham |
[35.8]     vaiṣṇavaṃ sarvaviprāṇāṃ sāmānyamabhidhīyate || [ড়urātantra]
[35.9]     vaikhānasasya sūtrasya cāgneḥ śrāmaṇakasya ca |
[35.10]     nārāyaṇasya devasya māhātmyaṃ nāvabuddhyate || [ড়urātantra]
[35.11]     yathā vedeṣu sarveṣu sāmavedaḥ praśasyate |
[35.12]     tathā sūtreṣu sarveṣu sūtrametadpraśasyate || [ড়urātantra]
[35.13]     agnirvaikhānasaṃ śāstraṃ viṣṇurvedāśca śāśvatāḥ |
[35.14]     gāyatrī vaiṣṇavā viprāssaptaite'tipavitrakāḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[35.15]     yadadhīteṣu vedeṣu sāṅgeṣu labhate phalam |
[35.16]     tatphalaṃ labhate sadyaḥ sūtrametatpaṭhan dvijaḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[35.17]     agnīṣomādayo devā ye yajñāṃśahavirbhujaḥ |
[35.18]     ......
[35.19]     śārīrāṇyanyasūtrāṇi tathā svargaphalāni ca |
[35.20]     vaiṣṇavaṃ sūtramettu sarvasiddhikaraṃ param || [ড়urātantra]
[35.21]     ādyatvātsarvasūtrāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavatvācca tattu vai |
[35.22]     mayānuvartitaṃ tadvatkaśyapātrimarīcibhiḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[35.23]     gṛhe puruṣapūjāyā vidhānamṛṣisattamāḥ |




[36.1]     uktaṃ saṅkepataḥ sūtrenoktamālayapūjanam || [ড়urātantra]
[36.2]     proktaṃ vikhanasā sūtre ṛṣīṇāṃ brahmavādinām |
[36.3]     teṣān tu havirādatte viṣṇussākṣātsanātanaḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[36.4]     śatakratūnāṃ yajato yatphalaṃ parikīrtitam |

[ālaye pañcamūrtyarcanasya śraiṣṭhyam]

[36.5]     ālaye pañcamūrtīnāmarcanan tvadhikaṃ bhavet ||  [ড়urātantra]
[36.6]     ālāyārcā gṛhārceti cobhayaṃ śruticoditam |
[36.7]     tenādau sakalaṃ noktaṃ granthavistarabhīruṇā ||  [ড়urātantra]
[36.8]     uktaśeṣamanuktañca vistareṇa mayocyate |
[36.9]     viṣṇuñca puruṣaṃ satyamacyutañcāniruddhakam || [ড়urātantra]
[36.10]     "eṣa puruṣaḥ pañcadhā pañcātme" tyāha ca śrutiḥ |
[36.11]     asmābhirapi tatsūtra- matagāmibhireva tu || [ড়urātantra]
[36.12]     pañcamūrtividhānena śāstrametadudāhṛtam |
[36.13]     tasmādatra mayoktasya homasyāgnyālayasya ca || [ড়urātantra]
[36.14]     puṇyāhavāstusāmānya- homādīnāñca karmaṇām |
[36.15]     ye'nye ca mantrāste grāhyāssūtre vikhanasā smṛtāḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[36.16]     ālayārcavidhisso'yaṃ vaidikassarvasiddhidaḥ |
[36.17]     sarvalokahitārthāya prokto'smābhirviśeṣataḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]

[vaikhānasānaṃ garbhavaiṣṇavatvam]

[36.18]     ālayārcaṇakarmedaṃ vaidikaṃ bhagavatpriyam |
[36.19]     anuṣṭheyaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhaiḥ tadvaikhānasasūtribhiḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[36.20]     ye vaikhānasasūtreṇa saṃskṛtāstu dvijātayaḥ |
[36.21]     te viṣṇusadṛśā jñeyāḥ sarveṣāmuttamottamāḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[36.22]     vaikhānasānāṃ sarveṣāṃ garbhavaiṣṇavajanmanām |
[36.23]     nārāyaṇaḥ svayaṃ garbhe mudrāṃ dhārayate nijām || [ড়urātantra]

[vaikhānasapūjāyaḥ bhagavatprītikaratvam]

[36.24]     viprā vaikhānasākhyā ye te smṛtā bhagavatpriyāḥ |





[37.1]     ekāntinassusattvasthā dehāntaṃ nānyayājinaḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[37.2]     viṣṇoḥ priyatamā loke catvāraḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
[37.3]     aśvatthaḥ kapilā gāvastulasī vikhanāstathā  || [ড়urātantra.]
[37.4]     dvijeṣu brāhmaṇāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ brāhmaṇeṣu ca vaiṣṇavāḥ |
[37.5]     vaiṣṇaveṣu dvijāḥ śreṣṭhā ye vaikhānasasūtriṇaḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[37.6]     vaiṣṇavīṃ pratimāṃ loke vipraṃ vaikhānasaṃ tathā |
[37.7]     gaṅgāṃ viṣṇupadīṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || [ড়urātantra]
[37.8]     tasmādvaikhānasā viprā vaiṣṇavā munisattamāḥ |
[37.9]     ālayārcanayogyāśca yogyā nānye dvijātayaḥ |
[37.10]     purā tu pṛṣṭavān brahmā devadevaṃ janārdanam ||  [ড়urātantra]
[37.11]     "kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa pūjanīyo'si kairapi |"
[37.12]     ityevaṃ bhagavān pṛṣṭaḥ prāha vai caturānanam ||  [ড়urātantra]
[37.13]     "ālaye tyarcanaṃ syāttu parivārānvitasya me |
[37.14]     vaikhānasavidhānena vaidikena viśeṣataḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[37.15]     vaikhānasaistu viprendrairarcanaṃbishiernochṭelugu vergleichenmama tuṣṭidam |
[37.16]     amūrtañca samūrtañca dvidhā vaidikamucyate || [ড়urātantra]
[37.17]     amūrtaṃ gārhapatyādyaṃ samūrtaṃ berapūjanam |
[37.18]     yathā karmaṇa ekasya kalpitāḥ pañca vahnayaḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[37.19]     tathā caikavimānasya pañcaberāṇi kalpayet |
[37.20]     grāmādāvālaye tasmāddevadevo janārdanaḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[37.21]     anena vidhinā pūjyo viprairvaikhānasaistathā |
[37.22]     pūjā vaikhānasairviprairālaye syādviśeṣataḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[37.23]     sarvasampatkarañcaiva sarvāśubhavināśanam |
[37.24]     tadgrāmavāsināṃ tadvadaihikāmuṣmikapradam || [ড়urātantra]





[avaikhānasapūjane doṣaḥ, tatprāyaścittañca]

[38.1]     varṣadaṃ puṣṭidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rājarāṣṭrābhivṛddhidam |
[38.2]     karṣaṇādipratiṣṭhārcāprāyaścittāvasānakam |
[38.3]     anuṣṭheyo vidhisso'yaṃ viprairvaikhānasaistataḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[38.4]     avaikhānasasūtraistu viprairārādhanaṃ hareḥ |
[38.5]     vālaye sarvalokānāṃ vināśāya bhaviṣyati || [ড়urātantra]
[38.6]     ajñānādarthalobhādvā bhayādvā'pyālayārcanam |
[38.7]     yadyavaikhānasairviprairkriyate sarvanāśanam ||  [ড়urātantra]
[38.8]     yadyavaikhānaso vipra ālayārcanamācaret |
[38.9]     ṣaṇmāsātpatanaṃ yāti narakañcaiva gacchati || [ড়urātantra]
[38.10]     api sāṅgacaturvedī yadyavaikhānaso dvijaḥ |
[38.11]     ālaye tvarcanaṃ kuryātsadyaḥ patati devalaḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[38.12]     avaikhānasavipro yaḥ pūjayedālaye harim |
[38.13]     sa vai devalako nāma sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[38.14]     ālāpaṃ darśanañcaiva sparśanañca viśeṣataḥ |
[38.15]     śrāddhādau varaṇañcaiva tasya viprasya varjayet || [ড়urātantra]
[38.16]     avaikhānasasūtreṇa spṛṣṭe bimbe pramādataḥ |
[38.17]     snāpayetkalaśairdevaṃ prokṣayetpañcagavyakaiḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[38.18]     evameva tathā kuryādviprairanyaiḥ prapūjite |
[38.19]     kalaśaissnāpayitvā tu dhruvoktāṃ śuddhimācaret || [ড়urātantra]
[38.20]     tryahan tu pūjite tadvadvāstuhomaṃ samāpya ca |
[38.21]     paryagnipañcagavyābhyāṃ mahāśāntiñca kārayet || [ড়urātantra]
[38.22]     arcane tu mahāviṣṇossaptāhan tu viśeṣataḥ |
[38.23]     mahāśāntiṃ yajitvā tu śuddhiṃ pūrvavadācaret || [ড়urātantra]
[38.24]     dvādaśāhaṃ samārabhya māsāntan tu viśeṣataḥ |





[39.1]     avaikhānasapūjāyāṃ tantrasaṅkara eva ca || [ড়urātantra]

[avaikhānasapūjane bhagavatsānnidhyavirahaḥ]

[39.2]     māsatrayaṃ ca ṣaṇmāsamanyavipraistu pūjane |
[39.3]     na tatra bimbe sānnidhyaṃ devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ || [ড়urātantra]
[39.4]     tasmāttaddoṣaśāntyarthaṃ mahāśānteranantaram |
[39.5]     adhivāsatrayaṃ kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāṃ punarācaret || [ড়urātantra]
[39.6]     saṃvatsare tu devasya viprairvaikhānasetaraiḥ |
[39.7]     pūjābhicārikī proktā tatra sarvaṃ vinaśyati || [ড়urātantra]
[39.8]     tasmāttatkarṣaṇādyañca niṣkṛtyantālayārcanam |

[tāntrikārcāyāmapi tantradīkṣitānāmevādhikāraḥ]

[39.9]     kārayedvidhivadvidvān tadvaikhānasasūtribhiḥ |
[39.10]     tāntrikārcāvidhau cāpi tāntrikaireva dīkṣitaiḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[39.11]     pūjanīyo hariḥ samyakvipraiḥ nānyaiḥ kadācana |
[39.12]     anyena vidhinā mantrairanyairanyārcakena vā ||  [ড়urātantra]
[39.13]     ālaye tvarcanaṃ syāccenmahādoṣo bhaveddhruvam |
[39.14]     tasmāttu vaidike tvasminnālayārcāvidhau dvijāḥ   || [ড়urātantra.]
[39.15]     pratiṣṭhādiṣu saṅgrāhyāḥ proktā vaikhānasā dvijāḥ |
[39.16]     brahmāpi bhagavān devo harirnārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[39.17]     nāviṣṇurjāyate viṣṇurnāviṣṇurviṣṇumarcayet |
[39.18]     suprītenārcakenaiva yaduktaṃ devasannidhau   || [ড়urātantra.]
[39.19]     taddevenaiva samproktaṃ tathaiva ca bhaviṣyati |
[39.20]     arcakaṃ toṣayedviṣṇuṃ yastoṣayitumicchati ||  [ড়urātantra]
[39.21]     tuṣṭe'rcake jagannāthastuṣṭha eva na saṃśayaḥ ||  [ড়urātantra]
[39.22]     svapne svarūpaṃ bhaktāya pratyakṣayati mādhavaḥ |
[39.23]     pravakti bhagavān viṣṇuḥ sukhaduḥkhadvayaṃ nṛṇām ||" iti [ড়urātantra]

[arcakamahimā]

[39.24]     skānde:
[39.25]     "vaikhānasīṃ mahāśākhāṃ svasūtre viniyuktavān |





[40.1]     padmabhūḥ paramo dhātā tasminnārādhanatrayam || [ṣkandaড় ]
[40.2]     uktavānnigamārthānāmācāraṃ pravibhāgaśaḥ ||" iti  [ṣkandaড় ]

[ekasya yajurvedasya vyāsakatṛkaṃ vibhajanam]

[40.3]     brahmakaivartavacanāni pūrvamevoktāni.
[40.4-5]     nanu ṛgyajussāmātharvāṇa iti vedānāṃ loke prasiddheḥ sarvaśākhāmūlabhūto yajurveda iti pūrvoktaṃ kathamupapadyate iti ceducyate.
[40.6]     śrīviṣṇupurāṇe
[40.7]     "vedadrumasya maitreya śākhābhedāssahasraśaḥ |
[40.8]     na śakyā vistarādvaktuṃ saṅkṣepeṇa śṛṇuṣva tam   || [Viṣṇu.ড় 3.3.4]
[40.9]     dvāpare dvāpare viṣṇurvyāsarūpī mahāmune   || [Viṣṇu.ড় ]
[40.10]     vedamekaṃ subahudhā karoti jagatāṃ hitaḥ  || [Viṣṇu.ড় 3.3.5]
[40.11]     vīryaṃ tejo balañcālpaṃ manuṣyāṇāmavekṣya ca |
[40.12]     hitāya sarvabhūtānāṃ vedabhedaṃ karoti saḥ   || [Viṣṇu.ড় 3.3.6]
[40.13]     ityārabhya
[40.14]     "eka āsīdyajurvedastaṃ caturdhā hyakalpayat |
[40.15]     cāturhotramabhūdyasmiṃstena yajñamathākarot  || [Viṣṇu.ড় 3.4.11]
[40.16]     ārdhvaryavaṃ yajurbhistu ṛgbhirhautraṃ tathā muniḥ |
[40.17]     audgātraṃ sāmabhiścakre brahmatvañcāpyatharvabhiḥ   || [Viṣṇu.ড় 3.4.12]
[40.18]     tatassa ṛca uddhṛtya ṛgvedaṃ kṛtavānmuniḥ |
[40.19]     yajurbhiśca yajurvedaṃ sāmavedañca sāmābhiḥ   || [Viṣṇu.ড় 3.4.13]
[40.20]     rājñāntvatharvavedena sarvakarmāṇi sa prabhuḥ |
[40.21]     kārayāmāsa maitreya brahmatvañca yathāsthiti |||  [Viṣṇuড় 3.4.14]
[40.22]     so'yameko mahān vedatarustena pṛthakkṛtaḥ |
[40.23]     caturdhā ca tato jātaṃ vedapādapakānanam ||"  [Viṣṇuড় 3.4.15]
[40.24-25]     ityantena spaṣṭaṃ pratipāditatvātyajurveda eka eva prathamāsītityavagamyate.





[yajurvedasya vaikhānasaśākhātvaprasiddhiḥ]

[41.1-3]     sa ca yajurvedaḥ: "bahuśṛṅgaścatussānuḥ pṛthulo vedaparvataḥ" ityārabhyoktarītyā vedavyāsarupiṇā bhagavatā yadā vyastaḥ, tataḥ pūrvamekarūpeṇāvasthitasya tasya vedaparvatasya vaikhānasaśākhātvaprasiddhirāsīditi pūrvameva pratipāditam.
[41.4]     vaikhānasānāṃ śraiṣṭhyam

[vaikhānasānāṃ śreṣṭhyaṃ pañcakālaparāyaṇatvañca]

[41.5-6]     prasaṅgātvaikhānasaśākhāmūlakatvena praṇītasūtrānuṣṭhātṝṇāṃ śreṣṭhatvamacchidrapañcakālaparāyaṇatvādikañca pratipādyate.
[41.7]     yathā sāmabrahmaṇe:
[41.8]     "vaikhānasā vā ṛṣaya indrasya priyā āsan" iti [ChāndogyaBr2.23.1]

[vaikhānasānāṃ bhagavatpriyatamatvam]

[41.9]     vārāhe:
[41.10]     "aśvatthaḥ kapilā gāvastulasī vikhanāstathā |
[41.11]     catvāro matpriyā rājaṃsteṣāṃ vaikhānaso varaḥ ||" iti  [Varāhaড়]

[vaikhānasadarśanaphalam]

[41.12]     vṛddhayājñavalkye:
[41.13]     "vaikhānaso vaidikeṣu brahmanirdiṣṭamācaret |
[41.14]     taṃ brāhmaṇan tu dṛṣṭvaiva sarvaṃ pāpaṃ vyapohati   || [Vṛddhayājñavalkya.]
[41.15]     vaiṣṇavīṃ pratimāṃ loke vipraṃ vaikhānasaṃ tathā |
[41.16]     gaṅgāṃ viṣṇupadīṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||" iti  [Vṛddhayājñavalkya]

[viṣṇubhakteṣu vaikhānasasya śraiṣṭhyam]

[41.17]     śāntiparvaṇi:
[41.18]     mantriṇāñca sahasrebhyo brahmacārī viśiṣyate |
[41.19]     brahmacārisahasrebhyo nityayājī viśiṣyate || [ṃBh]
[41.20]     nityayājisahasrebhyassomayājī viśiṣyate |
[41.21]     somayājisahasrebhyo vedavedāntapāragaḥ || [ṃBh]
[41.22]     vedāntagasahasrebhyo viṣṇubhakto viśiṣyate |
[41.23]     viṣṇubhaktasahasrebhyo vipro vaikhānaso varaḥ ||" iti [ṃBh]





[puṣkaratīrthamāhātmyam]

[vaikhānasānāmekāntitvam]

[42.1]     brahmakaivarte: "nimnagānāṃ yathā gaṅgā devānāṃ bhagavān hariḥ |
[42.2]     varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaḥ āśramāṇāṃ yatiryathā   || [Brahmakaivarta.ড় ]
[42.3]     śrutīnāmādibhūtānāṃ śrutirekāyanī yathā |
[42.4]     tasyāṃ niṣadyātha yasyāṃ niṣaṇṇo bhagavān hariḥ ||  [Brahmakaivartaড় ]
[42.5]     vyūhānāmādibhūtastu vāsudevo yathā paraḥ |
[42.6]     trimūrtīnāṃ yathā viṣṇuḥ śivānān tu sadāśivaḥ   || [Brahmakaivarta.ড় ]
[42.7]     dharmāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavo dharmaḥ smṛtīnāṃ mānavī smṛtiḥ |
[42.8]     viprāṇāṃ vedaviduṣāṃ yathā vaikhānaso varaḥ   || [Brahmakaivarta.ড় ]
[42.9]     yathā munīnāṃ vikhanā ādibhūta udāhṛtaḥ |
[42.10]     sūtrāṇāṃ tatpraṇītan tu yathā śreṣṭhatamaṃ smṛtam   || [Brahmakaivarta.ড় ]
[42.11]     tathaiva puṣkaro rājaṃstīrthānāmuttamottamaḥ ||" [Brahmakaivartaড় ]
[42.12] kaurme:
[42.13]     "maccittā madgataprāṇā mayi saṅgatamānasāḥ |
[42.14]     ananyaśaraṇā rājaṃstasmādvaikhānasā varāḥ ||"  [Kūrmaড় ]

[vaikhānasādhiṣṭhatadeśe viṣṇusānnidhyam]

[42.15]     purāṇāntare:
[42.16]     "yatra vaikhānasā viprā yatra santi gavāṃ gaṇāḥ |
[42.17]     yatra bilvāḥ palāśāśca tatra sannihito hariḥ ||"
[42.18]     bṛhannāradīye:
[42.19]     "jagatpavitrāste nityaṃ viṣṇumabhyarcayanti ye |
[42.20]     durvṛttā vā suvṛttā vā pūjanīyā viśeṣataḥ ||" [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[42.21]     hārīte:
[42.22]     tasmātte śuddhasattvasthāśśuddhā vaikhānasāḥ smṛtāḥ |
[42.23]     yān lokān prāpnuvantyete tān śṛṇuṣva samāhitaḥ ||"
[42.24]     pāñcarātre pauṣkare:
[42.25]     "viprā vaikhānasākhyā ye te bhaktāstattvamucyate || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.260 d]
[42.26]     ekāntinassusattvasthā dehāntaṃ nānyayājinaḥ |





[43.1]     kartavyamiti deveśaṃ saṃyajante phalaṃ vinā || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.261]
[43.2]     prāpnuvanti ca dehānte vāsudevatvamabjaja ||" iti  [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.262 b]
[43.3]     gautamīye:
[43.4]     "vaikhānasavidhānajñastattvātmārthavyavasthitaḥ |
[43.5]     sūkṣmātsūkṣmamavāpnoti paraṃ brahmāṇamavyayam ||" iti

[puṣkare vaikhānasāśramaḥ]

[43.6]     āraṇyaparvaṇi: rameśaḥ:
[43.7]     "dhyātañca saindhavāraṇyaṃ munibṛndaniṣevitam | [ṃBh3.87.12 d]
[43.8]     pitāmahasaraḥ puṇyaṃ puṣkaraṃ nāma bhārata ||  [ṃBh3.87.12 f]
[43.9]     vaikhānasānāṃ siddhānāmṛṣīṇāmāśramaṃ priyam ||" iti  [ṃBh3.87.13]
[43.10]     anyatra:
[43.11]     "saindhavāraṇyamāsādya kulyāṃ tāṃ lokadarśanām |
[43.12]     puṣkareṣu mahārāja sarveṣu ca jalaṃ spṛśan ||" ityārabhya  [ṃBh3.125.12]
[43.13]     "etaccandrasaro nityamarpayantyṛṣayastathā |
[43.14]     vaikhānasaprabhṛtayo vālakhilyāstathaiva ca ||" iti [ṃBh3.125.14]

[vaikhānasānāṃ pūjanīyatvam]

[43.15]     anyatra: gālavaḥ:
[43.16]     "sarvān lokān prapaśyāmi prasādāttava suvrata |
[43.17]     vaikhānasānāṃ japatāmeṣa śabdo mahātmanām ||" iti [ṃBh3.114.15]
[43.18]     gāruḍe:
[43.19]     "kauṣītakīnāṃ hotṝṇāṃ grāmatrayamakalpayat |
[43.20]     akalpayacca trīn grāmān vaikhānasamahātmanām ||" iti [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[43.21]     parameśvare trayodaśādhyāye pavitraviniyogāvasare:
[43.22]     "pūjayedāptapūrvāṃśca yatipūrvāṃstapasvinaḥ || [ড়ārameśvaraṣ12.437 d]
[43.23]     pañcavaikhānasāṃścaiva viprādīṃścaturastathā |
[43.24]     pañcayogaratādyāṃstu tathānyān vaiṣṇavān dvijān || [ড়ārameśvaraṣ12.438]
[43.25]     ekāyanīyaśākhoktiśrotṝṇāṃ prathamaṃ tataḥ ||" iti  [ড়ārameśvaraṣ12.439 b]





[44.1]     nāradīye: "sāttvike tu sadādhikyaṃ pūjanaṃ mama nārada |
[44.2]     viddhi vaikhānase dattaṃ dānaṃ brahmavidastathā ||" [ṇāradaড়]

[vaikhānasānāṃ śrīrāmakṛtā pūjā]

[44.3]     vāsiṣṭharāmāyaṇe: "kṛtābhiṣekassantuṣṭassāmātyassasuhṛjjanaḥ |
[44.5]     pūjayāmāsa dharmātmā rāmo vaikhānasānmunīn ||" iti [Vāsiṣṭharāmāyaṇa]
[44.6]     vaikhānasānāṃ pañcakālaparāyaṇatvam

[pañcakālaparāyaṇasya lakṣaṇam]

[44.7]     śāṇḍilyasmṛtau: "ye pāvayanti dharaṇīṃ caraṇaiḥ pāñcakālikāḥ |
[44.8]     darśanātsparśanātteṣāṃ kṛtārthāssarvajantavaḥ   || [śāṇḍilyasmṛti.]
[44.9]     ijyāmadhye tathā home yoge ca japakarmaṇi |
[44.10]     āgataṃ pañcakālajñaṃ sampūjyairvācayetparam   || [śāṇḍilyasmṛti.]
[44.11]     ye toṣayantyavirataṃ pañcakālaparāyaṇān |
[44.12]     sakāmāstatphalaṃ yānti niṣkāmāḥ paramaṃ padam ||  [śāṇḍilyasmṛti]
[44.13]     sadāhitāgnayo ye ca śāntāśśūdrānnavarjitaḥ |
[44.14]     māmarcayanti madbhaktāstebhyo dattamihākṣayam   || [śāṇḍilyasmṛti.]
[44.15]     dvādaśākṣaratattvajñaścaturvyūhavibhāgavit |
[44.16]     acchidrapañcakālajñassa viprastārayiṣyati ||" [śāṇḍilyasmṛti]

[vaikhānasānāṃ dvādaśākṣaratattvajñatvam]

[44.17]     yathā:
[44.18]     dvādaśākṣaratattvajña iti. dvādaśākṣaratattvāni.
[44.19]     "cijjīvaḥ prakṛtirbuddhirmanassattvādayo guṇāḥ |
[44.20]     vyomāgnimarutaścāpo varṇānāṃ tattvamīritam ||" ityuktāni. [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 24.19]

[vaikhānasānāṃ caturvyūhavibhāgajñatvam]

[44.21]     caturvyūhavibhāgavititi

[vaikhānasapāñcarātrayoḥ vyūhacatuṣṭaye saṃjñāvaiṣamyam]

[44.22]     "puruṣañca tatassatyamacyutañca yudhiṣṭhira | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1659]
[44.23]     aniruddhañca māṃ prāhurvaikhānasavido janāḥ || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1660]
[44.24]     anye tvevaṃ vijānanti māṃ rājan pāñcarātrikāḥ |  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1661]
[44.25]     vāsudevañca rājendra saṅkarṣaṇamathāpi vā || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1662]




[45.1]     pradyumnañcāniruddhañca caturmūrtiṃ pracakṣate |  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1663]
[45.2]     etāścānyāśca rājendra saṃjñābhedena mūrtayaḥ || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1664]
[45.3]     viddhi me'rcāntarāṇyeva māmevañcārcayedbudhaḥ ||" [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1665]
[45.4]     ityādyuktānāṃ caturṇāṃ vyūhānāṃ vibhāgāvit.

[vāsudevasya daivikamānuṣabhedena dvaividhyam]

[45.5]     evaṃ vaikhānase daivikamānuṣabhedena vāsudevo dvividhaḥ pratipādyate. yathā -
[45.6-8]     khile: "athordhvaṃ vāsudevasya mānuṣasya vidhiṃ paraṃ". ityārabhya "mānuṣyavapuṣaṃ devaṃ dvibhujaṃ śaṅkhacakriṇam" ityādinā rukmiṇīpradyumnāniruddhasāmbabrahmagaruḍādiyutaṃ mānuṣavāsudevaṃ nirvarṇya punaḥ:
[45.9]     "daivikaṃ vāsudevaṃ taṃ pravakṣyāmyānupūrvyaśaḥ |
[45.10]     somacchandavimānaṃ vā bṛhadvṛttamathāpi vā ||
[45.11]     caturbhujadharaṃ devaṃ śaṅkhacakradharaṃ param |
[45.12]     abhayaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ vāmaṃ kaṭyavalambitam ||
[45.13]     kirīṭādisamastaiśca bhūṣaṇairapi bhūṣitam |
[45.14]     raktāmbaradharaṃ devaṃ daśātālena mānataḥ ||
[45.15]     ubhe devyāvubhe pārśve pūrvavatkārayedbudhaḥ |
[45.16]     balabhadrādipūrvoktaṃ sāyudhaṃ vā nirāyudham ||
[45.17]     tattaddevībishiertnochtelugu vergleichensamāyuktaṃ vinā vā samyagācaret |
[45.18]     revatī rohiṇī caiva ramācendukarīti ca ||
[45.19]     raktaṃ nīlaṃ tathā śyāmaṃ pītavarṇaṃ samācaret |
[45.20]     kirīṭamakuṭādyuktāssarvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ ||
[45.21]     evaṃ puṣpadharaṃ kuryādekañcaiva prasāritam |
[45.22]     devyā yuktaṃ vinā vāpi dvimārgaṃ samyagācaret ||"
[45.23-24]     ityārabhya "evaṃ daivikamārgan tu sthāpanārambhamācared" ityādinā daivikaḥ vāsudevaḥ pradarśitaḥ.





[vaikhānasetarasūtriṇāṃ pañcakālaparāyaṇatvābhavāḥ]

[46.1-5]     itthaṃ vāsudevādīnāṃ daivikamānuṣabhedena dvaividhyasya, "puruṣaṃ paramapuruṣaṃ paramātmānaṃ paraṃ brahmāṇam" ityādinā pratipāditānāṃ caturṇāṃ puruṣādīnāṃ mūrtivibhāgasya ca śrīvaikhānasaśāstre pratipādanāt, etassarvavibhāgajña eva caturvyūhavibhāgavidityanenocyate. ācchidrapañcakālajña iti:
[46.6]     "prātaḥkāle'bhigamanamupādānaṃ tataḥ param |
[46.7]     madhyāhna ijyāṃ kurvīta svādhyāyañcāparāhṇake |
[46.8]     yogaṃ sāyāhnakāle tu pañcakālā udāhṛtāḥ ||" [Vā ]
[46.9]     iti vāsādhikāre pratipāditapañcakālaparāyaṇamadhikṛtya prakṛttāni -
[46.10-14]     "ye pāvayanti dharaṇīṃ" "ijyāmadhye tathāhome" "ye toṣayantyavirataṃ" iti vacanāni, sāmanyena pañcakālaparāyaṇasya darśanasparśanādibhissarveṣāṃ pāvanatāṃ kṛtārthatāṃ, ijyāyogādisamayeṣu cāgatasya tasya pūjanāntaraṃ śeṣakarmācaraṇañca pratipādayanti, etādṛśaḥ pañcakālaparāyaṇaḥ kīdṛśā ityākaṅkṣāyāṃ "dvādaśākṣaratattvajña" ityādinā "acchidrapañcakālajña" ityantena tallakṣaṇamucyate.
[46.15-17]     īdṛśācchidrapañcakālaparāyaṇā vaikhānasā eva. vaikhānasaśāstra eva pañcakālavibhāgasya pratipāditatvāt. svasvasūtre'nuktatvādāpastambīyādayastu na. pañcakālapratipādanarahitasūtrāntaraniṣṭhānāṃ:
[46.18]     "brāhme muhūrte buddhyeta dharmārthāvanucintayan |" ityārabhya
[46.19]     "dvitīye ca tathā bhāge vedābhyāso vidhīyate ||
[46.20]     tṛtīye ca tathā bhāge poṣyavargārthacintanam |
[46.21]     bhāge caturthe kurvīta snānamambhasyakṛtrime ||
[46.22]     pañcame tu tathā bhāge saṃvibhāgo yathārhataḥ |
[46.23]     itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ ṣaṣṭaṃ saptamamabhyaset |
[46.24]     aṣṭame lokayātrāñca vicārya ca gṛhī tataḥ ||"





[47.1-2]     ityantena dakṣādismṛtyantareṣu brāhmaṇajāteḥ sāmānyena tattatkāleṣu kartavyatvena pratipāditakarmācaraṇa eva yathāyogyaṃ yogyatāsambhavāt.
[47.3]     anyathā
[47.4]     "vidharmaḥ paradharmaśca ābhāsa upa vā cchalaḥ |
[47.5]     adharmaśākhāḥ pañcemā dharmajño'dharmavattyajet ||
[47.6]     dharmabādho vidharmassyātparadharmo'nyacoditaḥ |
[47.7]     upadharmastu pāṣaṇḍo dambhovāṃśasurbhiśchalaḥ (?) |
[47.8]     yastvicchayā kṛtaṃ pumbhirābhāso hyāśramādbahiḥ ||".
[47.9]     iti bhāgavatavacanoktaparadharmāśrayaṇadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ.
[47.10]     "śreyān svadharmo viguṇaḥ paradharmātsvanuṣṭhitāt |
[47.11]     svadharme nidhanaṃ śreyaḥ paradharmo bhayāvahaḥ ||" [ṃBh6.25.35]
[47.12]     iti bhagavadvacanavirodhaśca.

[sūtrāntaranuṣṭhānāṃ pāñcarātrānuyāyināñca devalakatvam]

[47.13-19]     kiñca tatsūtreṣu dvādaśākṣaratattvādipratipādanamapi nāsti. evaṃ "sati kuḍye citrakarme"ti nyāyena yajñopavītadhāraṇasandhyopāsanabhagavadārādhanādividhipratipādanarahitasūtrāntaraniṣṭhānāṃ, tathā śrīmannārāyaṇasya samānatvena rudrādidevatāntarārādhanavidhipratipādakasūtraniṣṭhānāñca caturvyūhavibhāgajñānāsambhavātbhagavadārādhanasvarūpajñānābhāvena pañcakālaparāyaṇatvādigandhaleśo'pi na sambhavati. sūtrāntaroktamārgeṇa vā pañcarātroktamārgeṇa vā bhagavadārādhanaṃ kurvato'nadhikāriṇo devalakatvaṃ pratipādyate.

[pāñcarātre durgādidevatāntarārcanapratipādanam]

[47.20]     tathā hi - pañcarātre viṣvaksenasaṃhitāyāṃ nāradaṃ prati viṣvaksenoktiḥ:
[47.21]     "idan tu tava vakṣyāmi guhyādguhyatamaṃ mune |
[47.22]     yo'sau nārayaṇassākṣātsa hi vighneśa ucyate || [Viṣvaksenaṣ10.114]
[47.23]     yo vai vighneśa ityuktassa vai nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ   || [Viṣvaksena.ṣ10.115 b]
[47.24]     naiva bhedaṃ vijānanti munayastattvadarśinaḥ ||" ityārabhya  [Viṣvaksenaṣ10.116 b]




[48.1]     "kuryātpāśāṅkuśau vāpi śaṅkhacakrāvathāpi vā |
[48.2]     ākhuṃ vāpi khagendraṃ vā tasya vāhanamācaret || [Viṣvaksenaṣ]
[48.3]     nārāyaṇāśakatvāccāvyāvṛttatvānmahāmune |
[48.4]     vīśastadvāhanaṃ proktamākhuṃ vāpi samācaret  || [Viṣvaksena.ṣ]
[48.5]     tasmācchriyañca durgāñca vāṇīṃ vighneśameva ca |
[48.6]     svatantreṇārcayeddhīmān sarvakāryārthasiddhaye ||" ityādi ca dṛśyate. [Viṣvaksenaṣ]
[48.7-10]     devatāntarāṇāmaṅgabhāvenārcanaṃ na niṣiddhyate. "svatantrabuddhyā kurvan vai brāhmaṇo narakaṃ vrajet" ityādi vacanaiḥ aṅgatvena vinā svātantryeṇāṅgivyatiriktadevatāntarārādhanaṃ niṣiddhyate. tathāvidhaniṣiddhadevatāntarārcanapratipādakaśāstraniṣṭhasya pañcakālaparāyaṇatvaṃ na sambhavati.

[paramaikāntināmeva pañcakālaparāyaṇatvam]

[48.11]     "yairiṣṭaḥ pañcakālajñairharirekāntibhirnaraiḥ |
[48.12]     nūnaṃ tatra mato devo yathā tairvāgudīritaḥ ||" ityuktarītyā

[vaikhānasavyatiriktānāṃ paramaikāntitvābhāvaḥ]
