[48.13-15]     paramaikāntitvāsambhave tad(pañcakālaparāyaṇatvā)sambhavāt. paramaikāntitvañca bhagavadekaparatvameva. tacca svātantryeṇānyadevatāyājināṃ vaikhānasavyatiriktānāṃ hi na sambhavati.
[48.16-17]     atra "yairiṣṭaḥ pañcakālajñair" ityanena pañcakālavyatiriktakālāntarapratipādakaśāstraniṣṭhānāṃ vyavṛttiḥ. "ekāntibhirnarair" ityanena
[48.18]     "viprā vaikhānasākhyā ye te bhaktāstattvamucyate || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.260 d]
[48.19]     ekāntinassusattvasthā dehāntaṃ nānyayājinaḥ || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.261 b]
[48.20-23]     ityādi pauṣkarasaṃhitāvacanānusāreṇa svātantryeṇa durgādidevatāntarārcanapratipādakapāñcarātraśāstraniṣṭhānāṃ vyāvṛttiḥ. "nūnamatra mato deva" ityanena - "yatra vaikhānasā viprā yatra santi gavāṃ gaṇāḥ |
[48.24]     yatra bilvāḥ palāśāśca tatra sannihito hariḥ ||"
[48.25-26]     ityasyārtho jñāpyate iti "yairiṣṭa" iti kṛtsnamapi vacanaṃ vaikhānasaparamityevāvagamyate.




[pāñcarātriṇāmapi pañcakālaparāyaṇatvamityākṣepaḥ parihāraśca]

[49.1]     nanu:
[49.2]     brahmā: "pañcakālavidhijñānaprāśastyaṃ bhagavanniha |
[49.3]     kathitaṃ pañca ke kālā vidhayaścāpi pañca ke || [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.1]
[49.4]     mahyaṃ jijñāsamānāya kathayasva yathātatham |
[49.5]     na cedrahasyamatyantaṃ yadahaṃ pṛṣṭavāniha || [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.2]
[49.6]     śrībhagavān:

[pāñcarātroktamabhigamanam]

[49.7]     ādyaṃ karmābhigamanamupādānaṃ tataḥ param |
[49.8]     ijyā tapaśca svādhyāyastathā yogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.3]
[49.9]     pañcaite vidhayasteṣāṃ kālāḥ pañcaiva te kramāt |
[49.10]     kalyāṇācaraṇāntaṃ yatkarmajātaṃ caturmukha   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.4]
[49.11]     utthānādikramādetadabhiyānamudīritam |
[49.12]     brāhme muhūrte buddhyeta dhyāyannārāyaṇaṃ param   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.5]
[49.13]     utthāyāsīta śayane kīrtayannāma vaiṣṇavam |
[49.14]     krameṇa sṛṣṭvā tattvāni dehamutpādya tattvavit  || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.6]
[49.15]     svasya dehe ṣaḍaṅgāni mantramuccārya mānasam ||"  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.7]
[49.16]     ityādinā abhigamanamupakramya
[49.17]     "añjanālepanaissragbhiḥ vāsobhirbhūṣaṇaistathā   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.29 b]
[49.18]     alaktakarasādyaiśca tāmbūlairmukhaśodhanaiḥ |
[49.19]     upeto maṅgalairanyairnayedabhigamakriyām ||"  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.30]
[49.20]     ityabhigamanamuktā

[pāñcarātroktamupādānam]

[49.21]     "upādadyāttataḥ pūjā- sādhanāni yathātatham |
[49.22]     puṣpāṇi phalamūlāni vividhānyoṣadhīrapi   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.31]
[49.23]     dadhyādi ca haviryogyaṃ taṇḍulādi guḍādi ca |
[49.24]     snānīyāni ca vastrāṇi svādūni salilāni ca   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.32]





[50.1]     darbhān parṇāni samidho yathāśakti yathāvasu |
[50.2]     āhṛtya yājñiyān daivān pūjāsthāne niveśayet || [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.33]
[50.3]     nītvopādānasamayamitthaṃ tadanupūjayet |"
[50.4]     ityupādānamuktam.

[pāñcarātroktā ijyā]

[50.5]     "prāpte madhyāhnasamaye snānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi ||" [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.34]
[50.6]     ityārabhya
[50.7]     "uttīrya karma nikhilaṃ madhyāhnasamayocitam | [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.41 d]
[50.8]     kṛtvā yajeta deveśaṃ homāntaṃ kamalāsana   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.42 b]
[50.9]     ijyāyāssamayastasyāḥ kathitaḥ kamalāsana ||"  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.66 b]
[50.10]     iti bhagavadyajanamuktvā

[pāñcarātroktaḥ svādhyāyaḥ]

[50.11]     "mandire vā harernadyāstīre vāmarabhūmiṣu   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.66 d]
[50.12]     vivikte vipine vāpi parvate vā gṛhe'pi vā |
[50.13]     ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni vidyamānānyanekadhā || [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.67]
[50.14]     śākhābhedairmūlaśākhāmekāyanasamāhvayām |
[50.15]     trayīmayīmadhīyīta sukhāsīnassamāhitaḥ ||" [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.68]
[50.16]     svādhyāyamuktvā

[pāñcarātroktaḥ yogaḥ]

[50.17]     "tataḥ paścimasandhyāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ tatra coditam ||  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.72 d]
[50.18]     japahomādikaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtvā paramapūruṣam |
[50.19]     arcayitvā yathānyāyyaṃ yathāpūrvamaśeṣataḥ ||  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.73]
[50.20]     bhuktvānnaṃ viśya śayane samutthāya mahāniśi |
[50.21]     ācamya prayato bhūtvā dhyātvā paramapūruṣam ||  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.74]
[50.22]     yogāsane samāsīno yuñjītātmānamātmanā |
[50.23]     yathoktena prakāreṇa yathāśakti caturmukha   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.75]
[50.24]     sahārakramamāśritya tattvānyātmani saṃharet |
[50.25]     ātmānañcāpi hṛtpadme paramātmani niṣṭhite   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.76]





[51.1]     saṃhareditthato yogātsvāpaṃ kleśāpahaṃ vrajet |
[51.2]     ityeṣa kathito brahman yogaḥ kālaśca pañcamaḥ   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.77]
[51.3]     pañcaite samayāḥ pañcavidhayasteṣu darśitāḥ |
[51.4]     itthameteṣu kāleṣu darśitaiḥ karmabhirnaraḥ ||  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.78]
[51.5]     ārādhanaṃ bhagavataḥ kurvannaivāvasīdati |
[51.6]     varṣeṣu dvādaśasu evamanutiṣṭhan samāhitaḥ   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 13.79]
[51.7]     kriyākalāpamakhilaṃ yāti sāyujyasampadam |
[51.8]     ṣaṭsu varṣeṣu sārūpyaṃ sāmīpyaṃ triṣu niścitam || [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.80]
[51.9]     sālokyaṃ phalamekasmin vatsare nātra saṃśayaḥ ||" [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 13.81 b]
[51.10-11]     iti pādme caryāpāde trayodaśe'dhyāye avicchinnatayā pañcakālānāṃ pratipāditatvātteṣāmapyacchidrapañcakālaparāyaṇatvaṃ sambhavatīti cet- tadasat.

[pāñcarātriṇāmaniyataparimitacchidradrapañcakālaparāyaṇatvam]

[51.12]     tatraiva:
[51.13]     "pūtyūṣamardharātrañca prātarmadhyaṃdināni ca |
[51.14]     pañcakālā ime proktāstataḥ kālacatuṣṭaye ||
[51.15]     eteṣu hīnāḥ pratyūṣaṃ (?) vijñeyāścaturānana ||"
[51.16-20]     ityabhigamanādīnāmekarūpeṇa vinā nyūnātiriktakālatvenoktatvāt"kālaṃ madhyandinacchidrapañcakālaparāyaṇair" ityāgamasiddhānte "sāntarālamanuṣṭhānaṃ pañcakāloditaṃ tathā" iti tantrasiddhānte ca chidrapañcakālaparatvasyoktatvāt, kālacatuṣṭayapratipādanena niyatapañcakālaparāyaṇatvābhāvāt, dvādaśavarṣamārabhya ekavarṣaparyantaṃ parimitapañcakālaparatvasyoktatvātdvādaśavarṣādūrdhvaṃ pañcakālaparatvābhāvācca.
[51.21]     tathā, mārkaṇḍeye
[51.22]     "mahāniśā tu vijñeyā rātrermadhyaṃ dvitīyayoḥ |
[51.23]     tasyāṃ snānaṃ na kurvīta samyagācamanaṃ tathā ||"  [ṃrkaṇḍeyaড়]





[52.1]     ityācamanasya mahāniśāyāṃ niṣedhāt.
[52.2]     "yāmadvayaṃ śayānastu brahmabhūyāya kalpate ||"
[52.3-4]     iti dharmaśāstroktanidrākāle yogasyāyuktatvācca. yogakālasyārdharātrikatve svādhyāyakālādvyavahitatvena chidrapāñcakālikatvāpātācca.
[52.5-6]     kālacatuṣtvāsyaivoktatvena abhigamanādiṣvekasya lopasambhavena aniyatapañcakālaparasya pañcakālaparāyaṇaśabdābhilāpāsambhavācca.
[52.7]     tathā:
[52.8]     "kliśyantīha suṣuptānāmindriyāṇi dravanti ca |
[52.9]     aṅgāni samatāṃ yānti uttamānyadhamaissaha ||"
[52.10]     iti dharmaśāstreṣu prātarārabhya sāyaṅkālaparyantameva karmācaraṇakālatvena pratipādanāt.
[52.11]     tathā:
[52.12]     "atyantamalinaḥ kāyo navacchidrasamanvitaḥ |
[52.13]     sravatyeva divārātraṃ prātassnānaṃ viśodhanam ||
[52.14]     asnātvā nācaretkarma japahomādi kiñcana |
[52.15]     snātvādhikārī bhavati daive pitrye ca karmaṇi ||"
[52.16-20]     iti smṛteḥ snānātpūrvaṃ tattvasṛṣṭerayuktatvācca. pretaprāyasya saṃhṛtatattvasya svāpayogyatāsambhavācca. tasmāt"abbhakṣo vāyubhakṣa" ityādiśabdavadavadhāraṇagarbhasya pāñcakālikaśabdasya aniyataparimitapāñcakālikeṣu pāñcarātrikeṣu prayogāsaṃbhavādaniyataparimitachidrapāñcakālikāḥ pāñcarātriṇo na niyatāchidrapañcakālaparāyaṇavaikhānasatulyā bhavitumahantīti siddham.

[devatāpūjakasāmānasya devalakatvamityākṣepaparihāraḥ]

[52.21]     nanu:
[52.22]     "yo devaṃ pūjayedvipro vittārthī vatsaratrayam |
[52.23]     sa vai devalako nāma havyakavyabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||"
[52.24-53.2]      iti devatāpūjakasya devalakatvaṃ sambhavatīti cet- tadasat. sāmānyataḥ devatārādhanaṃ ye kurvanti te devalakā iti vā? uta śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇādiṣu mukhyādhikāritvena pratipāditā apīti vā?. sāmānyataḥ devatārādhanaṃ ye kurvanti te iti cet- tarhi gṛhadevatāpūjakānāmapi devalakatvaprasaṅgaḥ. kiñca:


[vaikhānasānāṃ devalakatvābhāvaḥ]

[53.3]     "brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vipro vaikhānasastathā |
[53.4]     kurvanti nijakarmāṇi viṣṇumeva yajanti te ||"
[53.5-11]     iti sandhyopāsanādīnāṃ bhagavadārādhanarūpatvoktyā teṣāñca tyāgassambhavati. atassāmānyataḥ iti vaktumayuktam. śrutismṛtipurāṇādiṣu mukhyādhikāritvena pratipāditā devalakāḥ iti cet- tadasat. "'agnirvai devānāmavamo viṣṇuḥ paramastadantareṇa sarvā anyā devatā' iti brāhmaṇam. tasmādgṛhe paramaṃ viṣṇuṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sāyaṃ prātarhomānte'rcayati" iti vaikhānasasūtre, "pravaḥ pāntamandhaso dhiyāyate maheśūrāya viṣṇave cārcata" iti ṛgbrāhmaṇamanusṛtya viṣṇupradhānatayā "sa vā eṣa puruṣaḥ pañcadhā pañcātmā" ityādiśrutyuktaviṣṇvādipañcamūrtīnāmārādhanasya pratipāditatvāt.
[53.12-13]     āśvamedhike "kathaṃ tvamarcanīyo'sī"tyādeḥ, vṛddhamanau "vedāntavedibhir" ityādeścoktatvāt.
[53.14-15]     evameva pāñcarātraśaivaśilpajyautiṣādiṣu utkṛṣṭamukhyādhikāripratipādakānāṃ vacanānāṃ vaiyarthyaprasaṅgāttanmūlabhūtaśrutismṛtipurāṇādīnāmaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgo duṣpariharaḥ.

[vaikhānasānāṃ bhagavaddravyopabhogādau doṣābhāvaḥ]

[53.16-17]     evaṃ ca pramāṇabhūteṣu śrutismṛtipurāṇādiṣu pratipāditānāmācārādīnāṃ tyāgaḥ prasajyet, niṣphalatvāt. nanu paradravyoṇālayārcanaṃ kurvatāṃ devalakatvamiti cetna.
[53.18-19]     śrutismṛtipurāṇādiṣu tattacchāstreṣu ca ālayārcakānāṃ mukhyādhikāritvena taddravyopabhogārhatvena ca pratipādanāt.
[53.20]     yathā - marīciproktāyāmānandasaṃhitāyāṃ vikhanasotpattyādikaṃ pratipādya:
[53.21]     "tathā tvadanujīvitve tvaddravyānubhave sati |
[53.22]     yadbhaveddoṣa utpanna iti cintākulo'smyaham ||"  [āṣ4.77]
[53.23]     iti bhagavaddravyopajīvanadoṣabhīroḥ vikhanasaḥ bhagavantaṃ prati praśne -
[53.24]     "athovāca hṛṣīkeśo bhīruṃ taṃ munisattamam | [āṣ4.78 b]
[53.25]     vaikhānasānāṃ taddoṣo nāsti matkṛtakarmaṇām || [āṣ4.83 b]





[54.1]     maddravyaṃ havyakavyārthe dānābhyāgatapūjane |  [āṣ4.84 d]
[54.2]     mukhyamevāstu bhavatāṃ sadā matkarma kurvatām   || [ā.ṣ4.85 b]

[vaikhānasānāṃ bhagavadvigrahavikraye'pi doṣābhāvaḥ]

[54.3]     kimebhirbahubhirvākyairanyatte kathayāmyaham || [āṣ4.85 d]
[54.4]     maddravyakṣetrabimbānāmālayānāṃ tathāpadi |
[54.5]     vikrīṇanādikāryeṣu nāsti doṣo madājñayā || [āṣ4.87]
[54.6]     tasmādbhavānabhimatassarvaṃ karma sadā kuru ||  [āṣ]
[54.7]     madīyadhanabhogena matkriyālopatastava |
[54.8]     maccharīratayā satyamapacāro na vidyate ||"  [āṣ4.86]
[54.9]     iti bhagavadvacanodāharaṇena bhagavaddravyopabhogādiṣu doṣābhāvaḥ pratipāditaḥ.
[54.10-14]     atra "ahaṃ bhaktaparādhīno hyasvatantra iva dvija" ityādivacanānusāreṇārcakaparādhīnatāṃ darśayituṃ, bhagavatprītiviṣayīkṛtānāṃ vaikhānasānāṃ taddravyopabhogādiṣu doṣagandho nāstīti pratipādanāvasare "maddravyaṃ kṣetrabimbādī"tyādinā kaimutikanyāyamabhipretya bhagavadvigrahavikraye kṛte'pi doṣo nāstītyucyate. tāvatā na tadabhipretam.
[54.15-16]     nanvevamapi devadravyopabhogadoṣassāmānyataḥ mukhyāmukhyādhikāriṣu sarveṣvāpadyate eva iti cetna.

[māṃsabhakṣane narakapatanam]

[54.17]     "ye bhakṣayanti māṃsāni sattvānāṃ jīvitaiṣiṇām |
[54.18]     bhakṣyante te'pi taissarvairiti brahmābravītsvayam || [ṃBh13.117.33]
[54.19]     māṃ sa bhakṣayitāmutra yasya māṃsamihādmyaham |
[54.20]     etanmāṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ || [ṃBh13.117.34]
[54.21]     na hi māṃsaṃ tṛṇātkaṣṭhādupalādvāpi jayate |
[54.22]     hatvā jantuṃ bhavenmāṃsaṃ tasmāttatparivarjayet || [ṃBh13.116.26]
[54.23]     hantā caivānumantā ca viśastā krayavikrayī |
[54.24]     saṃskartā copakartā ca khādakaścāṣṭa ghātukāḥ || [ṃBh13.116.47]





[55.1]     dhanena krāyako hanti khādakaścopabhogataḥ |
[55.2]     ghātuko vadhabandhābhyāmityeṣa trividho vadhaḥ || [ṃBh13.116.38]
[55.3]     svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitumicchati |
[55.4]     nāradaḥ prāha dharmātmā narake sa vipacyate ||" [ṃBh13.117.10]

[kutraciddharmasyāpyadharmaṃ, adharmasyāpi dharmatvañca]

[55.5-6]     ityādi vacanaiḥ māṃsabhakṣaṇena narakapatanasmaraṇātbhagavatprītyarthaṃ kriyamāṇeṣu yajñeṣvapi māṃsabhakṣaṇasattvātyājñikānāmapi narakapatanaṃ sambhavatīti yāgasyāpi tyāgaprasaṅgāt.
[55.7]     "tasmācchāstraṃ pramāṇaṃ te kāryākāryavyavasthitau |
[55.8]     jñātvā śāstravidhānoktaṃ karma kartumihārhasi ||" [ṃBh6.38.24]
[55.9-12]     iti bhagavadvacanātśāstroktānāṃ karmaṇāṃ doṣo nāstīti cet. "maddravyopabhogādau doṣo nāstī"ti bhagavatā pratipāditvātprakṛte'pi doṣo nāstyeva. "vacanātpravṛttirvacanānnivṛttir" iti khalu śiṣṭoktiḥ. evaṃ kutraciddharmo'dharmaḥ adharmaśca dharmo bhavati.
[55.13]     yathā
[55.14]     "ukte'nṛte bhavetkiñcitprāṇināṃ prāṇarakṣaṇam |
[55.15]     anṛtaṃ tatra satyaṃ syātsatyamatrānṛtaṃ bhavet ||
[55.16]     kāminīṣu vivāheṣu gavāṃ pīḍāsu bādhane |
[55.17]     brāhmaṇābhyavapattau ca śapathe nāsti pātakam ||"
[55.18-19]     "yadbrāhmaṇaścābrāhmaṇaśca praśnameyātāṃ brāhmaṇāyādhibrūyātyadbrāhmaṇāyādhyātmane'dhyāha yadbrāhmaṇaṃ parāhātmānaṃ parāha tasmādbrāhmaṇo na parocyaḥ" ityśrutiśca.
[55.20-21]     śrautasūtre ca: "sarva evamabhivādayanti nābhivādayati na juhotyanyatra somāṅgebhyaḥ" ityādi pratipādyate.
[55.22]     "sarvasvaharaṇe bhāryāveśyayoḥ (?) prāṇasaṃśaye |
[55.23]     godvijārthe vivāde ca śapathe nāsti pātakam ||"
[55.24]     ityādi smṛtiśca.
[55.25]     evaṃ ca śāstroktaprakāreṇa bhagavadarcanaṃ kurvatāṃ taddravyopabhoge na doṣaḥ.





[devalakatvalakṣaṇavicāraḥ]

[56.1-2]     kiñca - kiṃ devalakatvaṃ jātiparam, uta karmaparam? jātiparamiti vaktuṃ na śakyate. tathānuktatvāt. icchayā kalpitumaśakyatvācca.

[avihitakarmakartṛtvameva devalakatvam]

[56.3-4]     atha karmaparamiti cet. vihitakarmaparam, uta avihitakarmaparam? vihitakarmaparamiti vaktumaśakyam - śrutismṛtītihāsasūtrapurāṇādīnāmapramāṇyaprasaṅgāt.
[56.5]     vihitadharmaparatvenānuktatvāt.
[56.6]     "śrutismṛtī mamaivājñā yastāmullaṅghya vartate |
[56.7]     ājñācchedī mama drohī madbhakto'pi na vaiṣṇavaḥ ||"

[paradravyakṛtasyāpyārādhanasya nirdoṣatvam]

[56.8]     iti bhagavadājñābhaṅgadoṣācca. evaṃ ca avihitakarmaparamiti hi pariśiṣyate.
[56.9-10]     nanu - paradravyeṇa kṛtaṃ bhagavadārādhanaṃ kratuvatparārthameva bhavatīti cetmaivam.
[56.11]     "anyadharmaparo vipro bhraṣṭo gacchatyadhogatim |
[56.12]     matkarmanirato vipro nādhaḥ patati kutracit ||"
[56.13]     iti bhagavadvacanāt.
[56.14]     purāṇāntare: "svasminnarpitamātreṇa yena kenāpi karmaṇā |
[56.15]     tuṣṭo dadāti svapadamaho vatsalatā hareḥ ||" iti.
[56.16]     kiñca:

[dakṣiṇāpratigrahe doṣābhavaḥ]

[56.17]     "ekaviṃśatiniṣkantu dadyādācāryadakṣiṇām |
[56.18]     ṛtvijān tu tadardhaṃ syāttadardhamitarasya ca ||"
[56.19]     iti dakṣiṇādānaṃ pratipādyate.
[56.20]     nanu - "pratigraheṇa viprasya brāhmaṃ tejo vinaśyati ||" iti manuvacanāt [ṃnuḍhś4.186 d]
[56.21-25]     "pratigraheṇa tejo hi viprāṇāṃ śāmyate" ityānuśāsanikavacanācca pratigraheṇa brahmatejonāśanaṃ smaryate. śrūyate pratigrahe prāyaścittamapi. "yo yājayati prati vā gṛhṇāti yājayitvā pratigṛhya vānaśnaṃstrissvādhyāyamadhīyīta trirātraṃ sāvitrīṃ gāyatrīmanvatirecayati varo dakṣiṇā vareṇaiva varaṃ spṛṇotyātmā hi varaḥ" iti.
[56.25]     ataḥ pratigrahe doṣo'stīti cet- na.




[harismaraṇasya mokṣapradatvam]

[57.1]     "trāyante khalu nātyantamanyadharmāssvanuṣṭitāḥ |
[57.2]     api matkarma viguṇaṃ trāyate mahato bhayāt ||
[57.3]     merumandaramātro'pi rāśiḥ pāpasya karmaṇaḥ |
[57.4]     keśavaṃ vaidyamāsādya abhiernochṭelugu vergleichendurvyādhiriva naśyati ||
[57.5]     nidhisthānaṃ khanan dveṣātmṛdarthaṃ vāpnuyānnidhim |
[57.6]     ajñaḥ kāmācca doṣācca smṛtvaivaṃ mokṣabhāktathā ||
[57.7]     dveṣiṇā vāprayatnena kṣipto'gniḥ kakṣamādahet |
[57.8]     kathamabhyarcito viṣṇuḥ na dahetsarvakilbiṣam ||
[57.9]     yathājño vajrakāyassyātspardhannapi sudhāṃ piban |
[57.10]     evaṃ viruddhabhāvo'pi mucyatyeva hariṃ smaran ||" iti
[57.11]     nāradīye: "kṛtāpi dambhahāsyādyairyatsevā tārayejjanān |
[57.12]     viphalā nānyakarmeva kṛpāluḥ ko'nvataḥ param   || [ṇāradaড়.]
[57.13]     hāsyānādaramāyābhirapi bhaktiḥ kṛtā tvayi |
[57.14]     nṛṇāṃ dadātīndrapadaṃ sāttvikānāṃ kimucyate ||  [ṇāradaড়]
[57.15]     vastusvabhāva evaiṣa yanmokṣāya harismṛtiḥ |
[57.16]     pūṣeva dhyāntanāśāya śśītaśāntyai yathānalaḥ   || [ṇāradaড়.]
[57.17]     saiṣā harismṛtiṛ daityaṃ krodhādapi kṛtā satī |
[57.18]     ānayetsadgatiṃ vipraṃ sānugaṃ kimu varṇyate   || [ṇāradaড়.]
[57.19]     yathāmṛtārthaṃ yatatāṃ surāṇāmabdhimanthane |
[57.20]     pārijātādikānyāsan phalānyaprārthitānyapi   || [ṇāradaড়.]
[57.21]     evaṃ mokṣaikacittānāṃ yatatāmīśasaṃsmṛtau |
[57.22]     bhavanti siddhayo divyāḥ puṇya puṇyānubandhi yat ||" ityādi [ṇāradaড়]
[57.23]     tatraiva: "surātithyarcanakṛte gurubhṛtyarthameva ca |
[57.24]     sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyātna tu duṣyetsvayaṃ tataḥ ||" [ṇāradaড়]





[58.1-2]     iti ca smaraṇāt. ataḥ śāstroktamārgeṇa bhagavadyajanavidhau pravṛttānāṃ tattatkarmasu dakṣiṇāpratigrahe kṛte'pi na doṣaḥ.

[ālayārcanāyāḥ mahābhāratādisaṃmatatvam]

[58.3]     gṛhārcanāvyatirekeṇālayārcanā bhāratādiṣu pratipāditā dṛśyate. yathā -
[58.4]     mahābhārate āśvamedhike "kathaṃ tvamarcanīyo'sī"tyādi. [ṃBh14 āpp. 4.1652 r]
[58.5]     viṣṇudharmottare ṣaṣṭhe'dhyāye:
[58.6]     "yastu devagṛhasyārthaṃ prākāraṃ parikalpayet |
[58.7]     sa tu svargaṃ samāsadya vimānādhipatirbhavet  || [Viṣṇudharmottara.ড় ]
[58.8]     yastu devālayasyāgre śilādārubhireva vā |
[58.9]     karoti toraṇaṃ ramyaṃ sa tu svargādhipo bhavet  || [Viṣṇudharmottara.ড় ]
[58.10]     yastu devālayaṃ viṣṇordāruśailamṛdā tathā |
[58.11]     iṣṭakākalpitaṃ vāpi kuryāttasya phalaṃ bahu   || [Viṣṇudharmottara.ড় ]
[58.12]     ahanyahanyaśvamedha- sahasrāṇi karoti yaḥ |
[58.13]     prāpnoti maṇḍapaṃ viṣṇoḥ yaḥ kārayati mānavaḥ   || [Viṣṇudharmottara.ড় ]
[58.14]     keśavasya sa tallokamakṣayyaṃ pratipadyate |
[58.15]     pratimārthaṃ mahāviṣṇossādhayeduttamāṃ śilām ||  [Viṣṇudharmottaraড় ]
[58.16]     ṣaṣṭirvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||" iti. [Viṣṇudharmottaraড় ]
[58.17-19]     eteṣāṃ ca prākāratoraṇamaṇḍapavigrahaśilādīṇāṃ gṛhadevatāsvasaṃbhavaḥ śrīvaikhānasasaṃhitāsu pratipāditaḥ. prasaṅgādarcakamahimādīha kathyate.

[arcakamāhātmyam]

[58.20]     bṛhannāradīye: "sa kadācinmahāpāpo jantūnāmantakopamaḥ ||" [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[58.21]     ityārabhya
[58.22]     "tasyopavanamadhyasthaṃ ramyaṃ keśavamandiram |
[58.23]     chāditaṃ hemakavacaiḥ dṛṣṭvā vyādho mudā yayau ||  [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[58.24]     harāmyatra suvarṇāni bahūnīti sa niścitaḥ |
[58.25]     jagāma viṣṇubhavanaṃ kīnāśaścauryalolupaḥ || [Bṛhannāradaড়]


------

------



[59.1]     tatrāpaśyaddvijavaraṃ śāntaṃ tattvārthakovidam |
[59.2]     paricaryāparaṃ viṣṇorudaṅkaṃ tapasāṃ nidhim ||  [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[59.3]     ekāntinaṃ dayāluñca nisspṛhaṃ dhyānalolupam |
[59.4]     dṛṣṭvā taṃ lubdhako mene svakāryasyāntarāyinam || [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[59.5]     devasya dravyajātan tu samādātumanā niśi ||"  [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[59.6-7]     ityādināpṛthagālayārcakasadbhāvādikamuktvā svakāryāntarāya bhūtārcakahiṃsāyāṃ pravṛttasya tasya maraṇamapi pratipādya.

[viṣṇupādodakamāhātmyam]

[59.8]     "udaṅkaḥ patitaṃ prekṣya lubdhakantu dayāparaḥ |
[59.9]     viṣṇupādodakenainamabhyaṣiñcanmahāmuniḥ ||
[59.10]     haripādodakasparśāllubdhako vītakalmaṣaḥ |
[59.11]     divyaṃ vimānamāruhya munimenamathābravīt ||"
[59.12] iti bhagavatpādodakamāhātmyaṃ ca pratipāditam.
[59.13] tatraiva - yajñadhvajavītihotrasaṃvāde:

[ālaye dīpasthāpanādeḥ phalam]

[59.14]     "sa kadācittu kāmāndho rantukāmaḥ parastriyam |
[59.15]     śūnyaṃ pūjādibhirviṣṇormandiraṃ prāptavānniśi ||
[59.16]     tatra kāmopabhogārthaṃ śayituṃ tena kāminā |
[59.17]     svavastraprāntato brahman kṛtaṃ taddeśamārjanam ||
[59.18]     yāvantaḥ pāṃsukaṇikāḥ tena sammārjitāstadā |
[59.19]     tāvajjanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tadaiva kṣayamāgatam ||
[59.20]     pradīpaḥ sthāpitastatra suratārthaṃ dvijottama |
[59.21]     tenāpi tasya duṣkarma niśśeṣaṃ kṣayamāgatam ||
[59.22]     avaśenāpi yatkarma kṛtvemāṃ siddhimāgataḥ |
[59.23]     bhaktimadbhiḥ praśāntaiśca kiṃ punassamyagarcanāt ||



[ālayapradakṣiṇaphalam]

[60.1]     tasmācchruṇuta viprendrāḥ devo nārāyaṇo'vyayaḥ |
[60.2]     jñānato'jñānato vāpi pūjakānāṃ vimuktidaḥ ||"
[60.3]     tathā tatraiva - indrasudharmasaṃvāde:
[60.4]     "ahamāsaṃ purā śakra gṛdhraḥ pāpāvaśeṣataḥ |
[60.5]     sthitaśca bhūmibhāge vai amedhyāmiṣabhojanaḥ ||
[60.6]     ekadāhaṃ viṣṇugṛhe prākāre saṃsthitaḥ prabhoḥ |
[60.7]     patito vyādhaśastreṇa sāyaṃ viṣṇugṛhaṃ gate ||
[60.8]     mayi kaṇṭhagataprāṇe kaścitśvā māṃsalolupaḥ |
[60.9]     jagraha māṃ svavaktreṇa śvabhiranyairabhidrutaḥ ||
[60.10]     gataḥ pradakṣiṇākāraṃ viṣṇostanmandiraṃ prabhoḥ |
[60.11]     tenaiva tuṣṭimāpannaḥ antarātmā jaganmayaḥ ||
[60.12]     mama cāpi śunaścāpi dattavān paramaṃ padam |
[60.13]     pradakṣiṇākāratabishiernochṭelugu vergleichenyā gatasya sadṛśaṃ phalam ||
[60.14]     samprāptaṃ tu mayā śakra kiṃ punassamyagarcanāt |
[60.15]     ye'rcayanti sadā bhaktyā nārāyaṇamanāmayam |
[60.16]     tānarcayanti vibudhā brahmādyā devatāgaṇāḥ ||" iti

[amedhyāni pañca]

[60.17]     evamāśvamedhike: "loke trīṇyapavitrāṇi pañcāmedhyāni bhārata |  [ṃBh13, āpp. 4.3249]
[60.18]     śvā ca śūdraśśvapākaśca tryapavitrāṇi pāṇḍava | [ṃBh13, āpp. 4.3250]
[60.19]     devalaḥ kukkuṭo yūpaḥ udakyā vṛṣalīpatiḥ ||" [ṃBh13, āpp. 4.3251]
[60.20]     pañcamedhyāni iti.

[bhagavatpūjādyanadhikāriṇaḥ]

[60.21]     śrīvaikhānase: "sūtādipratilomāśca pāṣaṇḍāḥ patitāstathā |
[60.22]     nāstikā bhinnamaryādāstathā vedavidūṣakāḥ ||
[60.23]     pāparogayutāścaiva tathaiva gurunindakā |
[60.24]     arcanaṃ devadevasya havirdānañca vīkṣitum |





[61.1]     nārhanti tasmātkurvīta dvāraṃ yavanikāvṛtam ||"
[61.2-3]     iti pratipādanātbhagavadarcanadarśanādiṣu yogyatārahitānāṃ nikṛṣṭānāṃ (devalakānāṃ) autkṛṣṭyaṃ na sambhavati.

[advārakabhagavadyājināmutkṛṣṭatvam]

[61.4-6]     śrutismṛtipurāṇādiṣūktaprakāreṇa kalpasūtre yeṣāmadvārakabhagavadyajanādhikāro vidhīyate te utkṛṣṭā jñeyāḥ. yathā śrutiḥ: "yathā kraturasmin loke bhavati tathetaḥ pretya bhavati" iti.
[61.7]     smṛtiśca: "vedoktenaiva bhārgeṇa sarvabhūtahṛdi sthitam |
[61.8]     māmarcayanti ye bhaktāssāyujyaṃ yānti te mama ||
[61.9]     tasmādyāvajjano jīvettāvatsampūjayeddharim |
[61.10]     pāpairna lipyate martyo harimandiramāpnuyāt ||
[61.11]     jagatpavitrāste nityaṃ viṣṇumabhyarcayanti ye |
[61.12]     durvṛttā vā suvṛttā vā pūjanīyā viśeṣataḥ ||"

[vaikhānasānāṃ bhagavatprītipātratvam]

[61.13-16]     vyāsenapi ānuśāsanike (90. adhyāye) pātrakathanāvasare devalakānāmapātratāṃ satkarmaniṣṭhānāṃ pātratāñca pratipādya svavacanadṛḍhīkaraṇārthaṃ "vaikhānasā vā ṛṣaya indrasya priyā āsan" iti bhagavatprītiviṣayāṇāṃ vaikhānasānāṃ vacanamapi śrūyate iti pratipāditam. yathā:
[61.17]     "ṛṣīṇāṃ samayaṃ nityaṃ ye rakṣanti yudhiṣṭhira |
[61.18]     niścitāḥ sarvadharmajñāstān devā brāhmaṇān viduḥ   || [ṃB.h13.9.42]
[61.19]     svādhyāyaniṣṭhā ṛṣayo jñānaniṣṭhāstathaiva ca |
[61.20]     taponiṣṭhāśca boddhavyāḥ karmaniṣṭhāśca bhārata ||  [ṃBh13.9.43]
[61.21]     kavyāni jñānaniṣṭhebhyaḥ pratiṣṭhāpyāni bhārata |
[61.22]     tatra ye brāhmaṇān ke cinna ca nindanti te varāḥ || [ṃBh13.9.44]
[61.23]     ye tu nindanti jalpeṣu na tāñśrāddheṣu bhojayet |
[61.24]     brāhmaṇā ninditā rājan hanyustripuruṣaṃ kulam || [ṃBh13.9.45]





[62.1]     vaikhānasānāṃ vacanamṛṣīṇāṃ śrūyate nṛpa |
[62.2]     dūrādeva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇān vedapāragān |
[62.3]     priyo vā yadi vā dveṣyaḥ teṣu tacchrāddhamāvapet || [ṃBh13.9.46]
[62.4]     yaḥ sahasraṃ sahasrāṇāṃ bhojayedanṛcāṃ naraḥ |
[62.5]     ekastānmantravitpūtaḥ sarvānarhati bhārata ||" [ṃBh13.9.47]

[aṣṭākṣarādimantrajapaniṣṭhānāṃ mahimā]

[62.6-7]     evamaṣṭākṣara dvādaśākṣara puruṣasūktādimantrajapaniṣṭhānāṃ darśanena brahmahā'pi śuddhyatīti purāṇoktam. yathā:
[62.8]     padmapurāṇe: "sāṅgaṃ samantraṃ sanyāsaṃ sarṣicchandodhidaivatam |
[62.9]     sadīkṣāvidhi sadhyānaṃ sayantraṃ dvādaśākṣaram   || [ড়dma.ড় ]
[62.10]     sāṣṭākṣarañca mantreśaṃ ye japanti narottamāḥ |
[62.11]     tān dṛṣṭvā brahmahā śuddhyetkiṃ punarvaiṣṇavassvayam ||" iti  [ড়dmaড় ]
[62.12-14]     śruyate'pi. "yo ha vai nārāyaṇasyāṣṭākṣaraṃ padamadhyeti anupabravassarvamāyureti vindate prājāpatyaṃ rāyaspoṣaṃ gaupatyaṃ tato'mṛtatvamaśnute tato'mṛtatvamaśnuta iti".
[62.15]     śrīpāñcarātre kapiñjalasaṃhitāyām:
[62.16]     "yājanādhyāpanayutaḥ śiṣyānugrahakārakaḥ |
[62.17]     ācārya iti vijñeyo mantratantraparāyaṇaḥ || [Kapiñjalaṣ]
[62.18]     āśrame vā vane vā'pi sa vaikhānasa ucyate |
[62.19]     grāmyadharmāṇi sarvāṇi varjayedvanavāsakaḥ ||" iti. [Kapiñjalaṣ]
[62.20]     yamasmṛtau: "yo'nucānaṃ dvijaṃ martyo hatavānarthalobhataḥ |
[62.21]     sa vadetpauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ jalasthaścintayeddharim ||"
[62.22-24]     iti kevalāṣṭākṣaramantrajapaniṣṭhānāmapyevaṃ vidhāḥ prabhāvāḥ śrūyanta iti cetśrutismṛtivihitaistaireva mantraiḥ advārakabhagavadarcane mukhyādikāriṇāṃ vaikhānasānāṃ kaimutikanyāyasiddhamautkṛṣṭyaṃ kenāpodituṃ śakyam.





[devalakanirūpaṇam]
[63.1-3]     kiñca - yathā vaikhānasānāmutpattyādikaṃ śrutismṛtipurāṇādiṣu viśeṣeṇa pratipāditaṃ tathā itareṣāṃ viśeṣeṇa pratipāditañcettadā vaikhānasasāmyamitareṣāṃ syāt. tathā na dṛśyate hi.
[63.4]     atha vastuto devalakā nirūpyante.
[63.5]     vaikhānase: "avaikhānasavipro yaḥ pūjayedālaye harim |
[63.6]     sa vai devalako nāma havyakavyabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||
[63.7]     vaikhānasakule jātaḥ pāñcarātreṇa dīkṣitaḥ |
[63.8]     na dīkṣito na sañjāto (?) lobhānmohānmadarcanam |
[63.9]     kuryāccettu viśeṣeṇa bhaveddevalakasto saḥ ||"

[devalakatraividyam]

[63.10]     pāñcarātre saṃkarṣaṇasaṃhitāyām: "karmadevalakāḥ kecitkalpadevalakāḥ pare |
[63.11]     śuddhadevalakāścānye tridhā devalakāḥ smṛtāḥ   || [ṣaṅkarṣaṇasaṃhitā.]

[karmadevalakalakṣaṇam]

[63.12]     arthārthī kālanirdeśī yo devaṃ pūjayetsa hi |
[63.13]     karmadevalako nāma sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ   || [ṣaṅkarṣaṇasaṃhitā.]
[63.14]     pāñcarātravidhānajño dīkṣāvirahito'rcakaḥ |
[63.15]     caturvedādhikāro'pi kalpadevalakaḥ smṛtaḥ   || [ṣaṅkarṣaṇasaṃhitā.]
[63.16]     āgamoktavidhānajño bhadrakālyupajīvakaḥ |
[63.17]     śuddhadevalakaḥ proktaḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ   || [ṣaṅkarṣaṇasaṃhitā.]
[63.18]     ārṣoktena vidhānena devalatvaṃ na vidyate |
[63.19]     tasmātsarvaprayatnena vaidikenaiva pūjayet ||" iti [ṣaṅkarṣaṇasaṃhitā]
[63.20]     śivaśekharatantre - rudraḥ:
[63.21]     "atha devalakān vakṣye śṛṇu devi samāhitā |
[63.22]     karmadevalakāḥ kecitkalpadevalakāḥ pare ||  [śivaśekharatantra]
[63.23]     śuddhadevalakāstvanye tridhā devalakāsmṛtāḥ |
[63.24]     anyasūtroktamārgeṇa yassaṃvatsarapūjakaḥ ||





[śivaśekharatantra]

[kalpadevalakalakṣaṇam]

[64.1]     adīkṣitaḥ svayaṃ tantre karmadevalakastu saḥ |
[64.2]     anenaiva prakāreṇa vatsaratryapūjakaḥ |
[64.3]     kalpadevalakaḥ proktaḥ sa vai śailasute dhruvam ||  [śivaśekharatantra]

[śuddhadevalakalakṣaṇam]

[64.4]     ananujñātamācāryātāgamaṃ śuśruve ca yaḥ |
[64.5]     śīlataḥ tasya śiṣyaśca śuddhadevalakāvubhau || [śivaśekharatantra]

[trividhasyāpi devalakatvasya rudrādiviṣayakatā]

[64.6]     tridhā devalakatvaṃ tu na viṣṇuviṣaye kvacit |
[64.7]     rudrakālyādiviṣayametadāgamasaṃmatam ||" iti  [śivaśekharatantra]
[64.8]     smṛtyantare: "śarvaṃ sūryaṃ tathā candraṃ durgādī rudradevatāḥ |
[64.9]     yo'rcayetpaṇapūrvaṃ saḥ sadyaḥ patati mānavaḥ ||
[64.10]     durgā ca bhadrakālī ca buddhaśca kṣetrapālakaḥ |
[64.11]     pañcapūjā ca ityete pañcadevalakāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
[64.12]     ādityamambikāṃ viṣṇuṃ gaṇanāthaṃ maheśvaram |
[64.13]     gṛhe pūjā ca ityete pañcadevalakāḥ smṛtāḥ (?) ||" iti
[64.14]     viṣṇudharmottare: "caṇḍikāyāśca durgāyā jyeṣṭāyā bhairavasya ca |
[64.15]     rudrasya pūjakā ye vai te vai devalakāḥ smṛtāḥ ||" iti  [Viṣṇudharmottaraড় ]
[64.16]     viṣṇu: "devārcanaparo yo'pi parārthaṃ vittakāṅkṣayā |
[64.17]     caturvedadharo vipraḥ sa caṇḍālasamo bhaved ||" iti [Viṣṇuড় ]
[64.18-19]     evamebhirvacanaiḥ śāstrānanumatamārgeṇa pūjakānāṃ devatāntarapūjakānāmeva ca devalakatvaṃ siddhyatīti sphuṭamavagamyate.
[64.20]     kiñca: "vṛṣo dharmassamākhyataḥ tasya yaḥ kurute layam |
[64.21]     vṛṣalaṃ taṃ vidurdevā nikṛṣṭaṃ śvapacādapi ||"





[devalakaśabdanirvacanam]

[64.22-65.2]      iti vṛṣalaśabdo yathā vedaśāstroktamārgalayakārake vyutpāditaḥ, tathā devalakśabdo'pi "divkrīḍāvijigīṣāvyavahāradyutistutimodamadasvapnakāntigatiṣv" iti dhātvarthānusāreṇa gatyaparaparyāyamārgārthakadivdhātughaṭitassan vedoktamārgalayaprāpake vyutpādayituṃ śakyate iti tatpratīpeṣu vedamārgapratiṣṭhāpakeṣu vaikhānaseṣu na sarvātmanā pravartate. tathā ca:
[65.3]     hārītaḥ: "vaikhānasāstu ye viprā haripūjanatatparāḥ |
[65.4]     na te devalakāḥ proktā viṣṇupādābjasaṃśrayāt ||" iti  [Vṛddhahārīta 8.78]

[vaikhānasānaṃ devalakaśabdavācyatvābhāvaḥ]

[65.5]     smṛtyarthasāre: atri: "vaidikenaiva vidhinā dambhalobhavivarjitaḥ |
[65.6]     pūjayedbhaktito viṣṇuṃ sa hi devalako na vai ||" iti
[65.7-8]     anyathā śrutyuktamārgeṇālayārcakānāmapi devalakatve tādṛśālayārcanapratipādakānāṃ śrutismṛtipurāṇādivacanānāṃ vyarthatā syāt.
[65.9-10]     nanu - ajñānataḥ kṛtamapi bhagavadārādhanamapavargapradañcet. vinā śāstramanadhikāriṇāpi kṛtaṃ tatapavargapradaṃ kiṃ na syāditi cet. na. bhagavadājñābhaṅgāpatteḥ.
[65.11]     yathoktaṃ bhagavatā: "yaśśāstravidhimutsṛjya vartate kāmakārataḥ |
[65.12]     na sa siddhimavāpnoti na sukhaṃ na parāṃ gatim ||" iti  [ṃBh6.38.23]
[65.13]     "śrutismṛtī mamaivājñā yastāmullaṅghya vartate |
[65.14]     ājñācchedī mama drohī madbhakto'pi na vaiṣṇavaḥ ||" iti
[65.15]     "mama cāpi priyaṃ viṣṇordevadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ |
[65.16]     mānuṣo vaidikācāraṃ manasā'pi na laṅghayet ||" iti
[65.17-18]     ataḥ anadhikāriṇā kṛtaṃ śāstrāsammatamiti tantroktaprakāreṇa kṛtamapi tatnāpavargapradam.
[65.19]     nanu: "brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyaiḥ vaiśyaiḥ śūdraiśca kṛtalakṣaṇaiḥ |
[65.20]     arcanīyaśca sevyaśca pūjanīyaśca mādhavaḥ |
[65.21]     sāttvataṃ vidhimāsthāya gītaḥ saṅgarṣaṇena yaḥ ||"

[tantrānusāripūjāyāḥ apavargapradatvābhāvaḥ]

[65.22-24]     iti śūdrādīnāmapi pāñcarātroktamārgeṇārcane adhikāre siddhe kathaṃ tāntrikoktaprakāreṇārcanaṃ kurvataḥ brāhmaṇādeḥ (anadhikāriṇaḥ) tatapavargapradaṃ na bhavatītyucyata iti ceducyate.




[caturvidhatantreṣu ekatra dīkṣitasya tantrāntare'nadhikāraḥ]

[66.1]     "prathamaṃ mantrasiddhāntaṃ dvitīyañcāgamāhvayam | [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 19.112 d]
[66.2]     tṛtīyaṃ tantrasiddhāntaṃ tantrāntaramataḥ param ||"  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 19.113 b]
[66.3]     iti caturvidheṣu siddhānteṣu
[66.4]     "ekatra dīkṣitastantre siddhānte vā dvijottama |
[66.5]     kriyāṃ na kuryādanyatra karṣaṇādi caturmukha   || [ড়ādma.ṣcaryāpāda 19.131]
[66.6]     ācāryakamathārtvijyaṃ pūjāñca madhuvidviṣaḥ |
[66.7]     mantre deve ca siddhānte tadevāsya hi yujyate ||" [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 19.132]
[66.8]     iti caturvidhatantreṣu ekatra dīkṣitasya tantrāntare praveśayogyatābhāvasya pratipādanāt.
[66.9]     "ekagotrasamutpannaṃ pañcagotraṃ pṛthakpṛthak | ityārabhya
[66.10]     sūtraṃ kātyāyanaṃ śākhāṃ yājuṣaṃ śuklameva ca ||
[66.11]     aupagāyanaśāṇḍilyau bhāradvājo'tha gautamaḥ |
[66.12]     mauñjāyanistu pañcaite pāñcarātrādhikāriṇaḥ ||"
[66.13]     ityadhikāribhedasya vidhīyamānatvāt.

[āpastambādisūtrasaṃskṛtasya pāñcarātroktasaṃskārayogyatābhāvaḥ]

[66.14]     "caturṇāmapi varṇānāmitareṣāñca tadbhuvām |
[66.15]     dīkṣā samaiva sarveṣāmācārastantravartmanā ||"  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 21.55]

[tantroktaprakāreṇa dīkṣitānāmeva tantroktārcanāyāmadhikāraḥ]

[66.16-18]     ityāpastambādisūtraiḥ saṃskṛtasya pāñcarātroktamārgeṇa niṣekādisaṃskārayogyatābhavakathanāt. tāntrikoktaprakāreṇa dīkṣitānāmeva tantroktārcanāyāmadhikāritva sambhavāt.

[paradharmānuṣṭhātuḥ patitatvādi]

[66.19]     "svasūtre vartamāne tu parasūtreṇa vartate |
[66.20]     apramāṇamṛṣiṃ kṛtvā so'ndhe tamasi majjati ||"
[66.21]     "svakīyadharmamutsṛjya paradharmaṃ ya āśrayet |
[66.22]     patitassa bhavedāśu sadya eva na saṃśayaḥ ||"
[66.23]     "svasūtrasya parityāgātparasūtrasamāśrayāt |
[66.24]     sadyaḥ patati vai vipro na vedasya samāśraye ||"






[67.1]     "śākhāṃ śikhāṃ tathā puṇḍraṃ samayācārameva ca |
[67.2]     pūrvairācaritaṃ kuryādanyathā patito bhavet ||"
[67.3]     "yassvaśākhāṃ parityajya paraśākhāsu vartate |
[67.4]     śākhāraṇḍassa vijñeyassarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||"  [āṣ4.59]
[67.5]     "yassvadharmaṃ parityajya paradharmaṃ niṣevate |
[67.6]     pāṣaṇḍinaṃ tamāhurvai nikṛṣṭaṃ śvapacādapi ||" iti.
[67.7]     bhagavān: "śreyān svadharmo viguṇaḥ paradharmātsvanuṣṭhitāt |
[67.8]     svadharme nidhanaṃ śreyaḥ paradharmo bhayāvahaḥ ||" iti.  [ṃBh6.25.35]
[67.9]     vyāsa: "svadharmaṃ yassamutsṛjya paradharmaṃ samāśrayet |
[67.10]     anāpadi sa vidvadbhiḥ patitaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||"
[67.11-12]     ityādibhiśca vacanaiḥ paradharmānuṣṭhātuḥ patitatvādismaraṇācca anyasūtroktaprakāreṇa tāntrikoktena mārgeṇa vā bāhmaṇādibhirapi anadhikāribhiḥ bhagavadārādhanaṃ kartuṃ na śakyate.

[śūdrasya pūjāyāmanadhikāraḥ]

[67.13]     tarhi "brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ" ityādi bhāratavacanasya kā gatiriti cet- ucyate.
[67.14]     mādhavāḥ: mukhyatvena advārakabhagavadyajanādhikāritvena nirūpitaiḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ pūjanīyaḥ.
[67.15-16]     itaraiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiścārcanīyaḥ. śūdraiḥ strībhiśca yathārhamijyassevyaśca iti tasya vacanasyārthaḥ.
[67.17]     "... śūdraiḥ strībhistathetaraiḥ |
[67.18]     yathārhamijyassevyaśca nityaṃ sarveśvaro hariḥ ||"
[67.19-22]     iti bhāradvājavacanāt"arghyāntamarcanaṃ proktaṃ havirantañca pūjanam ||" ityarcanapūjanayoḥ bhinnārthakatvapratipādanāt, sarvairekarītyā pūjane'bhimate ekapadasyaiva paryāptatvātitarayoḥ padayorvaiyarthyāpātācca. anyathā śūdrasyāpi pūjārcanādiṣvadhikārāṅgīkāre śrutismṛtisūtrapurāṇādivirodho bhavet.
[67.23-25]     śūdrasyānadhikārapratipādakāni vacanāni caitāni. śruti "tasmācchūdro yajñe'navakḷptaḥ. sa hi devatā anvasṛjata tasmācchūdro bahupaśurayajñīyo daiva" iti.





[68.1]     smṛti: "japastapastīrthasevā pravrajyā mantrasādhanam |
[68.2]     devatārādhanañcaiva strīśūdrapatitāni ṣaṭ ||" iti

[śūdrasyadvijaśuśrūṣaiva dharmaḥ]

[68.3]     śrībhāgavate: "śuśrūṣaṇaṃ dvijagavāṃ devānāñcāpyamāyayā |
[68.4]     tatra labdhena santoṣaḥ śūdraprakṛtayastvimāḥ ||" iti [bhāgavatapurāṇa  11.17.19]
[68.5]     manau: "brāhmaṇasya tapo jñānaṃ tapaḥ kṣatrasya rakṣaṇam |
[68.6]     vaiśyasya tu tapo vārtā tapaśśūdrasya sevanam ||" iti [ṃnuḍhś11.235]
[68.7]     anyatra: "yadi śūdrastapaḥ kuryādvedadṛṣṭena vartmanā |
[68.8]     iha cāsya parikleśaḥ pretya cāsyāśubhā gatiḥ ||" iti.
[68.9-11]     kimarthaṃ śūdrasya dvijaśuśrūṣā sarvadā abhihiteti cet- ucyate. janmāntarīyakarmavaśātjananakāle'pi tamoguṇairabhibhūtasya tasya śūdrasya tamonirasanārthaṃ dvijaśuśrūṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇā prayuktam.
[68.12]     āśvamedhike: "udite savitaryasya kriyāyuktasya dhīmataḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1690]
[68.13]     caturvedadharasyāpi dehe ṣaḍvṛṣalāḥ sthitāḥ || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1691]
[68.14]     kṣatriye sapta vijñeyā vaiśye tvaṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1692]
[68.15]     niyatāḥ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha śūdre'ṣṭāviṃśatiḥ khalu ||" ityārabhya [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.1693]
[68.16]     "rāgadveṣau ca mohaśca pāruṣyañca nṛśaṃsatā | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3340]
[68.17]     śāṭhyañca dīrghavairañca atimānamanārjavam || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3341]
[68.18]     anṛtañcāpavādaśca paiśunyamatilobhatā | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3342]
[68.19]     hiṃsā steyaṃ mṛṣāvādo vañcanā roṣalobhate || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3343]
[68.20]     abuddhatā ca nāstikyaṃ bhayamālasyameva ca | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3344]
[68.21]     aśaucañcākṛtajñatvaṃ dambhatā stambha eva ca || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3345]
[68.22]     niṣkṛteścāpyavijñānaṃ janane śūdramāviśan | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3346]
[68.23]     dṛṣṭvā pitāmahaḥ śūdramabhibhūtan tu tāmasaiḥ ||  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3347]
[68.24]     dvijaśuśrūṣaṇaṃ dharmaṃ śūdrāṇān tu prayuktavān | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3348]
[68.25]     naśyanti tāmasā bhāvāḥ śūdrasya dvijabhaktitaḥ ||" iti. [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.3349]






[vaikhānasetarasūtriṇāṃ bhagavadārādhanaprakāraḥ]

[69.1]     ataḥ tamoguṇābhibhūtasya śūdrasya bhagavadyajanādhikāro nāstyeva.
[69.2-7]     nanveṣāṃ śaunakādyuktaprakāreṇa bhagavadārādhane'dhikāro bhavediti cet- tadasat. tatra adhikāriviśeṣapratipādanābhavāt; sāmānyenoktatve'pi śrutismṛtisūtrapurāṇamahābhāratavaikhānasapāñcarātraśaivaśilpajyautiṣādiṣu adhikāriṇāṃ viśiṣya pratipāditatvātsarvaśākhāpratyayanyāyena sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyena "matprakaraṇādhītaṃ sarvaṃ madartham" iti nyāyena prakaraṇasāmyena, upāsya devataikyātguṇopasaṃhāranyāyena ca śaunakādibhiruktārcanāvidherapi atraiva paryavasānaṃ yuktam.
[69.8-9]      itarasūtriṇāṃ kathamiti cet- svāśramadharmācaraṇenaiva bhagavadārādhanaphalaṃ siddhyati. yathocyate:
[69.10]     smṛtiṣu: "bahvalpaṃ vā svagṛhyoktaṃ yasya karma pracoditam |
[69.11]     tasya tāvati śāstrārthe kṛte sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet ||" iti
[69.12]     śrīviṣṇupurāṇe: "varṇāśramācāravatā puruṣeṇa paraḥ pumān |
[69.13]     viṣṇurārādhyate panthā nānyastattoṣakārakaḥ ||"  [Viṣṇuড় 2.8.9]
[69.14]     tathānyatra: "varṇāśrameṣu ye dharmāḥ śāstroktā munisattama |
[69.15]     teṣu tiṣṭhannaro viṣṇumārādhayati nānyathā || [Viṣṇuড় 3.8.19.1-2?]
[69.16]     ye yajanti pitṝn devān brāhmaṇāṃśca hutāśanam |
[69.17]     sarvabhūtāntarātmānaṃ viṣṇumeva yajanti te ||" iti
[69.18]     purāṇāntare: "agnikāryaṃ japaṃ snānaṃ tapassvādhyāyameva ca |
[69.19]     tamevoddiśya deveśaṃ kuru nityamatandritaḥ ||
[69.20]     prātarutthāya vidhivatsnātvā nityaṃ samāhitaḥ |
[69.21]     yajetkarmabhistaistaiḥ viṣṇuṃ devādisaṃjñitam ||"





[70.1]     śāṇḍilya:"prakṛttaiśca nivṛttaiśca svargadairmokṣadairapi |
[70.2]     ārādhyo bhagavāneva vedadharmaissanātanaiḥ   || [śāṇḍilyasmṛti.]
[70.3]     sa eva sarvathopāsyo nānyassaṃsāratārakaḥ |
[70.4]     ubhābhyāṃ jñānakarmabhyāmārādhyo bhagavān hariḥ ||" iti [śāṇḍilyasmṛti]
[70.5]     śāntiparvaṇi: "svayambhūvihito dharmo yo yasya hi nareśvara | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.222]
[70.6]     sa tena kṣapayetpāpaṃ samyagācaritena hi ||" iti [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.223]
[70.7]     pādme: "dharmānnijāṃśca śāstroktān varṇadharmānaśeṣataḥ |
[70.8]     pālayantīha ye vaiśyā na te yānti yamālayam ||" iti  [ড়dmaড় ]
[70.9]     smṛtyantare: "śrutismṛtisadācāravihitaṃ karma yassadā |
[70.10]     svasvadharmaṃ prayatnena śreyo'rthīha samāśrayet ||"
[70.11-12]     "nānyaḥ panthā vidyate'yanāye"'ti "brāhmaṇā vividaśanti yajñena dānena tapasānāśakena" iti ca śrutirimamarthaṃ draḍhayanti.
[70.13-15]     ataḥ advārakabhagavadyajanādhikāribhiḥ śrīmadvaikhānasasūtribhissāmyaṃ keṣāñcidapi nopapadyate iti kṛtaṃ pallavitena. etāvatā "akhilajagatkāraṇabhūtena vikhanasā praṇītatvāt" iti prathamo hetuḥ nirūpitaḥ.
[70.16]     iti prathamahetunirūpaṇam





[71.1]     atha dvitīyahetunirūpaṇam
[71.2]     athāsya vaikhānasasūtrasya sarvasūtrāṇāmāditvaṃ nirūpyate. tathā hi:

[vaikhānasasūtrasya sarvasūtrāpekṣayā āditvam]

[71.3]     brahmakaivarte: " yathā munīnāṃ vikhanā ādibhūta udāhṛtaḥ |
[71.4]     sūtrāṇāṃ tatpraṇītan tu tathā śreṣṭatamaṃ smṛtam ||" iti  [Brahmakaivartaড় ]
[71.5]     purātantre bhṛgu: "vedānāṃ vyāsanādarvākprāgrūpaṃ militan tu yat |
[71.6]     tān tu vaikhānasīṃ śākhāmiti vedavido viduḥ ||" ityārabhya  [ড়urātantra]
[71.7]     "vaiṣṇavaṃ sūtrametaddhi sarvasiddhikaraṃ param |
[71.8]     ādyatvātsarvasūtrāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavatvācca tattu vai ||  [ড়urātantra]
[71.9]     mayānuvartitaṃ tadvatkāśyapātrimarīcibhiḥ ||" iti  [ড়urātantra]
[71.10]     skānde ca: "vaikhānasīṃ mahāśākhāṃ svasūtre viniyuktavān |
[71.11]     padmabhūḥ paramo dhātā tasminnārādhanatrayam ||
[71.12]     uktavānnigamārthānāmācāraṃ pravibhāgaśaḥ ||" iti  [ṣkandaড় ]

[ādau caturaśrasya ravimaṇḍalasya kālāntare vartulatvam]

[71.13-14]     sūtrārthaparyālocanayāpi sarvasūtrāditvamasyāvagamyate. yathā -"mukhyādindraścāgniśce" ti bhagavanmukhādagnisṛṣṭiḥ śrūyate. tathā:
[71.15]     āśvamedhike - bhagavadvacanam:
[71.16]     "prathamaṃ manmukhātsṛṣṭo lokārthe pacanaḥ prabhuḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2572]
[71.17]     sṛṣṭamātro jagatsarvamattumaicchatpurā khalu || [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2573]
[71.18]     tataḥ praśamitasso'yamupāsīno mayā purā | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2574]
[71.19]     satatopāsanātso'yamaupāsana iti smṛtaḥ ||" iti [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2575]
[71.20-21]     evaṃ paramapuruṣamukhājjātasyaupāsanāgneḥ kuṇḍavidhānādikamatraiva sūtre pratipāditam.
[71.22-24]     kiñca navagrahamakhe savitṛmaṇḍalasya caturaśratvamucyate "caturaśraṃ vṛttamityādinā (sūtre). ādau caturaśrasya sato ravimaṇḍalasya kālāntare vṛttatvakalpanaṃ pramāṇairavagamyate. yathā:





[72.1]     viṣṇupurāṇe: "tejasaśśamanañcāsya viśvakarmā cakāra ha |
[72.2]     bhramimāropya sūryan tu tasya tejo'vaśātanam   || [Viṣṇu.ড় ]
[72.3]     kṛtavānaṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ sa vyaśātayadavyayam |
[72.4]     yatsūryādvaiṣṇavaṃ tejaśśātitaṃ viśvakarmaṇā   || [Viṣṇu.ড় ]
[72.5]     jājjvalyamānamapatattadbhūmau munisattama ||"iti [Viṣṇuড় ]
[72.6]     evaṃ ca sūryasāvarṇikamanvantaramārabhya ravimaṇḍalasya vṛttākārapratītiḥ.
[72.7-8]     tadanusāreṇa bodhāyanādibhiḥ vṛttākāratvaṃ ravimamaṇḍalasyoktam. tataḥ pūrvaṃ caturaśratvaṃ va. tadevāsmin sūtre pratipāditam.
[72.9-10]     evaṃ "vānaprastho vaikhānaśāstrasamudācāro vaikhānasa" iti vaikhānasasūtramanusmṛtya tṛtīyāśramadharmān bodhāyanaḥ pratipādayati. [ḍhṣ2.6.11.14]
[72.11-17]     tathā āgnivaiśyasūtre "athātassūtragaṇanāṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ. bodhāyanamāpastambaṃ satyāṣāṭhaṃ drāhyāyaṇamāgastyaṃ śākalyamāśvalāyanaṃ śāmbhavīyaṃ kātyāyanamiti navāni pūrvasūtrāṇi. vaikhānasaṃ śaunakīyaṃ bhāradvājamāgnivaiśyaṃ jaiminīyaṃ  vādhūlaṃ mādhyandinaṃ hiraṇyakeśīyaṃ kauṣītakamiti navānyaparasūtrāṇi. evamaṣṭādaśasūtrāṇyaṣṭādaśasūtrakārāśca. etāni caturvedamūlāni. pūrvasūtrāṇi śārīrāṇi sāmānyāni bhavanti. uttarasūtrāṇi vaikhānasādīni viṣṇusaṃbandhīni daivikānī" ti vaikhānasasūtrasyāditvaṃ vaiṣṇavatvañcocyate.
[72.18-19]     asya sūtrasyādhikyaṃ ca "ye vaikhānasasūtreṇa saṃskṛtāsdvijātaya" ityādinā pūrvameva pratipāditam.
[72.20-21]     ataḥ sarvasūtrāṇāmādiridaṃ sūtramiti siddham. tataḥ sarvasūtrottamatvañcāsyopapadyate iti nirūpito dvitīyo hetuḥ.
[72.22]     iti dvitīyahetunitūpaṇam.





[tṛtīyahetunirūpaṇam]

[vaikhānasasūtrasya śrutimārgānusāritvam]

[73.1-3]     athāsya śrīvaikhānasasūtrasya sūtrāntarāpekṣayā vailakṣaṇyāpādakaṃ pratyakṣaśrutimūlakatvamastīti jñāpayituṃ "sarvakarmasu śrutimārgānusāritvād" iti tṛtīyo heturnirūpyate. tathā hi:

[śuddhācamanavidheḥ śrautatvam]

[73.4-9]     śuddhācamanavidhau "yattrirācāmati tena ṛcaḥ prīṇāti" ityādi śrutyanusāreṇa sūtre "ṛgvedaḥ prīṇātv" ityādinā śuddhācamanamuktam. nanu bodhāyanenāpi śuddhācamanavidhirukta iti cetsatyam. "ācamanavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ" ityārabhya "gokarṇabaddhahastena trirācamet, prathamaṃ yatpibati tena ṛgvedaṃ prīṇāti yaddvitīyaṃ tena yajurvedaṃ yattṛtīyaṃ tena sāmavedaṃ prīṇātī"tyācamanena ṛgvedādīnāṃ prītirbhavatītyuktam. na tu mantraḥ.
[73.10-11]     śrutismṛtisūtravihitasya sandhyopāsanasya pradhānabhūtamarghyapradānādikamatraiva pratipāditam. "prātassūryaścetyādinācamya āpo hiṣṭhādibhistisṛbhiḥ prokṣya
[73.12]     gāyatryā'po'bhimantrya ādityābhimukhaṃ vikṣipya pradakṣiṇaṃ karoti" ityādi.
[73.13-17]     bodhāyanenāpi sandhyāvandanavidhiruktaḥ iti cetnaivam. "athātassandhyopāsanavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ. tīraṃ gatvāprayato'bhiṣiktaḥ prayato vā nābhiṣiktaḥ prakṣālipādapāṇirapa ācamya surabhimatyā abliṅgābhirvāruṇībhiḥ hiraṇyavarṇābhiḥ pāvamānībhiḥ vyāhṛtibhiranyaiśca pavitrairātmānaṃ prokṣya prayato bhavati. athāpyudāharanti." [ḍhṣ2.4.7.1-3]
[73.18]     "āpo'vagāhanaṃ snānaṃ vihitaṃ sārvavarṇikam |
[73.19]     mantravatprokṣaṇañcaiva dvijānāmavaśiṣyate ||" iti  [ḍhṣ2.4.7.3-2]
[73.20-74.3]      sarvakarmaṇāñcaivārambheṣu prāksandhyopāsanākālāccaitenaiva pavitrasamūhenātmānaṃ prokṣya prayato bhavati. athāpyudāharanti. "darbheṣvāsīno darbhān dhārayamāṇaḥ sodakapāṇinā pratyaṅmukhaḥ sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtvaḥ āvartayet. prāṇāyāmaśo vā śatakṛtvaḥ. tribhiśca prāṇāyāmaiḥ tato (tānto) brahmahṛdayena. vāruṇībhyāṃ rātrimupatiṣṭhate." ityādinā mārjanādikamuktam. pradhānabhūtamarghyapradānādikamanuktam. [ḍhṣ2.4.7.4-9]





[śrutyukto brahmayajñaprakāraḥ]

[brahmavaraṇapūrvakasomavaraṇam]

[74.4-9]     brahmayajñaḥ. talavakāropaniṣadi "tasyā vā eṣa prathamaḥ pādaḥ oṃ bhūstatsaviturvareṇyamoṃ bhuvaḥ bhargo devasya dhīmahi oṃ suvaḥ dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayātiti pacchaḥ, oṃ bhūrbhuvastatsaviturvareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi oṃ suvaḥ dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayātityardhacaśaḥ, oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvastatsaviturvareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayātiti samastāñca sāvitrīṃ japtveti yathodiṣṭaḥ, tathātra sūtre'pi ukto'sti.
[74.10-11]     āghārādiṣu sarvamapi śrautavat. atra brahmavaraṇānantaraṃ somavaraṇamapyuktamasti. "somo'smākaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ rājā - somarājāno brāhmaṇā" iti.
[74.12-13]     "viṣṇuśca vai somaśca brahmavādyamavadetāṃ'ahaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ pratiṣṭhe'ti viṣṇurabravīt'ahaṃ pratiṣṭhe'ti somaḥ. tau prajāpatiṃ praśnamaitāṃ so'bravītprajāpatiḥ
[74.14]    'chandāṃsi viṣṇumadhigacchanti, nakṣatrāṇi somaṃ. tāvubhau brahmaṇyāv' iti so'bravīt.
[74.15-17]     pūjitau pūjayantau stutau stunvantau priyau priyavantau brahmaṇyau brahmavittau brāhmaṇānāṃ pratiṣṭhātārau bhavata iti yanmāṃ brahmaṇā vakṣyanti yajñeṣu so'hamiti viṣṇurabravīt. tasmādviṣṇuryajño brāhmaṇānāṃ pratiṣṭhā iti. vijñayate ca.
[74.18-22]     "brāhmaṇo vai chandāṃsī"tyetasmātbrāhmaṇātyanmāṃ brāhmaṇā vakṣyanti nakṣatreṣu tatrāhamiti so'bravīt. tasmātbrāhmaṇānāṃ somo rājeti tasmādvijñāyate ca brāhmaṇe vā aṣṭāviṃśo nakṣatrāṇāṃ tasya vacaḥ puṇyamiti tāvubhau brahmaṇyau brahmavittau brāhmaṇānāṃ pratiṣṭhātārau brāhmaṇeṣu pratiṣṭhitau ya evaṃ brāhmaṇapuraskṛtāni karmāṇi karoti yajñasya samṛddhyā" iti ca śrutiprasiddhatvāt.
[74.23-25]     evamatra śāntikapauṣṭikādiṣu pradhānakarmatvātnavagrahamakhasya pañcāgnikuṇḍavidhānamuktam. "atha grahaśāntiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ. grahyāyattā lokayātrā tasmādātmaviruddhe prāpte grahān samyakpūjayatī" ti.



[grahaśānteḥ prādhānyam]

[75.1-8]     grahaśānteḥ kathaṃ prādhānyamiti ceducyate. "udyantamastaṃyantamādityamabhidhyāyan kurvan brāhmaṇo vidvān sakalaṃ bhadramaśnute'sāvādityo brahmeti brahmaiva san brahmāpyeti ya evaṃ veda" iti "ya eṣo'ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣo dṛśyate" iti "yo'sau tapannudeti" ityādi "viṣṇuśakteravasthānaṃ sadāditye karoti ca" iti "dhyeyassadā savitṛmaṇḍalamadhyavartī" tyādibhiśca vacanaiḥ yatpratipāditaṃ savitṛmaṇḍalamadhyavartino bhagavato nārāyaṇasya yajanaṃ tadevedamityabhiprāyeṇa sūtrakārairasya grahayajñasya prādhānyamuktam. tathānte "anyathā mahattaro doṣo bhavatī"tyapyuktam.
[75.9]     bodhāyanaśca: "athāto grahātithyabalikarmopahārān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ"

[savitṛhomaḥ]

[75.10]     aśraddhadhānamaśucimajapaṃ tyaktamaṅgalam |
[75.11]     grahā nayanti suvyaktaṃ puruṣaṃ yamasādanam ||
[75.12]     grahāṇāmugraceṣṭānāṃ nakṣatrapathacāriṇām |
[75.13]     upahārān pravakṣyāmi śāntyarthan tu yathāvidhi ||"

[viṣṇvārādhanasya śrautatvam]

[75.14-16]     māsi māsyṛtāvṛtau candragrahe sūryagrahe viṣuve'yane śubhāśubhe janmanakṣatre tadgrahāṇāmātithyaṃ saṃvatsarādapi prayuñjānaḥ sarvān kāmānavāpnoti" iti grahaśāntikālaṃ nirūpya.
[75.17]     "grahā gāvo narendrāśca brāhmaṇāśca viśeṣataḥ |
[75.18]     pūjitāḥ pūjayantyenaṃ nirdahantyavamānitāḥ ||"
[75.19]     ityakaraṇe pratyavāyamapi darśitavān.
[75.20-22]     tasmādeva sūtrakāraiḥ sūryacandrau vinā lokapravṛttyasambhavātsūryapradhānatvācca grahāṇāṃ sarvakarmaṇāmādau grahapuraskāramuktvā tatra pañcāgnikuṇḍavidhānamupadiśya pradhāne sabhye viṣṇurūpasya savituḥ homaḥ "sabhye savitur" iti pratipāditaḥ.
[75.23-24]     evam - "nārāyaṇa paraṃ brahma" - "brahmaiva bhūtānāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ tena ko'rhati spardhitum" - "agnirvai devānāmavamo viṣṇuḥ paramaḥ tadantareṇa sarvā anyā devatāḥ"



[śrutimūlakatve prāmāṇyātiśayaḥ]

[76.1]     ityādi śrutyanusāreṇa "tasmādgṛhe paramaṃ viṣṇuṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sāyaṃprātarhomānte'rcayati"
[76.2-3]     iti nārāyaṇaparatvena sūtrāntareṣvanuktamārādhanavidhimuktvā tatra "sa vā eṣa puruṣaḥ pañcadhā pañcātmā" ityādi śrutyanusāreṇa pañcamūrtividhānamapyuktam.
[76.4-5]     āghārādiṣu śrautavadeveti pratyakṣasiddhamiti pūrvamevoktam. evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyam.
[76.6-7]     śrutimārgānusāritve ko viśeṣa iti cet- ucyate. śrutimūlatve hi prāmāṇyātiśayasambhavaḥ. yathāha
[76.8]     manu: "vedaḥ smṛtiḥ sadācāraḥ svasya ca priyamātmanaḥ |
[76.9]     etaccatuṣṭayaṃ prāhuḥ sākṣāddharmasya lakṣaṇam ||  [ṃnuḍhś2.12]
[76.10]     sarvaṃ tu samavekṣyaitadakhilaṃ jñānacakṣuṣā |
[76.11]     śrutiprāmāṇyato vidvān svadharme niviśeta vai ||"  [ṃnuḍhś2.8]
[76.12]     śrībhārate: "vedapraṇihito dharmo hyadharmastadviparyayaḥ |
[76.13]     vedo nārāyaṇassākṣātsvayambhūriti śuśrumaḥ ||"  [ṃBh]
[76.14]     pracetā: "amīṃāṃsyā bahiśśāstrā ye cānye vedabarjitaḥ |
[76.15]     yattadbrūyurna tatkuryādvedāddharmo vidhīyate ||"
[76.16]     smṛtyantare: "dharmaṃ jijñāsamānānāṃ pramāṇaṃ prathamaṃ śrutiḥ | [ṃBh14 āpp. 4.2492]
[76.17]     dvitīyaṃ dharmaśāstrān tu tṛtīyaṃ lokasaṅgrahaḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh14 āpp. 4.2493]
[76.18-20]     etāvatā sarvakarmasu śrutimārgānusāritva rūpaḥ tṛtīyo heturnirūpito bhavati. tathā ca śrutimārgānusāritvādasya sūtrasya sarvasūtrottamatvaṃ siddhyati iti nirūpitastṛtīyo hetuḥ.
[76.21]     iti tṛtīyahetunirūpaṇam.





[caturthahetunirūpaṇam]

[vaikhānasasūtrasya samantrakasarvakriyāpratipādakatvam]

[77.1-2]     idānīṃ tāvatvaikhānasasūtroktadharmānuṣṭhānena anantasthiraphalaprāptiḥ satkulīnatvaṃ phalabhūyastvamapi bhavatīti jñāpayituṃ "samantrakasarvakriyāpratipādakatvād" iti caturtho hetu nirūpyate. tadyathā:
[77.4-10]     atra sūtra nityānuṣṭhānavidhau "indro'hamubhābhyām" ityārabhya karaprakṣālanādīni samantrakatvenoktāni. brahmayajñe "ṛtañca satyañce" tyādi viśeṣaḥ. puṇyāhe samantrakatvaṃ spaṣṭam. sūtrāntareṣvanuktamaupāsanāgnikuṇdavidhānaṃ pratipāditam. āghāravidhau samantrakatvaṃ siddham. antahome tathaiva. nāndīmukhapūrvakopanayanādiṣu śārīreṣu saṃskāreṣu samantrakatvaṃ pratyakṣam. sthālīpākādiṣu "pūrvavad" ityanena pūrvaṃ vistareṇa pratipāditasyāghāravidheḥ prayogasaukaryārthaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa pratipādanam.

[aparakriyāyāmapi samantrakatvam]

[77.11-15]     aparakriyāyāmapyasti samantrakatvam. tatra kvacit"tūṣṇīm" ityuktvatvātsamantrakatvaṃ nāstīti svayamevāśaṅkya prakārāntareṇa samantrakatvaṃ pratyapādayat. tatra tatra kvacitmantrayogyatābhāve'pi vihitaviśaye sarvatra samantrakatvamastītyavagamyate. sūtrāntareṣu vihitaviśayeṣvapi samantrakatvānukteḥ teṣāṃ samantrakasarvakriyātvaṃ nāsti. tatra aparakriyāyāmapi sūtrāntarānapekṣā dṛśyate.
[77.16-19]     bodhāyanena agnimukhādiṣu paridhiparistaraṇādikṣepaṇaṃ tūṣṇīmevoktam. samantrakatvena karmaṇāmuktau ko viśeṣa iti cet- ucyate. karmaṇāṃ mantravattve hi vīryavattaratvaṃ karmārādhya bhagavatprīṇanadvārā anantasthiraphalaprāptiḥ satkulīnatvañca bhavantīti smṛtipurāṇādiṣvavagamyate. yathā

[karmaṇāṃ samantrakatve vīryavattaratvam]

[77.20-22]     chāndogye: "yadeva vidyayā karoti śraddhayopaniṣadā tadeva vīryavattaraṃ bhavatī"ti. tatraivaṃ vyākhyātam. "mantraprakāśitāni karmāṇi kriyante nāmantra kamasti karme"ti.





[mantrahīnakarmanindā]

[78.1]     ātharvaṇe: "ṛgyajussāmākhyā (?) mantreṣu karmāṇi kavayo yānyapaśyanniti"
[78.2]     āraṇyaparvaṇi: "vratopanayanābhyāṃ vā upavāsena vā dvijaḥ |
[78.3]     kriyāmantraiśca saṃyukto brāhmaṇassyānna saṃśayaḥ ||" [ṃBh3.81.134]
[78.4]     tatraiva: "guṇābhāve phalaṃ nyūnaṃ bhavatyaphalameva vā |
[78.5]     anārambhe tu na phalaṃ na guṇo dṛśyate hyutaḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh3.33.48]
[78.6]     bṛhannāradīye: "amantrato haviryattu hūyate jātavedase |
[78.7]     apātre dīyate yacca tadghoraṃ bhogasādhanam   || [Bṛhannāradaড়.]
[78.8]     hutaṃ haviścāśucinā dattaṃ yatkarma yatkṛtam |
[78.9]     tatsarvaṃ tava bhāgārhamadhaḥpātaphalaprada ||" [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[78.10]     iti balicakravartinaṃ prati bhagavatā vāmanenoktam.
[78.11]     bodhāyana: "gobhiraśvaiśca yānaiśca kṛṣyā rājopasevayā |
[78.12]     kulānyakulatāṃ yānti yāni hīnāni mantrataḥ || [ḍhṣ1.5.10.28]
[78.13]     mantratassusamṛddhāni kulānyalpadhanānyapi |
[78.14]     kulasaṃkhyāṃ ca gacchanti karṣanti ca mahadyaśaḥ ||" [ḍhṣ1.5.10.29]
[78.15]     manurapi: "kuvivāhaiḥ kriyālopairvedānadhyayanena ca |
[78.16]     kulānyakulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca || [ṃnuḍhś3.63]
[78.17]     śilpena vyavahāreṇa śuddhāpatyaiśca kevalaiḥ |
[78.18]     gobhiraśvaiśca yānaiśca kṛṣyā rājopasevayā || [ṃnuḍhś3.64]
[78.19]     ayājyayājanāccaiva nāstikyena ca karmaṇā |
[78.20]     kulānyakulatāṃ yānti yāni hīnāni mantrataḥ || [ṃnuḍhś3.65]
[78.21]     mantratastu samṛddhāni kulānyalpadhanāni ca |
[78.22]     kulasaṃkhyāṃ ca gacchanti karṣanti ca mahadyaśaḥ ||" [ṃnuḍhś3.66]





[79.1]     mantrapūtan tu yacchrāddham" iti somotpattau smaryate.
[79.2]     śrīgītāyāñca: "vidhihīnamasṛṣṭānnaṃ mantrahīnamadakṣiṇam |
[79.3]     śraddhāvirahitaṃ yajñaṃ tāmasaṃ paricakṣate ||" ityuktam. [bhagavadgītāī = ṃBh6.39.13]
[79.4-5]     yathopāsanaṃ phalamiti ca śrutisiddhaṃ "yathā kraturasmin loke puruṣo bhavati tathetaḥ pretya bhavatī"ti.
[79.6-7]     ataḥ śārīreṣu saṃskāreṣu samantrakasarvakriyāpratipādanātpūrṇatvaṃ, pūrṇatvādeva sarvasūtrottamatvaṃ cāsya sūtrasya siddhamiti nirūpitaḥ caturtho hetuḥ.
[79.8]     iti caturthahetunirūpaṇam.



[pañcamahetunirūpaṇam; vaikhānasasūtrasya niṣekādisaṃskārapratipādakatvam]

[80.1]     atha pañcamahetunirūpaṇam.
[80.2-5]     athāsya śrīvaikhānasasūtrasya sarvottamatve tatsūtroktadharmānuṣṭhātṝṇāmapi sarvotkṛṣṭatve ca itarasūtriṣu mukhyatvenānupapannaṃ niṣekādisaṃskārādimattvamasya sūtrasya mukhyata evopapadyata iti jñāpayituṃ niṣekādisaṃskārādimatvāditi pañcamo heturnirūpyate. yathā:

[saṃskāreṣu niṣekasya prathamatvam]

[80.6]     "atha niṣekādisaṃskārān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ, ṛtusaṅgamane"tyupakramya
[80.7]     "pāṇigrahaṇānī"tyantena śārīrasaṃskārā niṣekasaṃskārapūrvakatvena pratipāditāḥ.

[niṣekamantareṇa prajotpattyasaṃbhavaḥ]

[80.8-11]     nanu: jātakopanayanavivāhānāntarabhāvino niṣekasya kathamāditvaṃ pratipādyate? tathā "brāhmaṇo'sya mukhamāsīt" "manasā tveva bhūtāni pūrvaṃ dakṣo'sṛjattadā" ityādi śrutismṛtiṣu niṣekena vinā utpattidarśanācca niṣekasya kimarthamāditvaṃ pratipādyate? jātakasyāditvaṃ śrībhārate cāvagamyate.
[80.12]     "jātakarmādibhiryaistu saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaśśuciḥ |
[80.13]     vedādhyayanasampannaṣṣaṭsu karmasvavasthitaḥ   || [ṃB.h12.182.2]
[80.14]     śaucācāraratassamyakbhikṣārthī ca gurupriyaḥ |
[80.15]     nityavratassatyaparaḥ sa vai brāhmaṇa ucyate ||" iti [ṃBh12.182.3]
[80.16]     evaṃ harivaṃśe:"kva dārāḥ kva ca saṃsargaḥ kva ca bhāvaviparyayaḥ |
[80.17]     yadiyaṃ brahmaṇā sṛṣṭā manasā mānasī prajā || [ḥriV 35.42]
[80.18]     yadyasti tapaso vīryaṃ yuṣmākaṃ viditātmanām |
[80.19]     sṛjadhvaṃ mānasān putrān prājāpatyena karmaṇā ||" [ḥriV 35.43]
[80.20]     iti niṣekaṃ vināpi utpattiḥ śrūyate iti niṣekasyāditvaṃ na saṃbhavati iti cet-
[80.21-23]     tadasat. "niṣekavirahe'pi utpattissaṃbhavatī"tyetannopapadyate. tapobalādyogabalācca bhagavato nārāyaṇasya brahmaṇo maharṣīṇāṃ vā mānasaprajāsṛṣṭau śaktissambhavati. nānyeṣām. yathā:





[81.1]     śrīviṣṇupurāṇe: "saṅkalpāddarśanātsparśātpūrveṣāmabhavan prajāḥ" [Viṣṇuড় ]
[81.2]     tapoviṣeśairiddhānāṃ tadātyantatapasvinām ||" [Viṣṇuড় 1.15.79]
[81.3]     "yuge yuge bhavantyete dakṣādyā munisattamāḥ |
[81.4]     punaścaiva niruddhyante vidvāṃstatra na muhyati ||  [Viṣṇuড় 1.15.83]
[81.5]     kāniṣṭhyaṃ śryaiṣṭhyamapyeṣāṃ pūrvaṃ nāsti dvijottama || [Viṣṇuড় 1.15.84 b]
[81.6]     tapa eva garīyo'bhūtprabhāvaścaiva kāraṇam ||" iti [Viṣṇuড় ]

[niṣekenaiva rudrasyāpi utpattiḥ]

[81.7]     rudrotpattirapi niṣekeneti śrūyate. śatapathe: "abhūdvā idaṃ sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhā"
[81.8-10]     ityārabhya "bhūtānāñca prajāpatissaṃvatsarāyādikṣata bhūtānāṃ grahapatirāsīduṣā patnī"tyādikamuktvā "bhūtānāṃ patiḥ saṃvatsara uṣasi reto'siñcatsaṃvatsare kumāro'jāyata tasya rudrasya nāma karotī"tyādi.

[niṣekakriyāprakāraḥ]

[81.11-13]     niṣekakriyāprakāro'pi śrūyate hi. "nagnaṃ kṛtvorumupapravartayati yadā hi nagna ūrurbhavatyatha mithunībhavato'tha retassicyate'tha prajāḥ prajāyante" iti, "retasa eva siktasya bahuśo rūpāṇi vikaroti sa vai prajāyate śvaḥ śvo bhūyān bhavatī"ti,
[81.14-18]     "reto mūtraṃ vijahāti yoniṃ praviśadindriyaṃ garbho jarāyuṇā vṛtaḥ ulbaṃ jahāti janmanā" iti. muṇḍopaniṣadi - "pumān retassiñcati yoṣitāyāṃ bahvyaḥ prajāssṛjamānāssarūpāḥ" iti. chāndogye - "yoṣā vā va gautamāgnir" ityārabhya "tasya upastha eva samid. yadantaḥ karoti te'rciṣā. abhinandā visphuliṅgās. tasminnagnau devā reto juhvati. tasyā āhutergarbhassambhavatī"ti ca.

[sṛṣṭikālādārabhya niṣekādevotpattiḥ]

[81.19]     śrīviṣṇupurāṇe: "prajāssṛjeti vyādiṣṭaḥ pūrvaṃ dakṣassvayambhuvā |
[81.20]     yathā sasarja bhūtāni tathā śruṇu mahāmate || [Viṣṇuড় 1.15.86]
[81.21]     manasā tveva bhūtāni pūrvaṃ dakṣo'sṛjattadā |
[81.22]     devānṛṣīṃstu gandharvānasurān pannagāṃstathā || [Viṣṇuড় 1.15.87]
[81.23]     yadā'sya sṛjamānasya na vyavardhanta tāḥ prajāḥ |
[81.24]     tatassañcintya bahuśaḥ sṛṣṭihetoḥ prajāpatiḥ || [Viṣṇuড় 1.15.88]




[82.1]     maithunenaiva dharmeṇa sisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ |
[82.2]     tataḥ prabhṛti maitreya prajā maithunasambhavāḥ ||" [Viṣṇuড় 1.15.79 b?]
[82.3]     bhārate: "na caiṣāṃ maithuno dharmo babhūva bharatarṣabha |
[82.4]     saṅkalpādeva caiteṣāṃ garbhassamupapadyate || [ṃBh12.200.35]
[82.5]     tatra tretāyuge kāle saṃsparśājjāyate prajā |
[82.6]     na hyabhūnmaithuno dharmasteṣāmapi narādhipa   || [ṃB.h12.200.36]
[82.7]     dvāpare maithuno dharmaḥ prajānāmabhavannṛpa |
[82.8]     tathā kaliyuge kāle dvandvamāpedire janāḥ ||" iti [ṃBh12.200.37]
[82.9-10]     evaṃ śrutismṛtiṣu sṛṣṭikālādārabhya niṣekādevotpariti śravaṇātniṣekena vinā utpādanasāmarthyābhāvācca niṣekādimatvādityuktam.

[niṣekaśabdārthanirṇayaḥ]

[82.11-13]     atrāpastambīyā evamāhuḥ. "niṣeko nāma garbhādhānameva, niṣekagarbhādhānayoḥ paryāyatvātgarbhādhānātirekeṇa niṣekaśabdasyārthāntarāsambhavācca. garbhādhānaśabdasyārthe vicāryamāṇe'garbho'sminnādhīyate' iti vyutpattyā'indriyaṃ vai garbha'
[82.14-15]     iti śrutyanusāreṇa retassecanameva garbhādhānamityavagateḥ niṣekāditvamasmākamapyastī"ti.
[82.16-19]     atra baudhāyanīyaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhante. "yaduktaṃ'niṣeko nāma garbhādhānaṃ niṣekagarbhādhānayoḥ paryāyatvād' iti - tadasat. niṣekagarbhādhānayoḥ pṛthaktvena pratipādanātparyāyatvaṃ na ghaṭate. yathā -'caturthyāṃ snātāyāṃ niśāyāmalaṅkṛtya śayane'bhimantrayate'viṣṇuryonim' ityupakramya'athaināmupaiti' -'tvāṃ pūṣann ...' iti.
[82.20-23]     evaṃ niṣeka uktaḥ.'atha garbhādhānavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ. pūrvapakṣe puṇye nakṣatre brāhmaṇānannena pariviṣya puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā atha devayajanaprabhṛtyāgnimukhātkṛtvā pakvājjuhoti - pravedhase kavaye medhyāye'ti. iti garbhādhānamuktam. evaṃ niṣekagarbhādhānayoḥ pṛthaktvena kīrtanāt    niṣeka eva garbhādhānamiti vaktumayuktam.
[82.24-25]     kiñca āpastambasūtre niṣekapūrvakatvenānuktatvātcatvāriṃśatsaṃskāraparigaṇane agṛhītatvācca teṣāṃ niṣekādyāssaṃskārā na bhavantīti."





[83.1-6]     atra āpastambīyā ūcuḥ. "yadyasmākaṃ niṣekādyāḥ saṃskārā na bhavantītyucyate tarhi yuṣmākamapi tathaiva, bhavatsūtre'pi >yato etaddhutaḥ prahutaḥ āhutaḥ śūlagavo baliharaṇaṃ pratyavarohaṇamaṣṭakā pārvaṇahoma itī<tyārabhya vivāhādyevoktam - na tu niṣekāditvena. kiñca sūtropakrame saṃskāragaṇanāyāmapi niṣekastu saṃskāratvena vā śabdamātreṇa vā na pratipāditaḥ. kiñca >caturyyāṃ snātāyom< ityādinā pṛthaktvena vidhīyamānasya garbhādhānasya lakṣaṇapratipādanābhāvātviśiṣya kālanirūpaṇābhāvācca.

[vaikhānasānāmeva niṣekādisaṃskāravattvam]

[83.7]     (vaikhānase sūtre eva niṣekāditvena uktatvācca vaikhānasānāṃ niṣekāditvam),
[83.8]     āvayoḥ ubhayorapi niṣekādyāssaṃskārā na bhavanti iti. ayameva siddhāntaḥ.

[itareṣāṃ garbhadhānādisaṃskāravattvam]

[83.9]     tathā ca: "niṣekādyāḥ śmaśānāntāsteṣāṃ vai mantrataḥ kriyāḥ |  [ẏājñavalkyaḍhś1.10 d]
[83.10]     garbhādhānamṛtau puṃsasavanaṃ spandānātpurā ||" [ẏājñavalkyaḍhś1.11 b]
[83.11-14]     iti yājñavalkyoktau teṣāmiti śabdena "mukhye sambhavatyamukhyakalpanāyogād" iti nyāyena mukhyatvena śrīvaikhānasasūtre eva niṣekāditvasya vidyamānatvātvaikhānasā eva parāmṛśyante iti - teṣāmeva niṣekāditvam, itareṣāṃ niṣekāditvaṃ na sambhavati. api tu garbhādhānāditvameva. ayameva yājñavalkyābhiprāyaḥ.
[83.15-20]     athocyete. garbhādhānalakṣaṇamanuktaṃ viśiṣya kālaścānukta iti yaduktaṃ tatrocyate - sarveṣāṃ kāraṇabhūtena paramācāryeṇa vikhanasā praṇīte śrīvaikhānasasūtre "atha gṛhītagarbhāliṅgāni śarīrāṭopaḥ sakthisīdanaṃ dveṣo bharturarucirāhāre lālāprakopaḥ kharatā vācaḥ sphuraṇaṃ yoneriti garbhasya daivānubandhaṃ jñātvā āpūryamāṇapakṣe puṇye punnāmni śubhe nakṣatre" ityādinā garbhādhānalakṣaṇakālayorapi vaikhānasasūtre pratipāditvāt:
[83.21]     "vede vaikhānase sūtre yo dharmaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
[83.22]     sarvaiḥ sa dharmo'nuṣṭheyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||"
[83.23]     iti bhṛguṇoktatvāt"anuktamanyato grāhyam" iti nyāyātvikhanasaḥ ācāryatvena
[83.24-25]     bhāratādiṣu pratipāditavyācca "anuktāṃśeṣu vaikhānasoktaprakāreṇa asmābhirapi kartuṃ yuktam" iti. etadeva yuktataram. ṭathāhi:



[niṣekagarbhādhānayoraparyāyatvam]

[84.1-10]     āpastambādīnāmācāryeṇa bodhāyanena svaśiṣyān prati "athātra pākayajñānāṃ prāyaścittir" ityārabhya "vyāhṛtīnāṃ prayoge ayathākṛtaṃ yathākṛtaṃ bhavatītyācāryā bruvate. tatrāpyudāharanti" iti, "caturthīprabhṛtyā ṣoḍaśīmuttarāmuttarāmupaiti. prajāniḥśreyasamṛtugamanamityācāryāḥ. sarvāṇyupagamanāni mantravanti bhavantīti bodhāyanaḥ. yaccādau yaccartāviti śālīkir" iti - "athāsyātithayo bhavanti gurossamānavṛttirvaikhānasaḥ snātako rājā vā dharmayuktaḥ. teṣāmabhyutthāyāsanaṃ pādyamarhaṇamarghyaṃ vā prajuñjīta" iti - "ekamagnihotraṃ sāyamupakramaṃ prātarapavargamācāryā bruvate tatrodāharanti" iti, "vaikhānasānāṃ na druhyet" ityādi sūtreṣu "ācāryā bruvate" iti nirdiṣṭaḥ ācāryaḥ vaikhānasa eveti vaktuṃ yuktam - manvādīnāṃ śārīrasaṃskāravaktṛtvābhāvāt, bodhāyanena pratipāditasya vaikhānasasūtre vidyamānatvāt,
[84.11]     "guroḥ samānavṛttirvaikhānasaḥ" iti bodhāyanenaivoktatvāt, "vikhanasācārye"ti.
[84.12-13]     bhārate pratipāditvācca. tathā ca sarveṣāṃ kāraṇabhūtena vikhanasā praṇītatvāttatsūtroktaprakāreṇa sarvaiḥ kartuṃ yuktameva. kiñca araṇyādipātralakṣaṇamapi tatraiva pratipāditam.
[84.14]     anye'pyāhuḥ. "asmākamapi niṣekāditvaṃ samānam" iti. tadapyasat.
[84.15]     garbhādhānaśabdasyārthe vicāryamāṇe "yoniṃ praviśadindriyaṃ garbho jarāyuṇā vṛta" iti
[84.16-20]     pramāṇena garbhagole praviṣṭasya retasa eva garbhaśabdavācyatvamavagamyate. na tvitarasya kevala retasaḥ. tathā - "āsiñcatu prajāpatirdhātā garbhaṃ dadhātu" iti niṣekakriyānantarabhāvitvātgarbhādhānasya, niṣekasya tu pratyārtavaṃ kartuṃ vyogyatāsambhavātadhṛtaretasaḥ srāvadoṣaprasaṅgātśrutismṛtivirodhātpratyakṣavirodhāṅca niṣeka eva garbhādhānamiti vaktumayuktam.

[niṣekaprathamakasaṃskāravatāṃ śraiṣṭhyam]

[84.21]     atha niṣekāditve ko viśeṣa iti cet- ucyate. niṣekādikriyāvatāmeva
[84.22]     śrotriyatvaṃ dharmaśāstrādhikāritvaṃ brahmaśarīritvañca bodhāyanādibhiruktam. yathā:
[84.23]     "niṣeke garbhasaṃskāre jātakarmakriyāsu ca |
[84.24]     vidhivatsaṃskṛtā mantraiḥ cīrṇā vratasamāpanāt ||
[84.25]     śrotriyā iti vijñeyāḥ śākhāpārāśca ye dvijāḥ |
[84.26]     vidhivatgṛhyate pāṇimṛtau cīrṇavratāstu te ||




[85.1]     mantravatsamprayogena brāhmaṇyāṃ garbhamādadhuḥ |
[85.2]     niṣekādyāḥ śmaśānāntāḥ satkriyā brāhmaṇāśritāḥ ||" iti
[85.3]     yājñavalkya: "niṣekādyāḥ śmaśānāntāḥ teṣāṃ vai mantrataḥ kriyāḥ | [ẏājñavalkyaḍhś1.10 d]
[85.4]     garbhādhānamṛtau puṃsassavanaṃ spandanātpurā ||" iti  [ẏājñavalkyaḍhś1.11 b]
[85.5]     manu: "niṣekādiśmaśānāntāḥ mantrairyasyoditāḥ kriyāḥ |
[85.6]     tasya śāstre'dhikāro'smin jñeyo nānyasya kasyacit ||" iti [ṃnuḍhś2.16]
[85.7-8]     atra manuvākye "yasya - tasya - kasyacit" ityādi śabdānāṃ vaiyarthyaprasaṅgāt"sarveṣāṃ niṣekāditvaṃ samānam" iti vaktumayuktam.
[85.9]     āśvamedhike bhagavān: "vaidikaiḥ karmabhiḥ puṇyaiḥ niṣekādirdvijanmanām |
[85.10]     kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃskāraḥ pāvanaḥ pretya ceha ca   || [ṃB.h14, āpp. 4.2513]
[85.11]     gārbhairhomairjātakarmanāmacaulopanāyanaiḥ |  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2514]
[85.12]     svādhyāyaiśca tapobhiśca vivāhasnātakavrataiḥ ||  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2515]
[85.13]     mahāyajñaiśca yajñaiśca brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ |" iti [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2516]
[85.14]     evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyam.
[85.15]     bodhāyanena "yato etaddhutam" ityādinā sūtrādau vivāhapūrvakatvenoktam.
[85.16-21]     āpastambena "atha karmāṇyācārādyāni gṛhyante" ityārabhya "upanayanaṃ vyākhyāsyāma" iti. bhāradvājasatyāṣāḍhābhyāmāśvalāyanena ca "uktāni vaitānikānī"tyārabhya vaiśvadevapūrvakatvena. drāhyāyaṇena "athāto gṛhyakarmāṇi, udagayanapūrvapakṣa" ityārabhya ācārāditvena. kātyāyanaśca "athāto gṛhyasthālīpākānāṃ karme"tyārabhya pratipāditavān. "atha niṣekādisaṃskārān vyākhyāsyāma" ityārabhya niṣekapūrvakatvena bhagavatā vikhanasaiva pratipāditam.
[85.22-24]     evaṃ ca niṣekapūrvakatvenoktatvādasya sarvasūtrottamatvametatsūtroktadharmānuṣṭhānenaiva tatsūtriṇāṃ sarvotkṛṣṭatvañcopapadyate iti śrutismṛtipurāṇādimukhena pratipāditamiti nirūpitaḥ pañcamo hetuḥ.
[85.25]     iti pañcamahetunirūpaṇam




[ṣaṣṭhahetunirūpaṇam]

[vaikhānasasūtrasyāṣṭādaśasaṃskārapratipādakatvam]

[86.1]     atha ṣaṣṭhahetunirūpaṇam
[86.2-4]     athedānīṃ śrīvaikhānasasūtroktadharmānuṣṭhātṝṇāmeva sarvebhyaḥ śreṣṭhamatvaṃ sūtrāntareṣvanuktaṃ śrutisiddhāṣṭādaśasaṃskāravatvaṃ kriyābāhulyañca sambhavatīti jñāpayituṃ "aṣṭādaśasaṃskārapratipādakatvād" iti ṣaṣṭho heturnirūpyate. tathā hi:
[86.5-8]     "atha niṣekādisaṃskārān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ṛtusaṅgamana-garbādhāna-puṃsavana-sīmanta-viṣṇubali-jātakarmotthāna-nāmakaraṇānnaprāśana-pravāsāgamana-piṇḍavardhana-caulopanayana-pārāyaṇa-vratabandhavisargopākarma-samāvartana-pāṇigrahaṇānītyaṣṭādaśa-saṃskārāśśārīrāḥ" iti aṣṭādaśasaṃskārāḥ sūtre pratipāditāḥ. ete ca

["aṣṭādaśoktamavaraṃ yeṣu karme" ti śrutyarthavicāraḥ]

[86.9-10]     śrutisiddhāḥ. yathā muṇḍopaniṣadi - "plavā hyete adṛḍhā yajñarūpā aṣṭādaśoktamavaraṃ yeṣu karma. etacchreyo ye'bhinandanti mūḍhā jarāmṛtyuṃ te punarevāpi yanti" iti.
[86.11]     atra kecidevamūcuḥ: "yajñasya rtvijaṣṣoḍaśa sadasyassaptadaśaḥ yajamāno'ṣṭādaśaḥ"
[86.12-14]     iti śrutyanusāreṇa "aṣṭādaśoktamavaraṃ yeṣu karme"tyatra yajamānādaya eva vivakṣitā iti. tadasat. "aṣṭādaśoktamavaraṃ karmayeṣu" ityuktatvāt. ṛtvigyajamānādayo hi yāgakartāraḥ na tu karma. "yeṣu" ityasya padasya yeṣu sūtreṣvityarthaḥ.
[86.15-18]    'avaraṃ karma' jātakādayaḥ, smārtakarmatvāt. ātmaguṇarahitā yāgadayaḥ jātakarmādayaśca.'adṛḍhāḥ plavāḥ' saṃsārottārakā na bhavanti. tathā ca atra adṛḍhaplavatvena ninditā saṃskārāḥ yāgādayaśca ātmaguṇarahitā eva vivakṣitāḥ. tatsahitānāṃ teṣāṃ mokṣasādhakatvam. yathā:

[ātmaguṇarahitānāṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ bandhakatvam]

[86.19-21]     gautamadharme: "yasya tu khalu catvāriṃśatsaṃskārā na cāṣṭāvātmaguṇāḥ na sa brahmaṇassāyujyaṃ sālokyañca gacchati. yasya khalu catvāriṃśatsaṃskārāṇāmekadeśo pyaṣṭāvātmaṇāḥ atha sa brahmaṇassāyujyaṃ sālokyañca gacchati gacchatī"ti.

[aṣṭānāmātmaguṇānāṃ lakṣaṇāni]

[86.22-23]    'aṣṭāvātmaguṇāḥ': "dayā samastabhūteṣu kṣāntiranasūyā śaucamanāyāso maṅgalyamakārpaṇyamaspṛhe" ti. tallakṣaṇāni tu:

[tatra dayālakṣaṇaṃ]

[86.24]     "abandhau bandhuvarge vā mitre dveṣṭari vā tathā |
[86.25]     āpanne rakṣaṇaṃ yattu dayaiṣā parikīrtitā ||




[tatra kṣamālakṣaṇam]

[87.1]     bāhye vā'bhyantare vāpi duḥkha utpādite paraiḥ |
[87.2]     na prakupyati no hanti sā kṣamā parikīrtitā ||

[tatra anasūyālakṣaṇam]

[87.3]     saguṇān guṇi no hanti (?) stauti mandaguṇānapi |
[87.4]     nānyadoṣeṣu ramate sā'nasūyā prakīrtitā ||

[tatra śaucalakṣaṇam]

[87.5]     abhakṣyaparihāraśca saṃsargaścāpyaninditaiḥ |
[87.6]     svadharme ca vyavasthānaṃ śaucametadudāhṛtam ||

[tatra anāyāsalakṣaṇam]

[87.7]     śarīraṃ pīḍyate yena suśubhenāpi karmaṇā |
[87.8]     atyantaṃ tannakartavyamanāyāsassa ucyate ||

[tatra maṅgalyalakṣaṇam]

[87.9]     praśastācaraṇaṃ nityamapraśastavisarjanam |
[87.10]     etaddhi maṅgalaṃ proktamṛṣibhistattvadarśibhiḥ ||

[tatra akārpaṇyalakṣaṇam]

[87.11]     stokādapi pradātavyamadīnenāntarātmanā |
[87.12]     ahanyahani yatkiñcidakārpaṇyaṃ taducyate ||

[tatra aspṛhālakṣaṇam]

[87.13]     yathotpanneṣu santoṣaḥ kartavyassvāgatairdhanaiḥ |
[87.14]     parārthānnābhibhāṣeta sā'spṛhā parikīrtitā ||" iti
[87.15]     prasaṅgādidamuktam. prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ.

[sāttvikarājasatāmasapurāṇāni]

[87.16-17]     atha aṣṭādaśasmṛtyuktāni aṣṭādaśapurāṇeṣūktānīti vā muṇḍakaśrutyartho'stu iti cet. tadapyasat. smṛtipurāṇādiṣu aṣṭādaśakarmeti viśiṣyānuktatvāt.
[87.18-20]     atra  "aṣṭādaśoktamavaraṃ karma yeṣv" iti sāmānyenoktatvāttatroktānāṃ yogādīnāmapyapavargaprāpakatvābhāvātpunarapi jarāmṛtyuprāpakatvācca na smṛtipurāṇeṣūktānīti vaktuṃ yuktaṃ kiñca
[87.21]     pādmapurāṇe: "vaiṣṇavaḥ nāradīyañca tathā bhāgavataṃ śubham |
[87.22]     gāruḍañca tathā pādmaṃ vārāhaṃ śubhadarśane   || [ড়dma.ড় uttarabhāga 236.19]




[sātvikapurāṇaśraiṣṭhyam]

[88.1]     sāttvikāni purāṇāni vijñeyāni ca ṣaṭpṛthak |
[88.2]     brahmāṇḍaṃ brahmakaivartaṃ mārkaṇḍeyaṃ tathaiva ca   || [ড়dma.ড় uttarabhāga 236.20]
[88.3]     bhaviṣyadvāmanaṃ brāhmaṃ rājasāni ca ṣaḍvidhāḥ | [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.21 b]
[88.4]     mātsyaṃ kaurmaṃ tathā laiṅgaṃ śaivaṃ skāndaṃ tathaiva ca |
[88.5]     āgneyañca ṣaḍetāni tāmasāni nibodha me ||" iti [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.18]
[88.6]     mātsye: "saṅkīrṇāstāmasāścaiva rājasāssāttvikāstathā |
[88.7]     kalpāścaturvidhāḥ proktāḥ brahmaṇo divasāśca te |
[88.8]     yasmin kalpe tu yatproktaṃ purāṇaṃ brahmaṇā purā ||  [ṃtsyaড়]
[88.9]     tasya tasya tu māhātmyaṃ tena rūpeṇa vartate |
[88.10]     saṅkīrṇeṣu sarasvatyāḥ pitṝṇāñca nigadyate ||  [ṃtsyaড়]
[88.11]     agneśśivasya māhātmyaṃ tāmaseṣu prakīrtyate |
[88.12]     rājaseṣu tu māhātmyamadhikaṃ brahmaṇo viduḥ || [ṃtsyaড়]
[88.13]     sāttvikeṣvatha kalpeṣu māhātmyamadhikaṃ hareḥ |
[88.14]     teṣveva yogasaṃsiddhā gamiṣyanti parāṃ gatim ||" iti  [ṃtsyaড়]
[88.15]     pādme: "vāsiṣṭhañcaiva hārītaṃ vyāsaṃ pārāśaraṃ tathā ||  [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.23 d]
[88.16]     bhāradvājaṃ kāśyapīyaṃ smṛtiṣaṭkantu sāttvikam |
[88.17]     mānavaṃ yajñavalkyañca ātreyaṃ dākṣyameva ca || [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.24]
[88.18]     kātyāyanaṃ vaiṣṇavañca smṛtiṣaṭkantu rājasam |
[88.19]     gautamaṃ bārhaspatyañca sāṃvartañca yamasmṛtiḥ || [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.25]
[88.20]     śāmbhamauśanasañcaiva smṛtiṣaṭkantu tāmasam ||" iti [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.26 b]
[88.21]     tathā: "sāttvikā mokṣadā jñeyā rājasāḥ svargadāḥ smṛtāḥ | [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.21 d]
[88.22]     tathaiva tāmasā devi nirayaprāptihetavaḥ ||"  [ড়dmaড় uttarabhāga 236.22 b]
[88.23-24]     ityādi vacanasiddhasātvikapurāṇeṣu pratipāditānāṃ bhagavadārādhanādīnāṃ mokṣahetutvasmaraṇāt, sāmanyena aṣṭādaśapurāṇoktakarmaṇāṃ jīrṇaplavasādṛśyaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyam.





[vaikhānase kriyābāhulyam]

[89.1-2]     anyathā svargamokṣanirayāṇāmekarūpatvāṅgīkārāpātāt. ataḥ śrīmadvaikhānasasūtroktāṣṭādaśasaṃskārā evetyavagantavyam. bodhāyanasūtre saṃskārasaṅkhyā na kṛtā.
[89.3-5]     āpastambādināpi pañcamahāyajñānāṃ nityahomasya ca pākayajñatvenāṅgīkārātete ṣoḍasaṃskārā iti vivicyānuktatvātaparasaṃskārapratipādanābhāvācca na tatroktāḥ gṛhītuṃ śakyāḥ.
[89.6-8]     śrīvaikhānase tu kriyābāhulyamapyasti. tathā hi. aṣṭādaśasaṃskārāḥ pākayajñāśca pṛthaktvenopapaditāḥ. varṣavardhanādyāstu viśeṣāḥ. pañcamahāyajñasahitā dvāviṃśadyajñāḥ - pañcamahāyajñānāmekatvam. aparaścāṣṭādaśavyatirikta iti.
[89.9]     kriyābāhulyena kimādhikyamiti cet- ucyate. muṇḍopaniṣadi -
[89.10]     "kriyāvāneṣa brahmavidāṃ variṣṭhaḥ" iti "kriyāvantaḥ śrotriyā brahmaṇiṣṭhāḥ svayaṃ juhvata
[89.11]     ekarṣiṃ śraddhayantaḥ. teṣāmevaitāṃ brahmavidyāṃ vadeta" iti ca śrūyate.
[89.12-15]     bodhāyanaḥ: "tasmādyaḥ kaścana kriyāvān satāmanumatācārassa śrotriyassa eva vijñeyassa eva vijñeyaḥ" iti. kiñca - karmabhūyastvātphalabhūyastvañca "yathopāsanaṃ phalam" iti nyāyātsiddhyati. ataḥ: aṣṭādaśasaṃskārāpratipādakatvādasya sūtrasya śreṣṭḥtvametassūtriṇāṃ sarvotkṛṣṭatvañcopapadyata iti nirūpitaḥ ṣaṣṭho hetuḥ.
[89.16]     iti ṣaṣṭhahetunirūpaṇam.





[saptamahetunirūpaṇam]

[90.1-3]     atha śrīvaikhānasasūtrasya itarasūtrāpekṣābhāvātsvātantryamastīti jñāpayituṃ sāṅgakriyākalāpavattvāditi saptamo heturnirūpyate. tathā hi: atrasūtre aṣṭādaśasaṃskārāḥ śārīrāḥ prasiddhāssāṅgāḥ eva pratipāditāḥ.
[90.4-8]     nanu - aṅkurārpaṇaśatābhiṣekāgnidvayasaṃsargaputraparigrahabrahmamedhavṛṣotsarjanādipratipādanābhavātsāṅgakriyākalāpavattvaṃ nāstīti cet- tadasat. iyaṃ śaṅkā śrīvaikhānasasūtrasvarūpānabhijñatvakṛtā. idaṃ sūtramekādaśapraśnātmakam. atra na pratipāditānīti uktiḥ, atiriktatvena gṛhyapariśiṣṭasadbhāvājñānāt. gṛhyapariśiṣṭasadbhāvaḥ kathamavagamyate iti cet- ucyate.
[90.9]     "athāṅkurārpaṇavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ" ityārabhya khaṇḍadvayenāṅkurārpaṇamuktam.
[90.10-11]     "sāyaṃ sthaṇḍile sahasraṃ piṣṭena somarūpāṇi karotī"tyatra viśeṣeṇa sthaṇḍilādipratipādanam.
[90.12]     "śatābhiṣekaṃ pūrvāhṇe puṇyāhaṃ dvijabhojanam ||" ityārabhya  [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[90.13]     "sāyaṃ gomayasaṃlipte sthaṇḍile prāganunnate |
[90.14]     prācīrekhā dvipañcāśatsaṃkhyayā tatra saṃlikhet  || [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ.]
[90.15]     udīcīśca tathā rekhā dvatriṃśatsaṃkhyayā likhet ||"  [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[90.16]     ityādinā śatābhiṣekaprayogaśca uktaḥ.
[90.17]     tathā "dhautavastradhāriṇau syātām" ityārabhya [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[90.18]     "dīpādyalaṅkṛte gehe vrīhīnāstīrya tatra vai |
[90.19]     carmābhāve kaṭaṃ vāpi tadūrdhve vastramāstared ||" iti  [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[90.20]     "śeṣahome'ntahomānte āsīnau tatra carmaṇi |
[90.21]     visṛjāmi vratañceti tadvrataṃ carma santyajet ||" [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[90.22]     ityādinā agnidvayasaṃsargaprakāraśca pratipāditaḥ.





[91.1]     putraparigrahaprakāraśca
[91.2]     "tyāgavikrayadāneṣu prokteṣvapi tatassmṛtau |
[91.3]     ekaṃ na dadyānno gṛhyātna strī bhartrananujñayā ||" ityādinā [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[91.4]     brahmamedhavidhistu
[91.5]     "saṃskāro brahmaniṣṭhānāṃ sadācāravatāmapi |
[91.6]     brahmamedho'tiphalado na sarveṣāṃ dvijanmanām ||" ityādinā  [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[91.7]     vṛṣotsargavidhistu
[91.8]     "vṛṣotsargavidhiṃ vakṣye yena pretatvamocanam |
[91.9]     snātvātha vāstuhomānte pretatvasya vimuktaye ||" ityādibhiḥ  [ṅṛhyapariśiṣṭaṣ]
[91.10]     evamatrānuktāni sarvāṇi gṛhyapariśiṣṭe proktāni iti veditavyam.
[91.11-15]     bodhāyanādibhirapyete sāṅgā eva pratipāditā iti cet- na. "sati kuḍye cikrakarme" ti nyāyena pradhānakarmāṇi pratipāditāni cetaṅgavicāraḥ kartavyaḥ. sandhyopāsane arghyapradānādikamanuktam. vānaprasthāśramasvīkaraṇādikamapi na pratipāditam. vānaprasthāśramasvīkaraṇāsvīkaraṇāraṇyāharaṇapātralakṣaṇādiṣu vaikhānasasūtrāpekṣā astyeva. devatāpratiṣṭhādikamuktaṃ lakṣaṇamanuktam.
[91.16-18]     āpastambādibhiḥ sandhyopāsanāṅkurapratisaradevatārādhanayajñopavītadhāraṇādikamapyanuktam. āpastambādīnāmanyonyāpekṣā prasiddhā. anyonyāpekṣā nāma asvātantryamaṅgahīnavat.
[91.19]     manu: "yadyatparavaśaṃ karma tattadyatnena varjayet ||  [ṃnuḍhś4.159 d]
[91.20]     sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkhaṃ sarvamātmavaśaṃ sukham |
[91.21]     etadvidyātsamāsena lakṣaṇaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ ||" [ṃnuḍhś4.160]
[91.22-23]     anena anantasthiraphalaprāptirdarśitā. ataḥ sūtrāntaranairapekṣyātsarvasūtrottamatvaṃ svātantryañca ghaṭata iti nirūpitassaptamo hetuḥ.
[91.24]     iti saptamahetunirūpaṇam





[atha aṣṭamahetunirūpaṇam]

[vaikhānasasūtrasya akhilaśiṣṭaparigṛhītatvam]

[92.1-3]     idānīṃ tāvatśrutismṛtipurāṇādibhiḥ sarvottaratvenāvagatasya śrīvaikhānasasūtrasya akhilaśiṣṭaparigṛhītatvamastīti darśayituṃ manvādyaiḥ svīkṛtatvāditi aṣṭamo hetuḥ nirūpyate. tathā hi
[92.5]     manu: "ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi dharmaṃ vaikhānasāśramam ||" ityārabhya
[92.6]     "puṣpamūlaphalairvāpi kevalairvartayetsadā |
[92.7]     kālapakvaiḥ svayaṃ śīrṇairvaikhānasamate sthitaḥ ||" iti  [ṃnuḍhś6.21]
[92.8]     vyāsa: "mūlaireke phalaireke puṣpaireke dṛḍhavratāḥ | [ṃBh12.236.13 d]
[92.9]     vartayanti yathānyāyaṃ vaikhānasamate sthitāḥ ||" iti [ṃBh12.236.14 b]
[92.10]     gautama: śrāmaṇakenāgnimādhāyāgrāmyabhojī" iti
[92.11]     yama: śrāmaṇakena vidhānena sāyaṃ prātarupaspṛśet. iti
[92.12-13]     śaṅkhalikhitau: śrāmaṇakenāgnimādhāya kāle kuruvinda - veṇu - śyāmākanīvārādibhirvartayet. iti.
[92.14-16]     hārīta: vānaprastho grāmyā oṣadhī parivarjayet. tretāgnīnāṃ sthāne śrāmaṇakaṃ vāgnimādhāya valkalaśāṇacarmakuśamuñjavidalakaṃ vā vasāno vaikhānasaproktena vidhinā vānaprastho vikhanā bhavati. anujñāyiko'nuprasthāyikaś" ceti
[92.17]     vasiṣṭha: śrāmaṇakenāgnimādhāyāhitāgnirvṛkṣamūlikaḥ. iti  [Vāsiṣṭhaḍhś9.10]
[92.18]     bodhāyana: "vānaprastho vaikhānasaśāstrasamudācāraḥ"  [ḍhṣ12.6.11.14]
[92.19-21]     āpastamba: "ekāgniraniketaḥ syād" iti. asya dharmakarpardī "gārhapatyādīnāṃ tretāgnīnāṃ sthāne yasyaiko'gniḥ aupāsana eva - vaikhānasasūtre dṛṣṭatvāt, pāṇigrahaṇanimitvadvādaupāsanasya.
[92.22-23]     jaiminīyasūtre saumikacāturmāsyaprakāraṇe. "bhāradvājapūrvaṃ yathā vaikhānasapūrvaṃ pañcaḥ" meti (?)





[akhilaśiṣṭaparigrahasya prāmāṇyātiśayahetutvam]

[93.1-2]     atrobhayatra ātrivyākhyānam - ṣoḍaśartvijāṃ madhye bhāradvājasūtriṇaṃ prastotāraṃ vaikhānasasūtriṇaṃ pratihartāraṃ varayet" iti.
[93.3]     kapiñjalasaṃhitāyām:
[93.4]     "yājanādhyāpanayutaḥ śiṣyānugrahakārakaḥ |
[93.5]     ācārya iti vijñeyo mantratantraparāyaṇaḥ || [Kapiñjalaṣ]
[93.6]     āśrame vā vane vā'pi putradārasamāhitaḥ |
[93.7]     aṣṭākṣaraparo nityaṃ sa vaikhānasa ucyate || [Kapiñjalaṣ]
[93.8]     grāmyadharmāṇi sarvāṇi varjayedvanavāsakaḥ |
[93.9]     valkalājinasaṃvītaḥ sa maunītyabhidhīyate ||" iti [Kapiñjalaṣ]
[93.10]     pādme dvādaśākṣaraprakāre
[93.11]     "..... putradārasamanvitaḥ |
[93.12]     vane vā pattane vāpi japedvaikhānasaḥ śuciḥ ||
[93.13]     viyuktaḥ putradārādyaiḥ svayameva vane sthitaḥ ||" [ড়dmaড় ]
[93.14]     anyairapi evamevoktam.
[93.15-16]     akhilaśiṣṭaparigrahe ko viśeṣa iti cet- akhilaśiṣṭaparigrahātprāmāṇyātiśayo'stītyavagamyate.
[93.17]     āśvamedhike: "dharmaṃ jijñāsamānānāṃ pramāṇaṃ prathamaṃ śrutiḥ | [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2492]
[93.18]     dvitīyaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ hi tṛtīyaṃ lokasaṅgrahaḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh14, āpp. 4.2493]
[93.19]     manu: "vidvadbhissevitaṃ samyaknityamadveṣarāgibhiḥ |
[93.20]     hṛdayenābhyanujñāto yo dharmastaṃ nibodhata ||" iti [ṃnuḍhś2.1]
[93.21]     yājñavalkya: "catvāro vedadharmajñāḥ parṣattraividyameva vā |
[93.22]     sā brūte'yaṃ sa dharmaḥ syādeko vādhyātmavittamaḥ ||  [ẏājñavalkyaḍhś1.9]
[93.23]     vedāḥ pramāṇaṃ smṛtayaḥ pramāṇaṃ dharmārthayuktaṃ vacanaṃ pramāṇam |
[93.24]     yasya pramāṇaṃ na bhavetpramāṇaṃ kastasya kuryādvacanaṃ pramāṇam ||





[94.1]     na yasya vedā na ca dharmaśāstraṃ
[94.2]     na vṛddhavākyaṃ ca bhavetpramāṇam |
[94.3]     so'dharmakṛdduṣṭahato durātmā
[94.4]     svātmāpi tasyaiva bhavetpramāṇam (?) ||"  [ẏājñavalkyaḍhś]

[smṛtervedamūlatve tredhā nirvāhaḥ]

[94.5-9]     smṛterapi vedamūlatve tredhā nirvāhaḥ kṛtaḥ. nityānumeyaśrutimūlatvaṃ prābhākarāḥ. "teṣāmutsannāḥ pāṭhāḥ prayogādanumīyanta" ityutsannaśākhāmūlatvamāpastambādyāḥ. tatprābhākarādhibhiḥ dūṣitaṃ bhāṣyakārānabhimatañca. prābhākarapakṣe'pi nityānumeyaśrutimūlatve akṣaraśo vā ānupūrvīviśeṣaviśiṣṭatvena vā. akṣaraśa iti yadyucyate tarhi kaṭatapayādīnāmapi nityānumeyaśrutimūlatvātsmṛtitvaṃ syāt.
[94.10-15]     ānupūrvīviśeṣaviśiṣṭatveneti cetnityānumeyatvaṃ bhajyetiti. utsannaśākhāmūlatvamiti pakṣe śākhāsu tatratatrotsannā vā utaikā śākhā utsannā vā. tatratatreti cetna - padakramādirūpeṇādhīyamānatvādutsannaśākhāmūlatvaṃ na ghaṭate. ekaivotsanneti cet- na. viṣṇoraṃśāvatārasya tapoyogabalopetasya sarvavedasākṣātkāravato vyāsasya ajñātāśāsaṃbhavena ekasyāpi śiṣyasya tadadhyāpanasāmarthyasambhavāttathā vaktumayuktatvāditi. ato viprakīrṇaśākhāmūlatvaṃ vaktavyam. tathoktam.
[94.16]     "durbodhā vaidikāśśabdāḥ prakīrṇatvācca ye khilāḥ |
[94.17]     tadjñaista eva sṣaṣṭārthāḥ smṛtitantre pratiṣṭhitāḥ ||" iti
[94.18-20]     ataḥ śrutismṛtipurāṇādibhiḥ prāmāṇatvenāvagatasya śrīvaikhānasasūtrasya akhilaśiṣṭaparigrahātprāmāṇyātiśayo'stīti "manvādyaissvīkṛtatvād" iti nirūpito'ṣṭamo hetuḥ.
[94.21]     ityaṣṭamahetunirūpaṇam






[navamahetunirūpaṇam]

[vaikhānasasūtriṇāṃ nārāyaṇaikaparatvam]
[95.1-3]     athedānīmāpastabādisūtrāntaraniṣṭhānāṃ sūtrakārādārabhya avicchinnatvena paramparayā ekāntitvaṃ nāstīti jñāpayituṃ vaikhānasasūtriṇāmeva tatsambhavatīti darśayituṃ.
[95.4]     "pratyakṣamanumānañca śāstrañca vividhāgamam |
[95.5]     trayaṃ suviditaṃ jñeyaṃ dharmasiddhimabhīpsatā ||" [ṃnuḍhś12.105]
[95.6-7]     iti manusmaraṇānusāreṇa pratyakṣādibhiravagamayituñca akhilajagadekakāraṇabhūtaśrīmannārāyaṇaikaparatvāditi navamo heturnirūpyate.
[95.8]     asya sūtrasya nārāyaṇaikaparatve:

[tatra ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgāni]

[95.9]     "upakramopasaṃhārāvabhyāso'pūrvatā phalam |
[95.10]     arthavādopapattī ca liṅgaṃ tātparyanirṇaye ||"

[nārāyaṇasyaiva parabrahmaśabdavācyatvam]
