[95.11-17]     ityuktāni ṣaḍvidhāni tātparyaliṅgāni santi. tathā hi ādyapraśne prathamakhāṇḍe "nārāyaṇaparāyaṇa" ityuktam. evaṃ "sadādhyātmarato dhyānayogī nārāyaṇaparaṃ brahma paśyan dhāraṇaṃ dhārayedakṣaraṃ paraṃ brahmāpnoti - "nārāyaṇaparaṃ brahmeti śrutiḥ" ityupasaṃhāre sannyāsavidhau pratipāditam. sandhyopāsanādi - puṇyāhāghārāntahoma - nāndīmukhopanayana - pāṇigrahaṇaparyanteṣu antakarmaṇi ca ato devādivaiṣṇavā mantrāḥ viniyuktāḥ. evamāghāraprakaraṇe "śaro'ṅgāre'gnau juhuyād" iti sūtreṇa idhmādhānaṃ taittirīyabrāhmaṇavākyānusāreṇa parabrahmāgnau vihitam.






[brahmarudrādīnāṃ yajñāṃśabhāktvaṃ bhagavatprasādalabdham]

[95.18-96.5]      parabrahmaśabdābhidheyaśca bhagavānnārāyaṇa eveti vedānteṣu nirṇītam. tathāhi: "sadeva somyedamagra āsīt, ekamevādvitīyam" ityādibhiḥ pratipannasya abhinnanimittopādānakāraṇasya "yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante - tadbrahme"tyādinā jagatkāraṇatvaṃ brahmaśabdavācyatvañca pratipādyate. tasya "satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahme" tyādinā śodhakavākyena svarūpaṃ nirūpyate.  satyatvādiviśiṣṭasya tasya "ānando brahma" iti niratiśayānandarūpatvaṃ pratipādyate. etādṛśabrahmarūpaṃ vastu "nārāyaṇaparaṃ brahma" "tattvaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ" "eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt" "apahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ" ityādi vākyaiḥ śrīmannārāyaṇa eveti niyamyate. evañca "adhyūhante" iti taittirīyavākyaśeṣasthabrahmaśabdo'pi nārāyaṇapara eveti prakṛtasūtroktedhmādhānamapi nārāyaṇaparameva.
[96.6-9]     evamādhāne - "dhyāyannārāyaṇamupoṣya" iti, agnihotre - bhūmānaṃ viṣṇuṃ dhyāyet" iti, darśapūrṇamāse - "udita āditye dhyāyannārāyaṇaṃ'viṣṇornu kam' iti japitvā" iti, some ca - "nārāyaṇaparāyaṇastūṣṇīmāste" iti ca sarvatrāpi abhyāsena nārāyaṇaparatvenoktatvātidaṃ sūtraṃ nārāyaṇaparameva.
[96.10-14]     nanu - atra sūtre pravāsāgamane āśvayujyāñca "pradakṣiṇamarcanaṃ praṇāmo guhasya" - "bhavaṃ devamāvāhya" ityuktatvātsarvatra nārāyaṇaparatvamasyeti kathaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate iti cet- ucyate. "aṅgānyanyā devatā" iti aṅgabhūtānāṃ brahmarudrādīnāmapi yajñāṃśabhāktvaṃ bhagavatprasādalabdhamiti bhāratādināvagamyate - tathāhi
[96.15]     "tataste surāssarve brahmā te ca maharṣayaḥ || [ṃBh12.327.48 d]
[96.16]     vedadṛṣṭena vidhinā vaiṣṇavaṃ kratumārabhan |
[96.17]     tasmin satre tathā brahmā svayaṃ bhāgamakalpayat |
[96.18]     devā mahārṣayaścaiva sarve bhāgānakalpayan || [ṃBh12.327.49]
[96.19]     te kārtayugadharmāṇo bhāgāḥ paramasaṃskṛtāḥ |
[96.20]     prāpurādityavarṇaṃ taṃ puruṣaṃ tamasaḥ param || [ṃBh12.327.50]
[96.21]     śrībhagavān: yena yaḥ kalpito bhāgaḥ sa tathā samupāgataḥ |
[96.22]     prīto'haṃ pradiśāmyadya phalamāvṛttilakṣaṇam   || [ṃB.h12.327.52]
[96.23]     yajñīye cāpi yakṣyanti sarvalokeṣu vai surāḥ |
[96.24]     kalpayiṣyanti vo bhāgāṃste narā vedakalpitān || [ṃBh12.327.54]





[rudrīdīnāṃ nārāyaṇāṅgatayā pūjārhatvam]

[97.1]     yo me yathā kalpitavān bhāgamasminmahākratau |
[97.2]     sa mahāyajñabhāgārho vedasūtre mayā kṛtaḥ ||" iti [ṃBh12.327.55]
[97.3]     śrībhāgavate: "ahaṃ bhavo dakṣabhṛgupradhānāḥ prajeśabhūteśasureśamukhyāḥ |
[97.4]     sarve vayaṃ yanniyamaṃ prapannā mūrdhnārpitaṃ lokadhuraṃ vahāmaḥ ||"
[97.5]     evaṃ ca bhavādīnāmapi nārāyaṇāṅgatvena pūjārhatvāvagamāt.
[97.6]     "aṅgabhāvena devānāmarcanaṃ na niṣiddhyate |
[97.7]     svatantrabuddhyā kurvan hi brāhmaṇo narakaṃ vrajet ||
[97.8]     brahmāṇamindramagniṃ vā harerāvaraṇaṃ vinā |
[97.9]     yastu sampūjayedvipraḥ sa pāṣaṇḍī bhaveddhruvam ||"
[97.10]     ityādinā aṅgabhāvena teṣāmarcanaṃ kurvato doṣābhāvasmaraṇācca sūtre tathoktiḥ.
[97.11]     tathā: "anyatra tvāṃ samāropya yadyupāsā vidhīṃyate |
[97.12]     sā vṛttiḥ pracyutā proktā tava tattvaṃ tathaiva ca   || [Kñ.K ]
[97.13]     tvayi sarvaṃ samāropya yadyupāsā vidhīyate |
[97.14]     sā vṛttiḥ sakalā jñeyā tava tattvaṃ tavaiva hi ||"  [KñK ]
[97.15-18]     iti jñānakāṇḍe kaśyapenopāsanasvarūpabhedapradarśanāt"viṣṇornityārcā sarvadevārcā bhavatī"ti mukhyatvena sūtreṇa pratipāditatvātpracyutiphalapradatvābhāvācca teṣāmaṅgabhāvena pūjanaṃ yujyate. āpastambādisūtreṣu sarvasaṃskārāṇāṃ sadvārakatvena pratipādanātnārāyaṇaikaparatvena sakṛdapi smaraṇābhavātteṣāṃ na nārāyaṇaikaparatvam.

[āpastambādisūtrāṇāṃ nārāyaṇaikaparatvābhāvaḥ]

[97.19-21]     nanu bodhāyanenāpi "athāto viṣṇupratiṣṭhākalpaṃ vyākhyasyāmo dvādaśyāmekādaśyāṃ śroṇāyāṃ vā yāni cānyāni śubhanakṣatrāṇi" ityārabhya "dvādaśanāmabhiḥ amuṣmai svāhā amuṣmai svāhā" ityādinā homādikamuktā.
[97.22]     "tvāmekamādyaṃ puruṣaṃ purātanaṃ
[97.23]     nārāyaṇaṃ viśvasṛjaṃ yajāmahe |
[97.24]     tvameva yajño vihito vighneyaḥ
[97.25]     tvamātmanātman pratigṛhṇīṣva havyam ||" [ṅṛśeṣṣ2.13.36]





[āpastambādīnāṃ nānādevatāparatvam]

[98.1-5]     iti nārāyaṇaikaparatvena pratipādanātkathaṃ teṣāṃ nārāyaṇaikaparatvaṃ nāstītyucyata iti cet- satyam. āpastambādīnāmācāryeṇānena bodhāyanena nārāyaṇaikaparatvena pratiṣṭhādikamanuktam. kintu. "atha rudrapratiṣṭhāvidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ. caturthyāmaṣṭamyāmārdrāyāmapabharaṇyāṃ vā yāni cānyāni śubhanakṣatrāṇi" iti prakṛtya "bhavāya devāya svāhe"tyādibhiḥ "bhavasya devasya patnyai svāhe"tyādibhiśca homādikamuktvā [ṅṛśeṣṣ2.16.1-2]
[98.6]     "tvāmekamādyaṃ puruṣaṃ purātanaṃ
[98.7]     rudraṃ śivaṃ viśvasṛjaṃ yajāmahe |
[98.8]     tvameva yajño vihito vidheyas
[98.9]     tvamātmanātman pratigṛhṇīṣva havyam ||" [ṅṛśeṣṣ2.16.39]
[98.10]     iti śivapratiṣṭhā, evaṃ "athāto mahādevasyāharahaḥ paricaryāvidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ"
[98.11-15]     ityārabhya "mahādevamāvāhayet- āyātu bhagavānmahādeva" iti pṛthaktvena rudrapūjā ca pratipāditā. tathā - anyatra "athāto'rdhamāse'rdhamāse'ṣṭamyāṃ brāhmaṇāḥ striyaścāhuḥ" ityārabhya "atha pradoṣe rudraṃ virūpākṣaṃ sapanīkaṃ sasutaṃ sapārṣadam" ityādibhiḥ rudrāvāhanamuktam. evaṃ "ādityamambikāṃ viṣṇuṃ gaṇanāthaṃ maheśvaram" iti pañcapūjā ca. ataḥ bodhāyanasūtraṃ nārāyaṇaikaparaṃ na bhavati.
[98.16-18]     atha āpastambādayaḥ kiṃ nārāyaṇaikaparaḥ - ata rudraparāḥ āhosvitnānādevatāparāḥ?. nārāyaṇaikaparā iti cenna. jātikulācārādiṣvāgotraprathamādārabhya:
[98.19]     "śvaśrūrasya digambaravratavatī sambandhinassaugatāḥ |
[98.20]     mātā cāsya pitā ca pāśupatinau vaiśeṣiko deśikaḥ |
[98.21]     bhrātā tiṣṭhati sāṅkhyayogasamaye cārvākaśiṣyassakhā
[98.22]     mithyācārasamāhvayassvayamasau viplāviko vaidikaḥ ||"



[98.23-99.4]     ityuktarītyā eteṣāṃ śaivapāśupatavaiṣṇavādyākariṇa vidyamānatvātāpastambādisūtrāṇyapi nārāyaṇaikaparāṇi na bhavanti. bhagavato nārāyaṇasya samārādhanavidhipratipādanābhāvācca. nārāyaṇaikaparañcedāpastambādisūtraṃ tarhi tatsūtroktadharmānuṣṭhātāraḥ sarve'pi nārāyaṇaikaparā eva syuḥ. na ca tathā dṛśyate. pāñcarātrādyuktamārgeṇa vaiṣṇavasaṃpādane "svasūtre vidyamāne tu" - "svasūtrasya parityāgāt" - "yassvadharmaṃ parityajya" "vidharmaḥ paradharmaśca" - śreyā svadharmo viguṇaḥ" - ityādi vacanapratipāditasvasūtraparityāgaparadharmasvīkāraśākhāraṇḍatvādidoṣāḥ prasajyeran.
[99.5-6]     "svaśāstranirṇaye satye satyāḥ syurlokanirṇayā" iti svasūtrasya prāmāṇyabuddhiśca teṣāmavarjanīyā.

[vaikhānasānāmavicchedena nārāyaṇaparatvam]

[99.7-9]     vaikhānasānāṃ sūtrakārādārabhya avicchinnatvena śrīmannārāyaṇaikaparatvenārādhanādikaṃ pratyakṣasiddham. tathā ca sūtram, "gṛhe devāyatane vā bhaktyā bhagavantaṃ nārāyaṇamarcayettadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ gacchatīti vijñāyate" iti.
[99.10-12]     itareṣvāpastambādiṣu pitṛ pitāmaha mātula mātāmaha putra pautra bhrātṛ sambandhiṣu kecicchaivāḥ kecidpāśupatāḥ kecicchāktāḥ nānādevatāparāścetyevamākāreṇa dṛśyate.
[99.13]     "janmāntarasahasreṣu tapojñānasamādhibhiḥ |
[99.14]     narāṇāṃ kṣīṇapāpānāṃ kṛṣṇe bhaktiḥ prajāyate ||"
[99.15-16]     iti keṣāñcitsvakṛtapūrvapuṇyaviśeṣānnārāyaṇaparatvasambhave'pi svasūtrakāramārabhya svaparyantamavicchinnatvena tadastīti vaktuṃ na śakyate. ataḥ -
[99.17]     "nānākāmahatajñānā nānādaivatayājinaḥ |
[99.18]     narā bhagavataikantye na sthāsyanti kalau yuge ||  [Vāyuড়]
[99.19]     āgamādanumānācca pratyakṣādupapattitaḥ |
[99.20]     parīkṣya nipuṇaṃ buddhyā śraddhātavyaṃ vipaścitā ||" [Vāyuড়]
[99.21-22]     iti vāyupurāṇādivacanādāgamādibhirnipuṇaṃ parīkṣya nirṇīta evārthaḥ vipaścidbhiḥ anuṣṭheyaḥ.

[nārāyaṇasya pāramyam]

[99.23-100.10] nanu - kimarthaṃ nārāyaṇaikaparatvena sūtraṃ praṇītamiti cet- ucyate. nārāyaṇa akhilajagadekakāraṇatvātparaṃbrahmaparamātmādiśabdavācyatvātapavargādiphalapradatvātsamābhyadhikarahitatvācca nārāyaṇaikaparatvena praṇītam. atra - "nārāyaṇādru jāyate. nārāyaṇādindro jāyate. nārāyaṇāddvādaśādityāḥ rudrā vasavassarvāṇi ca bhūtāni. nārāyaṇādeva samudpadyante. nārāyaṇātpralīyante" ityādyāḥ "nārāyaṇaparaṃ brahme"tyārabhya "antarbahiśca tatsarvaṃ vyāpya nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ" ityādyāḥ, "tameva vidvānamṛta iha bhavati. nānyaḥ panthā ayanāya vidyate" "utāmṛtatvasyeśānaḥ" ityādyāḥ, "na tasya kāryaṃ karaṇañca vidyate" "na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca dṛśyate" "parāsya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca" ityādyāḥ, "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ" ityādyāḥ, "eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīt" ityādyāḥ, "yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante. yena jātāni jīvanti. yatprayantyabhisaṃviśanti. tadvijijñāsasva. tadbrahme"ti "patiṃ viśvasyātmeśvaram" ityādyāśca śrutayaḥ pramāṇam.






[śvetāśvatarādeḥ rudrapāramyaparatvābhāvaḥ]

[100.11-23]     nanu - śvetāśvatare - "eko hi rudro na dvitīyāya tasthe - ya imān lokānīśata īśanībhiḥ - viśvādhiko rudro maharṣiḥ - hiraṇyagarbhaṃ janayāmāsa pūrvaṃ sa no buddhyā śubhayā saṃyunaktu - "umāsahāyaṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ triyambakaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ praśāntam. jñātvā munirgacchati bhūtayoniṃ samastasākṣiṃ tamasaḥ parastāt" ityādibhiḥ rudrasyāpi jagatkāraṇatvādi pratipādyata iti cet- ucyate. āpātato rudraparatvenāvagamyamāne'pi vicāryamāṇe nārāyaṇaparatvameva. eka eva rudra ityanupapannam. "sahasrāṇi sahasraśo ye rudrā" ityādi śrutibhiḥ "ekādaśarudrā" ityādibhiśca anekarudrāṇāṃ vidyamānatvāvagamātkrūrasya rudraśabdavācyasya "bṛhattvātbṛṃhaṇatvācca tadbrahmeti prakīrtitam" iti brahmaśabdavācyatvāsambhavāt, "teṣāmasurāṇāṃ tisraḥ pura āsann" ityārabhya, "idamasiṣyatīti rudramabruvan rudro vai krūrasso'syatviti. so'bravīdvaraṃ vṛṇai ahameva paśūnāmadhipatirasāviti tasmādrudraḥ paśūnāmadhipatiriti" "rudro vā akāmayata paśumān syāmiti - "mātā rudrāṇāṃ duhitā vasūnām" iti. paśupatitvayācakatvāt. "naiva devo na martyaḥ. na rājā varuṇo vibhuḥ. nāgnirnendro na pavamānaḥ.






[brahmarudrādīnāṃ nārāyaṇasāmyābhāvaḥ]

[100.24-101.7] mātṛkkaśca na vidyate - ityārabhya "etadrudrasya dhanuḥ. rudrasyatvena dhanurārtniḥ. śira utpipeṣa. sa pravargyo'bhavat. tasmādyassapravargyeṇa yajñena yajate. rudrasya sa śiraḥ pratidhāti. nainaṃ rudra āruko bhavati" ityuktatvācca rudrasya brahmaśabdavācyatvaṃ na sambhavati. rudra hara paśupati nīlagrīva śitikaṇṭha bhava śarva kapardītyādīnāmekatvena śruyamāṇānāmekatvaṃ na sambhavati. īśvara maheśvara paramamaheśvara iti paramamaheśvaraśabdaśravaṇāt. "śiva eko dhyeyaḥ śivaśśaṅkaraḥ sarvamanyatparityajye" tyādinā śivaśabdavācyādadhikatvena śivataraśivatamaśabdavācyasya vidyamānatvāt. śambhuśabdasya viśvaśambhutvaśravaṇātīśānasya jagadīśānatvaśravaṇātmahādevasya "tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ devatānāṃ paramañca daivatam" iti śravaṇācca rudrādīnāṃ paraṃbrahmaśabdavācyatvaṃ na sambhavati.
[101.8-12]     nanu - śvetāśvatare - umāsahayaviśeṣita paramamaheśvaraśabdavācyasya rudratvasambhavātparaṃbrahmaśabdavācyatvaṃ tasya sambhavatīti cet- na. paramamaheśvaraśabdasya umāsahāyamityatra rudrādiṣvanupapannatvāt. ukārastvavadhāraṇārthaḥ. u - mā sahāyaṃ lakṣmīsahāyaṃ paramaṃ maheśvarameva trilocanatvādiviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭhaṃ lakṣmīnarasiṃhaṃ dhyātvetyarthaḥ. yadvā. devamanuṣyādyapekṣayā paramamaheśvaratvāt,
[101.13]     "sṛṣṭisthityantakaraṇīṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikām |
[101.14]     sa saṃjñāṃ yāti bhagavāneka eva janārdanaḥ ||"
[101.15-18]     iti bhagavaccharīrabhūtatvācca rudrasya paramamaheśvaratvam. evaṃ bhūtaṃ rudraṃ dhyātvā munirmananaśīlassan bhūtayoniṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇaṃ samstarakṣakaṃ tamasaḥ parastātgacchati. "ādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastād" ityādibhiḥ tamasaḥ pāravartinaṃ prakṛteḥ paraṃ paramātmānaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ gacchati - prāpnoti ityarthaḥ.
[101.19]     kiñca - atra: sano devaḥ śubhayā smṛtyā saṃyunaktu. ityanena
[101.20]     "ārogya bhāskarādicchetśriyamiccheddhūtāśanāt |
[101.21]     śaṅkarātjñānamanvicchetmokṣamicchejjanārdanāt ||"
[101.22-23]     iti jñānapradatvātśaṅkaropāsanamucyate. ato bhagavatā nārāyaṇena sāmyaṃ brahmarudrādīnāṃ nopapadyate.
[101.24]     bhārate: "ye tu sāmānyabhāvena manyante puruṣottamam |
[101.25]     te vai pāṣaṇḍano jñeyāssarvakarmabahiṣkṛtāḥ   || [ṃB.h]

[rudrādisāmyena nārāyaṇāvamantṝṇāmaniṣṭam]

[102.1]     yastu nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ sāmānyenābhimanyate |
[102.2]     sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvaccandrādivākaram   || [ṃB.h]
[102.3]     yo mahādviṣṇumanyena hīnadevena durmati |
[102.4]     sādhāraṇaṃ sakṛdbrūte so'ntyajo nāntyajoṃ'tyajaḥ ||"  [ṃBh]
[102.5]     vārāhe: "anyadevasamaṃ ye tu manyante māṃ narādhamāḥ |
[102.6]     caṇḍālatāṃ vrajantyāśu janmani nava pañca ca ||" iti [Varāhaড়]
[102.7]     śrībhāgavate: "yastu nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ samatvenāvamanyate |
[102.8]     sakṛttenāvamānena narakānnātivartate || [bhāgavatapurāṇa  ]
[102.9]     asmādanyaṃ paratvena cintayāno hyacetanaḥ |
[102.10]     sa pāṣaṇḍīti vijñeyassarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||" iti [bhāgavatapurāṇa  ]
[102.11]     kaurme: "ye'nyaṃ devaṃ paratvena vadantyajñānamohitāḥ |
[102.12]     nārāyaṇājjagannāthātte vai pāṣaṇḍinaḥ smṛtāḥ || [Kūrmaড় ]
[102.13]     yastu nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ brahmarudrādidaivataiḥ |
[102.14]     samatvenaiva vīkṣeta sa pāṣaṇḍī bhaveddvijaḥ ||" iti [Kūrmaড় ]
[102.15]     bhāradvāja: "aśuddhā brahmarudrādyā jīvā viṣṇorvibhūtayaḥ |
[102.16]     tān vai kudṛṣṭayassamyakparatvenāpyupāsate ||" iti
[102.17]     vārāhe: agastyaṃ prati rudraḥ:
[102.18]     "yo yajñairijyate devo yasmātsarvamidaṃ jagat |
[102.19]     utpannaṃ sarvadā yasmin līyate sacarācaram || [Varāhaড়]
[102.20]     nārāyaṇaḥ paro devaḥ sattvarūpī janārdanaḥ |
[102.21]     tridhātmānaṃ sa bhagavān sasarja parameśvaraḥ ||  [Varāhaড়]
[102.22]     śuddhasattvassvayaṃ jajñe rajassattvādikaṃ vibhuḥ |
[102.23]     sasarja nābhikamale brahmāṇaṃ kamalāsanam || [Varāhaড়]
[102.24]     rajasā tamasā yuktaṃ so'pi māṃ sasṛje prabhur ||" iti [Varāhaড়]





[103.1]     nanu: "sarvapāpaharañceha paścātsaṃsāranāśanam |
[103.2]     suvarṇaraupyatāmrādyaiḥ lohenāpi tathaiva ca || [Varāhaড়]
[103.3]     tathā rayāṅgamaṣṭāṅgaṃ sunābhañca suvṛttakam |
[103.4]     jvālācatuṣkasaṃyuktaṃ kṛtvā caiva vidhānataḥ || [Varāhaড়]
[103.5]     agnau pratāpya vidhivatdhārayedbhaktisaṃyutaḥ ||" iti  [Varāhaড়]
[103.6]     "yajño dānaṃ tapo homo bhojanaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam |
[103.7]     cakralāñchanahīnasya viprasya viphalaṃ bhavet  || [Varāhaড়.]
[103.8]     śrāddhe dāne vrate yajñe vivāhe copanāyane |
[103.9]     cakrāṅkitaṃ viprameva pūjayedaparaṃ na tu ||" [Varāhaড়]
[103.10-11]     ityādivacanaiḥ cakrādinānaṅkitasya vaiṣṇavatvābhāvātvaikhānasānāṃ kathaṃ vā vaiṣṇavatvamiti cet- ucyate.

[cakrādyanaṅkitānāmapi vaikhānsānāṃ vaiṣṇavatvam]

[103.12-13]     "yasya me śriyamamitāṃ dātuṃ garbha eva kare cakraṃ dhārayāmi sa cakravartī bhavati" iti śruteḥ
[103.14]     "vaikhānasānāṃ sarveṣāṃ garbhavaiṣṇavajanmanām |
[103.15]     nārāyaṇaḥ svayaṃ garbhe mudrāṃ dhārayate nijām ||" iti
[103.16]     "sudarśanamabhītyuktvā dakṣiṇe tu sudarśanam |
[103.17]     ravipāmiti vāme tu śaṅkhañca bibhṛyātkare ||"

[vaikhānasānāṃ garbhato vaiṣṇavatvam]

[103.18]     iti bhṛguṇoktatvācca śrīviṣṇubalikarmaṇā vaikhānasānāṃ garbhavaiṣṇavatvādikaṃ pratipannam.
[103.19]     tathā: "indriyebhyaḥ parā hyarthā hyarthebhyaśca paraṃ manaḥ |
[103.20]     manasastu parā buddhirbuddherātmā mahān paraḥ ||
[103.21]     mahataḥ paramavyaktamavyaktātpuruṣaḥ paraḥ |
[103.22]     puruṣānna paraṃ kiñcitsā kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ ||"

[garbhagatasya prapadanopapattiḥ]

[103.23]     iti śruteḥ paramaikāntitvaṃ paramapuruṣavaśīkaraṇadvārā garbhagatasya prapattirapi sambhavati.
[103.24]     garbhagatasya prapattirapi garbhopaniṣadi śrūyate. "jñānāddhyanādakṣaramoṅkāraḥ cintayati."





[104.1-2]     ityuktvā "atha navame māsi sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇo bhavati. pūrvajātiṃ smarati. kṛtākṛtañca karma bhavati. śubhāśubhañca karma vindati."
[104.3]     "nānāyonisahasrāṇi dṛṣṭā caiva tato mayā |
[104.4]     āhārā vividhā bhuktāḥ pītāśca vividhāḥ stanāḥ   || [ṅarbha.ū 4.1]
[104.5]     jātasyaiva mṛtasyaiva janma caiva punaḥ punaḥ |
[104.6]     aho duḥkho dadhau magno na paśyāmi pratikriyām ||" ityuktvā [ṅarbhaū 4.2]
[104.7]     "yadi yonyāḥ pramucye'haṃ taṃ prapadye nārāyaṇām |
[104.8]     aśubhakṣayakartāraṃ phalamuktipradāyakam ||" iti [ṅarbhaū 4.6]
[104.9-14]     puruṣasūktabhāṣye - mudgalopaniṣadi - "vāmadevo'pyāṅgirasassan pūrvapūjāṃ samārātriṃ patnyanasūyāgarbha eva śayāno brahmaiva vedayāñcakre sa garbhaṃ puro bhitvā brahmalokamupacakrāma tamutkrāmāntamāha madhye'dhvānamindraḥ upaniṣada uvāca, bhagavan garbhapañjare evābhiniveśamāna anabhijñātopāyo'smātkiñciddhādityā sa garbhassarvo manyamāno'dhvanaḥ pāramupagacchasī"tyādi. tathā ca garbhagatasya prapadanavicāraśravaṇādyupapadyata eva.

[vaidikatāntrikabhedena prapatterdvaividyam]

[104.15-16]     vaidikatāntrikabhedena prapattirdvividhā - praṇavapūrvakatvena puruṣādyāvāhanādikaṃ vaidikam.

[vaidikaprapatterlakṣaṇam]

[104.17]     śrutyantare: "akāreṇocyate viṣṇussarvalokeśvaro hariḥ |
[104.18]     uddhṛtā viṣṇunā lakṣmīrukāreṇa tathocyate |
[104.19]     makārastu tayordāsa iti praṇavalakṣaṇam ||" iti
[104.20]     kiñca. praṇavoccāraṇamātreṇa śaṅkhacakrādidhāraṇamapi sambhavatīti śruyate.
[104.21-24]     kaivalyopaniṣadi - "dhṛtordhvapuṇḍraḥ kṛtacakradhārī viṣṇuṃ paraṃ dhyāyati yo mahātmā. svareṇa mantreṇa sadā hṛdisthaṃ parātparaṃ yanmahato mahāntam." iti. sarvakāraṇabhūtāya sarvarakṣakāya sarvaśeṣiṇe śriyaḥ pataye eva svābhāvikaśeṣabhūta iti sarvadānusandhānameva vaidikī prapattiḥ.





[105.1]     "svojjīvanecchā yadi te svasattāyāṃ spṛhā yadi |
[105.2]     ātmadāsyaṃ hareḥ svāmyaṃ svabhāvañca sadā smara ||"
[105.3]     ityeva sūtre pratipaditam.
[105.4]     pāñcarātre tu
[105.5]     "tyaktvā trayīṃ tantrameva prapadya śaraṇaṃ sthitāḥ ||" iti  [ড়ādmaṣcaryāpāda 21.56 d]
[105.6]     "ananyasādhye svābhīṣṭhe mahāviśvāsapūrvakam |
[105.7]     tadekopāyatā yācñā prapattiḥ śaraṇāgatiḥ ||"
[105.8-10]     iti ca upadiśyate. tasyāḥ prapatteḥ sakṛtkaraṇātsamantrakatvābhāvāttaccheṣagatyanusmaraṇābhāvācca tāṃ tāntrikaprapattiṃ vihāya "yadeva vidyayā karoti tadeva vīryavattaraṃ bhavatī"ti śrutyā vaidikaprapattimevoktavān sūtrakāraḥ.

[taptacakrādyaṅkaṃ na dvijanmanām]

[105.11]     skānde: "viṣṇvāgamāditantreṣu dīkṣitānāṃ vidhīyate |
[105.12]     śaṅkhacakragadāpūrvairaṅkanaṃ nānyadehinām ||  [ṣkandaড় ]
[105.13]     śivāgamādidīkṣāṇāṃ dhāryaṃ tiryaktripuṇḍrakam ||" iti [ṣkandaড় ]
[105.14]     viṣṇu: "śaṅkhacakrādyaṅkanañca gītanṛttādikaṃ tathā |
[105.15]     ekajāterayaṃ dharmaḥ na jātu syāddvijanmanām ||" iti [Viṣṇuড় ]
[105.16]     śrībhāgavate daśamaskandhe:
[105.17]     "trivakrāyāmupaślokaḥ putraḥ kṛṣṇamanuvrataḥ |
[105.18]     śiṣyassākṣānnāradasya dadau cittamakhaṇḍitam ||
[105.19]     tenoktaṃ sāttvataṃ tantraṃ yatjñātvā mokṣabhāgbhavet |
[105.20]     yatra strīśūdradāsānāṃ saṃskāro vaiṣṇavaḥ smṛtaḥ ||" iti  [bhāgavatapurāṇa  ]

[tāntrikaprapatterlakṣaṇādi]

[105.21]     padmapurāṇe: "vedajñe brāhmaṇe nityaṃ sarvāstiṣṭhanti devatāḥ |
[105.22]     iti tittiriśākhāyāṃ śrūyate nṛpasattama || [ড়dmaড় ]
[105.23]     dakṣiṇe'ṃse brāhmaṇasya śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ |
[105.24]     haristiṣṭhati vāme tu devakīsūnurādarāt ||  [ড়dmaড় ]





[106.1]     tato brāhmaṇadeho'yaṃ devatāgāra ucyate |
[106.2]     taddāhe devatāssarve palāyante diśo daśa ||  [ড়dmaড় ]

[taptacakraṅkaṇādividhīnāmadhikārī]

[106.3]     agāradāhī sa bhavettaddāhī brāhmaṇādhamaḥ |
[106.4]     śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ mṛdā yastu kuryāttaptāyasena vā || [ড়dmaড় ]
[106.5]     sa śūdravatbahiṣkāryaḥ sarvasmāddvijakarmaṇaḥ |
[106.6]     yathā śmaśānajaṃ kāṣṭhamanarhaṃ sarvakarmasu || [ড়dmaড় ]
[106.7]     tathā cakrāṅkito viprassarvakarmasu garhitaḥ ||" iti. [ড়dmaড় ]
[106.8]     tatraiva: "mohādvā lobhato vāpi vipraścakrāṅkito yadi |
[106.9]     anarhassa hi vijñeyaḥ śrautasmārteṣu karmasu   || [ড়dma.ড় ]
[106.10]     kriyāsvanarhatāmeti yathā kāṣṭhaṃ śmaśānagam |
[106.11]     tathā cakrādinā dagdhāḥ sarvakarmasvanarhatām ||  [ড়dmaড় ]
[106.12]     dagdhārdhakāyo yatrāsti grāme janapade'pi vā |
[106.13]     śmaśānaṃ taddhi vijñeyaṃ vāsānarhaṃ dvijanmanām ||" [ড়dmaড় ]
[106.14-16]     ityādinā dūṣaṇamuktam. etāni taptacakrāṅkanavidhāyakāni vacanāni "viṣṇvāgamāditantreṣu dīkṣitānām" ityuktatvātekajātiviṣayatvenoktatvāt"nānyadehinām" ityuktatvācca tāntrikāgamoktaprakāreṇa dīkṣitaviṣayāṇītyavagamyate.
[106.17-19]     yadvā vidhiniṣedhavacanānāṃ vaiyarthyaparihārāya "vapāśrapaṇībhyām  anvārabhate tannaivānvārabdhaṃ naivānanvārabdhaṃ" "avaghreyameva tatnaiva prāśitaṃ naivāprāśitam" ityādi śrutinyāyena samantrakatvena bhāvanārūpeṇa śrīvaikhānasaśāstre pratipāditam.. yathā:
[106.20]     "sudarśanamabhītyuktvā dakṣiṇe tu sudarśanam |
[106.21]     ravipāmiti vāme ca śaṅkhañca bibhṛyātkare ||" iti
[106.22]     mahopaniṣadi - brahmasūkte:
[106.23]     "dakṣiṇe tu bhuje vipro bibhṛyādvai sudarśanam |
[106.24]     savye tu śaṅkhaṃ bibhṛyāditi vedavido viduḥ ||" iti.





[pāñcarātroktataptacakradhāraṇasya sarvādhikārikatvābhāvaḥ]

[pāñcarātrādīnāṃ mohanaśāstratvam]

[107.1]     mohaśāstratvātśaptaviṣayatvācca pañcarātre tāpavidhānamuktamiti kecitvadanti.
[107.2]     padmapurāṇe gautama: "yanmayi brāhmaṇābhāsāḥ ṛjau kaitavamīdṛśam |
[107.3]     anuṣṭhitaṃ tato vedamārgabhraṣṭhāśca pāpinaḥ   || [ড়dma.ড় ]
[107.4]     kālamukhe pāñcarātre kālapāle śāmbhave'pi ca |
[107.5]     śākte ca dīkṣitā yūyaṃ bhavata brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||" iti [ড়dmaড় ]
[107.6]     brahmā: "bauddhe bhāgavate tantre tṛtīye pāñcarātrake |
[107.7]     dīkṣitāśca dvijā nityaṃ bhaveyurnirbhayā hare ||  [ড়dmaড় ]
[107.8]     śrautasmārtasamācāre sadyomuktikare śubhe |
[107.9]     tvadbhaktivimukhā nityaṃ bhaveyuḥ parimohitāḥ   || [ড়dma.ড় ]
[107.10]     śaṅkhvaikhanacakragadāpūrvavajrapāśāṅkuśādibhiḥ |
[107.11]     aṅkitāḥ śraddhayā yūyaṃ bhavata brāhmaṇādhamāḥ ||" iti  [ড়dmaড় ]
[107.12]     skānde - "sūtasaṃhitāyāṃ sarasvatīṃ prati rudraḥ:
[107.13]     "śrutirbalīyasītyuktā pramāṇānāṃ sulocane" ityārabhya  [ṣkandaড় ]
[107.14]     "pāñcarātrādimārgāṇāṃ vedamūlatvamāstike |
[107.15]     na hi svatantratastena bhrāntimūlāni rūpiṇi || [ṣkandaড় ]
[107.16]     tathāpi yo'ṃśo mārgeṇa vaidikenāviruddhyate |
[107.17]     so'ṃśaḥ pramāṇamityuktaṃ keṣāñcidadhikāriṇām || [ṣkandaড় ]
[107.18]     atyantaskhalitānān tu prāṇiṇāṃ vedamārgataḥ |
[107.19]     pāñcarātrādayo mārgāḥ kālenaivopakārakāḥ |
[107.20]     tāntrikāṇāṃ mahādevi na labhyo'vyavadhānataḥ ||" iti pratipāditam. [ṣkandaড় ]
[107.21]     kaurme: "purā dāruvane puṇye munayo gṛhamedhinaḥ ||" [Kūrmaড় 16.97 b]
[107.22]     ityārabhya māyayā gokalpanādi śāpaprāptiparyantamuktvā:
[107.23]     "kā no vṛttirmahādeva śāpānnaḥ trātumarhasi | [Kūrmaড় 16.112 d]
[107.24]     tathā pārśvasthitaṃ viṣṇuṃ samprekṣya vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ |






[pāñcarātrasya vedabhraṣṭānuddiśya pravṛttatvam]

[108.1]     kimeteṣāṃ bhavetkāryaṃ prāha puṇyaiṣiṇāmiti   || [Kūrma.ড় 16.113]
[108.2]     tatassa bhagavān viṣṇuḥ śaraṇyo bhaktavatsalaḥ |
[108.3]     gopatiṃ prāha viprendrānālokya praṇatān hariḥ ||  [Kūrmaড় 16.114]
[108.4]     na vedabāhye śāstre ca puṇyaleśo'pi śaṅkara |
[108.5]     saṅgacchate yato dharmo vedādeva hi nirbabhau || [Kūrmaড় 16.115]
[108.6]     tathāpi bhaktavātsalyātrakṣaṇīyā maheśvara |
[108.7]     āvābhyāṃ sarva evaite viprā vai nātrasaṃśayaḥ || [Kūrmaড় 16.116]
[108.8]     tasmādvai vedabāhyānāṃ rakṣaṇārthañca pāpinām |
[108.9]     vimohanāya śāstrāṇi kariṣyāmi vṛṣadhvaja || [Kūrmaড় 16.117]
[108.10]     evaṃ sañcodito rudro mādhavenāsurāriṇā |
[108.11]     cakāra mohaśāstrāṇi keśavo'pi śiveritaḥ ||  [Kūrmaড় 16.118]
[108.12]     kāpālaṃ yāmalaṃ vāmaṃ bhairavaṃ pūrvapaścimam |
[108.13]     pāñcarātraṃ pāśupataṃ tathānyāni sahasraśaḥ ||  [Kūrmaড় 16.119]
[108.14]     kṛtvā cārcya ca taddevān  kurvāṇāstatra coditam |
[108.15]     patanti narake ghore bahūn kalpān punaḥ punaḥ ||" ityādi coktam [Kūrmaড় 16.120]
[108.16]     skānde śaṅkarasaṃhitāyām
[108.17]     "nekṣeta pūjāvelāyāṃ naraṃ vatsa bahiṣkṛtam |
[108.18]     dadhīcinā gautamena ye śaptā brāhmaṇādhamāḥ || [ṣkandaড় ]
[108.19]     kalāvavatariṣyanti ninditāste bahiṣkṛtāḥ ||" iti [ṣkandaড় ]
[108.20]     sāmbapurāṇe: "śrutibhraṣṭaḥ smṛtiprokta prāyaścitte bhayaṃ gataḥ |
[108.21]     krameṇa śrutisiddhyarthaṃ manuṣyastantramāśrayet || [ṣāmbaড়]
[108.22]     dharmaśāstre purāṇe ca proktaṃ hi maraṇāntikam |
[108.23]     prāyaścittaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ pāpiṣṭhānāṃ sudāruṇam || [ṣāmbaড়]







[pāñcarātramārgānusāriṇāṃ śrautasmārtabāhyatvam]

[109.1]     bhayaṃ durbalacittānāṃ maraṇe jāyate bhṛśam |
[109.2]     teṣāmevābhirakṣārthaṃ tadahaṃ tantramuktavān ||  [ṣāmbaড়]
[109.3]     pāñcarātraṃ bhāgavataṃ tantraṃ sāttvatanāmakam |
[109.4]     vedabhraṣṭān samuddiśya kamalāpatirabravīt ||" iti [ṣāmbaড়]
[109.5]     anyatra: "athāṃśoḥ sāttvato nāma viṣṇubhaktaḥ pratāpavān |
[109.6]     mahātmā dānanirato dhanurvedavidāṃ varaḥ ||
[109.7]     sa nāradasya vacanādvāsudevārcane rataḥ |
[109.8]     śāstraṃ pravartayāmāsa kuṇḍagolādibhiḥ śritam ||
[109.9]     tasya nāmnā tu vikhyātaṃ sāttvataṃ nāma śobhanam |
[109.10]     pravartate mahacchāstraṃ kuṇḍādīnāṃ hitāvaham ||" iti
[109.11]     vāsiṣṭhe: "bṛddhaśāvaka nirgandhāḥ pāñcarātravido janāḥ |
[109.12]     kāpālikāḥ pāśupatāḥ pāṣaṇḍā ye ca tadvidhāḥ ||  [Vāsiṣṭhaḍhś]
[109.13]     yasyāśnanti havīṃśyete durātmānastu tāmasāḥ |
[109.14]     na tasya tadbhavecchāstraṃ pretyeha ca na tatphalam ||" iti. [Vāsiṣṭhaḍhś]
[109.15]     laiṅge - navamādhyāye yugadharme
[109.16]     "aṅkitāśca bhaviṣyanti devatāyudhalāñchanaiḥ |
[109.17]     nārāyaṇamaśṛṇvanti yugānte samupasthite ||  [ḻiṅgaড়]
[109.18]     anyāni yāni karmāṇi śrautāni munisattama |
[109.19]     smārtāni nācariṣyanti kalau brahmannarāssadā || [ḻiṅgaড়]
[109.20]     āmnāyaṃ dharmaśāstrañca purāṇaṃ bhārataṃ mune |
[109.21]     alpamātramiti brahman vadiṣyanti kalau yuge || [ḻiṅgaড়]
[109.22]     na kuryuḥ śrautakarmāṇi śraddhayā munisattama |
[109.23]     svadharme rucitā nāsti brāhmaṇānāṃ kalau yuge || [ḻiṅgaড়]
[109.24]     pāñcarātraṃ praśaṃsanti kecidbhāgavataṃ mune |
[109.25]     kecitkāpālamicchanti kecitpāśupataṃ mune ||  [ḻiṅgaড়]






[cakrāṅkanasya bhāvanārūpeṇaiva kartavyatā]

[110.1]     kecidbauddhaṃ praśaṃsanti keciddaigambaraṃ tathā |
[110.2]     kecillokāyataṃ brahman kecitsaumaṃ mahāmune ||  [ḻiṅgaড়]
[110.3]     lākulaṃ kecidicchanti tathā kecittu bhairavam |
[110.4]     kecidvāmaṃ praśaṃsanti kecicchāktaṃ tathaiva ca ||  [ḻiṅgaড়]
[110.5]     śāmbhavaṃ kecidicchanti yāmalaṃ bhuvi kecana |
[110.6]     anyāni yāni śāstrāṇi viruddhāni mahāmune ||  [ḻiṅgaড়]
[110.7]     tīrthavikrayiṇaḥ keciddevavikrayiṇaḥ pare |
[110.8]     kṣīravikrayiṇaḥ kecidājyavikrayiṇaḥ pare || [ḻiṅgaড়]
[110.9]     māṃsavikrayiṇaḥ kecitsurāvikrayiṇaḥ pare |
[110.10]     putravikrayiṇaḥ keciddāravikrayiṇaḥ pare ||  [ḻiṅgaড়]
[110.11]     bhartṛvikrayiṇaḥ kecidbhaviṣyanti kalau narāḥ ||" iti [ḻiṅgaড়]
[110.12]     ataḥ pāñcarātoktaṃ taptacakradhāraṇaṃ sarvasādhāraṇaṃ na bhavantīti siddham.
[110.13-16]     atha yaduktaṃ sarvasādhāraṇaṃ na bhavatīti tannopapadyate - ātharvaṇe "cakraṃ bibharti vapuṣābhitaptaṃ balaṃ devānāmamitasya viṣṇoḥ. sa eti nākaṃ duritā vidhūya prayānti yadyatayo vītarāgāḥ, "caraṇaṃ pavitraṃ vitataṃ purāṇaṃ" ityādi śrutibhiḥ sarvasādhāraṇatvena śrūyamāṇatvātiti cet- na.
[110.17-20]     taptacakradhāraṇe prāyaścittavidhānāt. ataḥ yathoktamārgeṇa bhāvanārūpeṇaiva cakrāṅkanaṃ na tu taptarūpeṇa. sarvasādhāraṇaśrutistu vaikhānasetaraviṣayā. "yasya me śriyam" ityādi śrutyavirodhena arthavyavasthāyāḥ kāryatvāt. ataḥ taptacakrāṅkanaṃ sarvasādhāraṇaṃ na bhavati.
[110.21]     kiñca: "yatra śāstragatirbhinnā sarvakarmasu bhārata |
[110.22]     udite'nudite caiva homabhede yathā bhavet |
[110.23]     sa garīyānmahābāho sarvaśāstroditādapi ||"
[110.24]     iti sumantusmaraṇācca vyavasthitavikalpotra vaktavyaḥ.







[viṣṇoreva saṃhārakartṛtvam]

[111.1-2]     kiñca brahmarudrādīnāmapi saṃhārakartā harireveti śrūyate. yajuṣi - hariṃ harantamanuyanti devāḥ viśvasyeśānaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ matīnām" ityabhierṭelugu berprfen

[viṣṇukartṛkarudropāsanasya kāraṇam]

[111.3]     brahmapurāṇe: "brahmāṇamindraṃ rudrañca yamaṃ varuṇameva ca |
[111.4]     nigṛhyāharate yasmāttasmāddharirihocyate   || [Brahma.ড় ]
[111.5]     brahmā śambhustathaivārkaścandramāśca śatakratuḥ |
[111.6]     evamādyāstathaivānye yuktā vaiṣṇavatejasā   || [Brahma.ড় ]
[111.7]     jagatkāryāvasāne tu viyujyante ca tejasā |
[111.8]     tejasaścaiva te sarve pañcatvamupayānti c |" iti  [Brahmaড় ]
[111.9-14]     nanu - "hariṃ harantaṃ" iti pūrvopāttayajuṣi īśānaśabdaśravaṇātrudra eva saṃhārakarteti śrūyate iti cet- tadasat. tatra "viśvasyeśāna," iti jaganniyāmakatvamucyate. bishiertelugu nochberprfentathā ca "patiṃ viśrasyātmeśvara," itivatnārāyaṇa eva atra pratipādyaḥ. "asyeśānā jagato viṣṇupatnī" sa hariḥ suvittamaḥ, "perurindrāya pinvata" "virāḍasi", "bṛhatī śravasīndrapatnī dharmapatnī", "sa brahmā sa śivassendraḥ", ityādi śrutayotra vibhāvanīyāḥ.
[111.15]     nanu kaurme aṣṭamādhyāye - rāmāyaṇasaṃkṣepe:
[111.16]     "sa kṛtvā vānaraśataiḥ laṅkādvāraṃ mahodadhau || [Kūrmaড় 21.44 d]
[111.17]     setuṃ paramadharmātmā rāvaṇaṃ hatavān prabhuḥ ||" [Kūrmaড় 21.45 b]
[111.18]     iti rāvaṇavadhādikaṃ pratipādya:
[111.19]     "setumadhye mahādevamīśānaṃ kṛttivāsasam || [Kūrmaড় 21.46 d]
[111.20]     sthāpayāmāsa liṅgasthaṃ pūjayāmāsa rāghavaḥ |
[111.21]     tasya devo mahādevaḥ pārvatyā saha śaṅkaraḥ || [Kūrmaড় 21.47]
[111.22]     pratyakṣameva bhagavān dattavān varamuttamam |" [Kūrmaড় 21.48 b]







[rudrasya varadātṛtvaṃ viṣṇuprasādalabdham]

[112.1-2]     iti bhagavate rudropāsanamucyate iti cet- satyam. rudrasya tadapi bhagavadvaraprasādalabdham. evaṃ hi vārāhe prāgitihāse rudragītāsu ucyate, yathā -
[112.3]     agastyaṃ prati rudraḥ:
[112.4]     "brahmaṇāhaṃ purā sṛṣṭaḥ proktaḥ sṛja iti prajāḥ |
[112.5]     avijñātāsamartho'haṃ nimagnassalile dvija ||" ityārabhya [Varāhaড়]
[112.6-7]     jalamadhye kālamedhasaṅkāśaḥ atiśobhanaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ kaścitpuruṣo rudreṇa dṛṣṭaḥ kastvamiti rudreṇa pṛṣṭasya tasya prativacanam:
[112.8]     "ahaṃ nārāyaṇo devo jalaśāyī sanātanaḥ |
[112.9]     divyaṃ cakṣurbhavatu te tena māṃ paśya yatnataḥ ||" iti pratipādya [Varāhaড়]
[112.10]     "evamuktastadā tena yāvatpaśyāmmahaṃ tanum |
[112.11]     tāvadaṅguṣṭhamātran tu jvaladbhāskaratejasam ||  [Varāhaড়]
[112.12]     tamevāhaṃ prapaśyāmi tasya nābhau tu paṅkajam |
[112.13]     brahmāṇaṃ tatra paśyāmi hyātmānañca tadaṅkataḥ || [Varāhaড়]
[112.14]     evaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahātmānaṃ tato harṣamupāgataḥ |
[112.15]     taṃ stotuṃ dvijaśārdūla matirme samajāyata ||"  [Varāhaড়]
[112.16-17]     ityupakramya mahatstotramuktvā ante rudreṇa bhagavantaṃ pratyabhīṣṭanivedanaṃ pratipāditam. yathā:
[112.18]     "iti stuto me bhagavannanāde juṣasva bhaktasya viśeṣataśca |
[112.19]     sṛṣṭiṃ sṛjasveti tu coditasya sarvajñatāṃ dehi namo'stu viṣṇo ||" iti [Varāhaড়]
[112.20]     evaṃ stotre parisamāpite
[112.21]     "varaṃ varava bhadraṃ te devadeva umāpate ||" [Varāhaড়]
[112.22]     iti bhagavatā niyuktaḥ rudra:
[112.23]     "brahmaṇā cāhamuktastu prajāssṛja iti prabho |
[112.24]     tatra jñānaṃ prayacchasva trividhaṃ bhūtabhāvana ||" iti prārthayāmāsa. [Varāhaড়]






[viṣṇoḥ śarabharūparudrādisaṃhārakatvam]

[113.1]     tadā śrībhagavān:
[113.2]     "sarvajñastvaṃ na sandeho jñānarāśisanātanaḥ |
[113.3]     devānāñca sadā pūjyaḥ sarvadā tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi   || [Varāhaড়.]
[113.4]     evamukte punarvākyamuvācomāpatirmudā |
[113.5]     anyaṃ dehi varaṃ deva prasiddhaṃ sarvajantuṣu ||  [Varāhaড়]
[113.6]     martyo bhūtvā bhavāneva māmārādhaya keśava |
[113.7]     māṃ vahasya ca deveśa varaṃ matto gṛhāṇa ca   || [Varāhaড়.]
[113.8]     yenāhaṃ sarvadevānāṃ pūjyātpūjyataro'bhavat ||" iti punaḥ tena prārthitaḥ [Varāhaড়]
[113.9]     śrībhagavān: "devakāryāvatāreṣu mānuṣyatvamupāgataḥ |
[113.10]     tvāmevārādhayiṣyāmi tvañca me varado bhava ||  [Varāhaড়]
[113.11]     yattvayoktaṃ vahasveti devadeva umāpate |
[113.12]     sa tvāṃ vahāmi deva tvāṃ megho bhūtvā śataṃ samāḥ ||" iti varamapi dadau.
[113.13]     "evameva harirdevaḥ sarveśassarvabhāvanaḥ |
[113.14]     varado'bhūtpurā mahyaṃ tenāhaṃ daivatairvṛtaḥ   || [Varāhaড়.]
[113.15]     nārāyaṇātparo devo na bhūto na bhaviṣyati |
[113.16]     etadrahasyaṃ devānāṃ purāṇānāñca sattama ||" iti [Varāhaড়]
[113.17]     kiñca
[113.18]     śrīviṣṇupurāṇe: "devatiryaṅmanuṣyeṣu śarīragrahaṇātmikā |
[113.19]     līleyaṃ sarvabhūtasya tava ceṣṭopalakṣaṇā ||  [Viṣṇuড় ]
[113.20]     devatiryaṅmanuṣyākhyā ceṣṭāvanti svalīlayā |
[113.21]     jagatāmupakārāya na sā karmanimittajā ||" iti  [Viṣṇuড় ]
[113.22-23]     evaṃ nṛsiṃharūpiṇā bhagavatā śarabharūpeṇāgatānāṃ brahmarudrādīnāmapi saṃhārādikaṃ nārasiṃhapurāṇādiṣvavagamyate. yathā:

[śarabhanirmāṇādiprakāraḥ]

[114.1]     hiraṇyakavadhānantaraṃ: tatra saptatriṃśādhyāye
[114.2]     "hiraṇyakaśipostrastān sendrān devān bṛhaspatiḥ |
[114.3]     kṣīrodasyottaraṃ gatvā stūyatāṃ tatra keśavaḥ || [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.4]     yuṣmābhissaṃstuto viṣṇuḥ prasanno bhavati kṣaṇāt ||" ityārabhya [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.5]     "yuṣmadāgamanaṃ sarvaṃ jānāmyasuramardanāḥ |
[114.6]     hiraṇyakavināśārthaṃ stuto'haṃ śaṅkareṇa ca || ityuktvā  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.7]     hiraṇyakavadhānantaraṃ
[114.8]     "tasya kopābhibhūtasya nṛsiṃhasya jagatpateḥ |
[114.9]     dṛṣṭvā bhayānakaṃ rūpaṃ vitresurdevadānavāḥ ||" [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.10]     ityārabhyoktastotrānantaraṃ brahmasamīpagamanādikaṃ pratipādya:
[114.11]     "tasmin bhagavati kruddhe nṛsiṃhe paramātmani |
[114.12]     pravepitaṃ jagadidaṃ deveśe kupite bhṛśam ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.13]     tvatto hi nānyaśśaraṇaṃ devānāmiha vidyate |
[114.14]     narasiṃhasamudbhūtaṃ bhayaṃ nāśaya me hare ||" [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.15]     ityādinā rudraprārthanādikamuktvā
[114.16] "hato hiraṇyakaśipuḥ yena daityo mahābalaḥ |
[114.17]     ko naśśamayitā tasya kopasya harimedhasaḥ   || [ṇarasiṃhaড়.]
[114.18]     tvaṃ me janayitā deva sa te janayitā hariḥ |
[114.19]     tasya devasya kaśśakto viṣṇorvai nigrahe bhavet ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.20]     yadbhayātpavate vāyuḥ sūryastapati yadbhayāt |
[114.21]     yadbhayāddharaṇī dhatte nigrahe tasya kaḥ prabhuḥ |
[114.22]     tathāpyupāyo dṛśyo me parameṇa samādhinā ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[114.23]     kṛte tasmin bhavecchreyaḥ tūṣṇīṃ bhāvo na rocate |
[114.24]     aśvānāṃ mahiṣaḥ śatruḥ vāraṇānāṃ mṛgādhipaḥ   || [ṇarasiṃhaড়.]
[115.1]     vānarāṇāṃ tathā meṣaḥ phaṇīnāṃ garuḍaḥ smṛtaḥ |
[115.2]     mūṣakānān tu mārjāro mṛgāṇāṃ śvā prakīrtitaḥ   || [ṇarasiṃhaড়.]
[115.3]     vāyasānāṃ divābhītaḥ siṃhānāṃ śarabhastathā |
[115.4]     tato nirmāpayiṣyāmaḥ śarabhaṃ bhayaśāntaye   || [ṇarasiṃhaড়.]
[115.5]     śarabho'dhiṣṭhito'smābhiḥ nṛsiṃhaṃ śamayiṣyati |
[115.6]     ityevamuktvā bhagavān sasarja śarabhaṃ tathā   || [ṇarasiṃhaড়.]
[115.7]     yasya sandarśanādeva trastamāsījjagattrayam |
[115.8]     tatastasya bhavānīśaḥ tuṇḍasthānamarocata ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[115.9]     pṛṣṭhabhāge caturvaktaṃ tasya rudro nyaveśayat |
[115.10]     somasūryau nayanayoḥ mārutaṃ pakṣayordvayoḥ ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[115.11]     pādeṣu bhūdharān sarvān śivastasya nyaveśayat |
[115.12]     evaṃ nirmāya śarabhaṃ bhavaḥ pramathanāyakaḥ ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[115.13]     jagarja narasiṃhaṃ taṃ samuddiśya bhayānakam ||"  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[115.14]     iti śarabhanirmāṇādikaṃ pratipādya
[115.15]     "tataḥ kṣaṇena śarabho nādapūritadiṅmukhaḥ |
[115.16]     abhyāśamagamadviṣṇoḥ ninadan bhairavaṃ svanam ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[115.17]     tamabhyāśagataṃ dṛṣṭvā nṛsiṃhaḥ śarabhaṃ ruṣā |
[115.18]     jaghāna niśitairugraiḥ nakhairnakhavarāyudhaḥ ||  [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[115.19]     nihate śarabhe tasmin raudre madhuvighātinā |
[115.20]     tuṣṭubuḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ devā devarṣayastathā ||" [ṇarasiṃhaড়]
[115.21]     iti bhagavatā brahmarudrādyadhiṣṭhitaśarabhahananaṃ pratipāditam.
[115.22]     gāruḍe aṣṭanavatitamādhyāye:
[115.23]     "hantumabhyāgataṃ raudraṃ śarabhaṃ narakesarī |
[115.24]     nakhairvidārayāmāsa hiraṇyakaśipuṃ yathā ||  [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[116.1]     nikṛttabāhūruśirāḥ vajrakalpamukhairnakhaiḥ |
[116.2]     merupṛṣṭhe nṛsiṃhena śarabhassahasāpatat ||" [ṅaruḍaড় ]
[116.3]     pādme: "tau yudhyamānau tu ciraṃ vegena balavattarau |
[116.4]     vināśaṃ jagmaturdevau nṛsiṃhaśarabhāviti   || [ড়dma.ড় ]
[116.5]     tataḥ kruddho mahābāhuḥ nṛsiṃho bhīmanissvanaḥ |
[116.6]     sahasrakarapannetraḥ tasya gātrāṇyakartayat  || [ড়dma.ড় ]
[116.7]     patitaṃ bhīmamatyugraṃ gatāṃsuṃ draṣṭumāgatāḥ |
[116.8]     ṛṣayo devagandharvā yatra śete bhavo haraḥ   || [ড়dma.ড় ]
[116.9]     taṃ dṛṣṭvā paramaṃ jagmuḥ vismayantu divaukasaḥ |
[116.10]     paśaśaṃsustathā karma nṛsiṃhasya mahādbhutam ||" iti [ড়dmaড় ]
[116.11]     śrībhāgavate: "tatastvaritamādāya rudraṃ śarabharūpiṇam |
[116.12]     vidadāra nakhaistīkṣṇaiḥ hiraṇyakaśipuṃ yathā ||" iti [bhāgavatapurāṇa  ]

[rudropāsakānāṃ śūdratulyatvam]

[116.13-14]     kiñca ānuśāsanike umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde bhagavato'ṅgabhāvena vinā rudropāsanaṃ kurvatāṃ śudratulyatvamuktam. yathā dakṣādhvare:
[116.15]     rudrāṇī: ahaṃ jyeṣṭhā viśiṣṭā ca tasmāttvaṃ māṃ supūjaya |
[116.16]     dakṣa: na tu jyeṣṭhā viśiṣṭā tvaṃ mama pūjyā imāssurāḥ ||"  [ṃBh]
[116.17]     ityādinā rudrāṇīdūṣaṇamadhvarabhaṅgādikaṃ ca pratipādya rudraṃ prati śāpotsargaḥ kriyate.
[116.18]     "etadvaṃśasamudbhūtairaspṛśyastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi |
[116.19]     naitadvaṃśeṣu yaḥ kaścittvadbhakto hi janiṣyati || [ṃBh]
[116.20]     ajānāttvāṃ hi yaḥ kaścitmunirvā brāhmaṇo'pi vā |
[116.21]     anujīvati cettasmāttulyaśśūdreṇa garhitaḥ || [ṃBh]
[116.22]     bhaveddevalakaścaiva rudrakālyupajīvakaḥ ||" iti  [ṃBh]




[bodhāyanādīnāṃ paramaikāntitvābhāve hetavaḥ]

[kātyāyanasūtriṇāṃ paramaikāntitvābhāvaḥ]

[116.23-117.2]     ato nārāyaṇasya rudreṇa samānatayā bodhāyanena pratipāditvātbodhāyanāpastambādisūtreṣu ṣadvidhatātparyaliṅgābhāvātteṣāṃ sṛṣṭikālādārabhya nārāyaṇaikaparatvābhāvātnānādevatopāsakatvadarśanācca paramaikāntitvaṃ na sambhavati. evaṃ kātyāyanasūtriṇāmapi:
[117.3]     "brāhmavaiṣṇavaraudraistu sāvitrairmaitravāruṇaiḥ |
[117.4]     talliṅgaireva mantraistu arcayettu samāhitaḥ ||" [Kātyāyanasūtra]
[117.5]     iti pratipādanātna paramaikāntitvam.
[117.6]     drāhyāyaṇasūtre tu rudraviṣayatvena karmārambhe prapadanamuktam.
[117.7-9]     ataḥ śrīmannārāyaṇaikaparatvātparamaikāntitvaṃ śrīmadvaikhānasānāmeveti pratijñā kartumucitā. evaṃ "akhilajagadekakāraṇaśrīmannārāyaṇaikaparatvāt" iti navamaheturnirūpitaḥ.
[117.10]     iti navamahetunirūpaṇam.

[daśamahetunirūpaṇam]

[vaikhānasānāṃ bhagavatpriyatamatvam]

[118.1-3]     athāsya śrīvaikhānasasūtrasya śīmannārāyaṇaikaparatvādtatsūtrānuṣṭhānavatāmeva bhagavatpriyatamatvamupapadyata iti pratipādanāya "bhagavatpriyatamatvopapatter" iti daśamaheturnirūpyate.
[118.4-5]     chandogabrāhmaṇe: "vaikhānasā vā ṛṣaya indrasya priyā āsan" iti. atra indraśabdaḥ paramātmapara iti pūrvamevoktam.
[118.6]     vārāhe: "aśvatthaḥ kapilā gāvaḥ tulasī vikhanāstathā |
[118.7]     catvāro matpriyā rājan teṣāṃ vaikhānaso varaḥ || [Varāhaড়]
[118.8]     yatra vaikhānasā viprā yatra santi gavāṃ gaṇāḥ |
[118.9]     yatra bilvāḥ palāśāśca tatra sannihito hariḥ ||" iti  [Varāhaড়]
[118.10]     kaurme: "maccitā madgataprāṇā mayi saṅgatamānasāḥ |
[118.11]     ananyaśaraṇā rājan tasmādvaikhānasā varāḥ ||"  [Kūrmaড় ]
[118.12]     pāñcarātre pauṣkare: "viprā vaikhānasākhyā ye te bhaktāstattvamucyate || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.260 d]
[118.13]     ekantinaḥ susatvasthā dehāntaṃ nānyayājinaḥ |
[118.14]     kartavyamiti deveśaṃ saṃyajante phalaṃ vinā || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.261]
[118.15]     prāpnuvanti ca dehānte vāsudevatvamabjaja || [ড়auṣkaraṣ36.262 b]

[devārcanaparāṇāṃ śraiṣṭhyam]

[118.16]     bṛhannāradīye: "devārcanaparā ye ca ye ca tatsādhakā narāḥ |
[118.17]     pūjāṃ dṛṣṭvānumodante te vai bhāgavatottamāḥ   || [Bṛhannāradaড়.]
[118.18]     yastu bhogatayā viṣṇoḥ satkṛtyaṃ kurute sadā |
[118.19]     sa bhavetparamaikāntī mahābhāgavatottamaḥ   || [Bṛhannāradaড়.]
[118.20]     ya aikāntyagatā viṣṇau bhagavatyātmabhāvane |
[118.21]     te vaiṣṇavā bhāgavatāssanta ityapi kīrtitāḥ ||  [Bṛhannāradaড়]






[viṣṇuniveditānnādeḥ bhojyatvam]

[119.1]     manmānasāśca madbhaktā mama pūjāsu lolupāḥ |
[119.2]     matkathāśravaṇe sattāste vai bhāgavatāḥ smṛtāḥ ||" iti [Bṛhannāradaড়]
[119.3]     "ye'rcayanti narā bhaktyā nārāyaṇamanāmayam |
[119.4]     tānarcayanti vibudhā brahmādyā devatāgaṇāḥ ||
[119.5]     mayi bhaktiparo yastu madyājī matparāyaṇaḥ |
[119.6]     maddhyānī svakulaṃ sarvaṃ nayatyacyutarūpatām ||
[119.7]     ye'rcayanti sadā viṣṇuṃ na te prākṛtamānuṣāḥ |
[119.8]     viṣṇulokātsamutpannāste devā nātra saṃśayaḥ || [āṣ14.58]
[119.9]     nāviṣṇurjayate viṣṇurnāviṣṇurviṣṇumarcayet |
[119.10]     rūpadvayaṃ hareḥ proktaṃ bimbamarcaka eva ca || [Krā 36.59 d]
[119.11]     bimbe tvāvāhanādūrdhvaṃ sadā sannihito'rcake | [Krā 36.60 b]
[119.12]     arcakastu prasannātmā harireva na saṃśayaḥ ||
[119.13]     kaliḥ kṛtayugasteṣāṃ kleśāścaiva sukhāvahāḥ |
[119.14]     yeṣāṃ śarīramaniśaṃ hariśuśrūṣaṇe ratam ||" iti
[119.15]     bhāgavate: "sā vāganantarasya guṇān gṛṇīte karau ca tatkarmakarau manaśca |
[119.16]     smaredvasantaṃ sthirajaṅgameṣu śruṇoti tatpuṇyakathāssa karṇaḥ ||
[119.17]     śiraśca tasyāṅghriṣu namramāṇaṃ tameva yaḥ paśyati taddhi cakṣuḥ |
[119.18]     aṅgāni viṣṇoratha tajjanānāṃ pādodakaṃ yāni vahanti nityam || [bhāgavatapurāṇa  10.80.4]
[119.19]     hṛdi rūpaṃ mukhe nāma naivedyamudare hareḥ |
[119.20]     pādodakañca nirmālyaṃ yasya mūrdhni sa vaiṣṇavaḥ ||" iti [bhāgavatapurāṇa  ]





[119.21-120.3]     śrutirapi: "tadasya priyamabhipātho aśyāṃ naro yatra devayavo madanti urukramasya sa hi bandhuritthā viṣṇoḥ pade parame madhva utsaḥ" iti. asyārthaḥ: viṣṇoḥ'priyaṃ' tatpāthaḥ niveditamannādikam'abhyaśyām'.'devayavo narāḥ' devārādhakāḥ'yatra madanti' yasminniveditatīrthaprasādādike svīkṛte'madanti' hṛṣṭā bhavanti. yadvā: brahmādīnapi na gaṇayanti.'itthā' itthaṃ pratidinaṃ vartamānaḥ'urukramasya viṣṇorbandhuḥ' jātyekavacanam. sa evātīvābhimataḥ'parame' sarvotkṛṣṭe'madhve' madhuvatbhogyatame viṣṇoḥ pade'utsa' utsukaḥ. yadvā utkṛṣṭaḥ.
[120.4-5]     śrutyantare: "viṣṇunāśitamaśnanti viṣṇunā pītaṃ pibanti viṣṇuna ghrātaṃ jighranti viṣṇunā rasitaṃ rasayanti tasmādvidvāṃso viṣṇūpahṛtaṃ bhakṣayeyuḥ" iti.
[120.7]     bhāgavate: "tvayopabhuktasraggandha vāso'laṅkāracarcitāḥ |
[120.8]     ucchiṣṭabhojino dāsāstava māyāṃ taremahi ||" iti [bhāgavatapurāṇa  11.6.46]
[120.9]     hārīta: "kṛpādattaṃ hastagataṃ viṣṇubhuktojjhitantu yat |
[120.10]     upavāsachalānnātti tena bhuktaṃ suropamam ||" iti
[120.11]     bhṛgu: "haviḥ kṛtvā caturbhāgamūrdhvabhāgau nivedayet |
[120.12]     adhastādekabhāgena homa balimathācaret ||
[120.13]     yadaṃśaṃ pātrasaṃśiṣṭaṃ pūjakāyaiva nirmitam |
[120.14]     agnikāryāvaśiṣṭañca baliśiṣṭañca yaddhaviḥ |
[120.15]     sarvaṃ tatpūjakāyaiva proktamevaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||" iti
[120.16]     yajñādhikāre bhṛgu: "ātmanaśca caturthāṃśaṃ vaiśvadevārthamarcakaḥ |
[120.17]     ādāya vaiśvadevānte śiṣṭānnaṃ bhojayetpunaḥ   || [ẏ.ā ]
[120.18]     baliśeṣaṃ homaśeṣaṃ vaiśvadeve viśeṣataḥ |
[120.19]     gṛhṇīyādvaiśvadevārthaṃ bhojanārthañca ṛtvijaḥ ||" iti  [ẏā ]
[120.20-23]     kāśyapa: "sarvahaviḥ pātreṣu śiṣṭamannaṃ sarvaṃ bhagavanniveditamapūpādikañca pūjakā gṛhṇīyuḥ. yathā gurorucchiṣṭaṃ putraśiṣyabhṛtyānāṃ bhojyaṃ. tathā sarvasya rakṣituḥ saumyamūrte sarvavyāpino viṣṇoḥ jagadgurorniveditamannādyaṃ viśvaṃ caturvarṇānāṃ bhaktimatāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhojyam, abhaktimatāmabhojyam" iti.
[120.24]     śrīpāñcarātre īśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ samārādhanādhyāye
[120.25]     skanda: "tvayā niṣedha ākhyāto mantradattasya vastunaḥ |
[120.26]     sarvasya patrapuṣpānna pūrvasya parameśvara || [īśvaraṣ]





[arcāvatārasya sakalalokojjīvanārthatā]

[121.1]     tasyedānīṃ pradānañca prāśanañca praśaṃsasi |
[121.2]     kimetadatra me jātassaṃśayaḥ parameśvara || [īśvaraṣ]
[121.3]     īśvarapṛṣṭharadānaṃ prāśanaṃ putra upabhogyatvameva ca |
[121.4]     śaive pāśupate dṛṣṭamacirādeva pāpakṛt ||
[121.5]     tatpunarvaiṣṇave mārge vartate vyatyayena tu |
[121.6]     anantaphaladaṃ dānātprāśānātkoṣṭaśuddhikṛt ||" iti  [īśvaraṣ]
[121.7]     tatraiva devatārcane: "karṣaṃ nayati pāpāni puṇyavṛddhiṃ karoti ca |
[121.8]     dānairdharmaiḥ tathā puṇyaistapobhiśca na tatphalam || [īśvaraṣ]
[121.9]     bhagavadbhaktipūtānāṃ yatphalaṃ prāpaṇādanāt ||" iti [īśvaraṣ]
[121.10]     anyatra: "satsaṅgadevārcanasatkathāsu paropadeśe ca rato manuṣyaḥ |
[121.11]     sa yāti viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ yaddehāvasāne'cyutatulyatejāḥ ||" iti
[121.12]     anyatra: "nārāyaṇaikaniṣṭhā ye sāttvikāstānnibodhata |
[121.13]     puruṣā rājasāścaiva nānādaivatayājinaḥ ||
[121.14]     bāhyā nirdevatāścaiva tāmasāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
[121.15]     rajastamo'bhibhūtānāṃ na tu mokṣaḥ kathañcana ||" iti
[121.16]     purāṇāntare: "yatraiva pūjyate viṣṇurvidhinā prayatairnaraiḥ |
[121.17]     na tatra hyādhidaurgatya vyādhicaurādikaṃ bhayam ||" iti
[121.18]     kiñca: "nityamuktopabhogyatvātparavyūhātmano hareḥ |
[121.19]     tatkālasannikṛṣṭaika lakṣyatvādvibhavātmanaḥ   || [ā.ṣ14.14]
[121.20]     viśuddhairyogasaṃsiddhaiścintyatvādantarātmanaḥ |
[121.21]     arcātmanyeva sarveṣāmadhikāro niraṅkuśaḥ   || [ā.ṣ14.15]
[121.22]     arcāvatāraviṣaye mamāpyuddeśatastathā |
[121.23]     uktā guṇā na śakyante vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi   || [ā.ṣ14.16]
[122.1]     evaṃ pañcaprakāro'hamātmanāṃ patatāmadhaḥ |
[122.2]     pūrvasmādapi pūrvasmātjyāyāṃścaivottarottaraḥ   || [ā.ṣ]
[122.3]     saulabhyataṃ jagatsvāmī sulabho hyuttarottaraḥ ||"  [āṣ]
[122.4-7]     iti pratipāditarityā bhagavataḥ paratvādiṣu saulabhyābhāvātakhilalokasamujjīvanārthamarcāvatāraḥ. tadekaparatvādvaikhānasāḥ tatpriyatamāḥ. etadṛśanārāyaṇāyaṇaikaparatvābhāvādbodhāyanasya āpastambādīnāñna kathañcadapi paramaikāntitvaṃ bhagavatpriyatamatvañca sambhavati.
[122.8-9]     evaṃ ca vaikhānasānāmeva paramaikāntitvaṃ bhagavatpriyatamatvañcopapadyate iti sarvaṃ samañjasamiti daśamo heturnirūpitam.
[122.10]     iti daśamahetunirūpaṇam
[122.11-14]     iti śrīramaṇaveṅkaṭācaleśvarapādāravindasaparyāniratasya sarvatantrasvatantrasya śrīnivāsamakhivedāntadeśikasya kṛtiṣu śrīvaikhānasasūtratātparyacintāmaṇau daśavidhahatunirūpaṇamupodghātabhāgaḥ samāptaḥ.


